```text
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0001I OAM initialization starting.
          Explanation:  The object access method (OAM) control task has
          received control.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM processing continues.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0002I OAM initialization completed.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) has successfully completed
          its initialization.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM processing continues.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0003I Invalid option specified with OSMC= keyword.  Parameters
          specified = parms.  Initialization terminated.
          Explanation:  The OSMC= startup keyword was specified with the PARM
          keyword on the JCL EXEC statement used to start object access
          method (OAM) An incorrect option was specified following the OSMC=
          startup keyword.
          In the message text:
          parms     The specified parameters.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
 |        System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Specify the OSMC= keyword as either
          OSMC=YES or OSMC=NO.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0005I Invalid name specified with APLAN= keyword.  Parameters specified
          = parms.  Initialization terminated.
          Explanation:  The APLAN= startup keyword was specified with the
          PARM keyword on the JCL EXEC statement used to start object access
          method (OAM).  Following the APLAN= keyword is the name of the DB2
          plan used by OAM to access the optical configuration database.  The
          DB2 plan name specified with the APLAN= keyword was less than one
          character or greater than eight characters.
          In the message text:
          parms     The specified parameters.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Verify the length of the name of the
          DB2 plan specified with the APLAN= keyword with the PARM keyword on
          the JCL EXEC used to invoke OAM.  The name should be from one to
          eight characters in length.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0006D DB2 subsystem ID not supplied by SMS.  Specify SSID or reply 'C'
          to cancel OAM.
          Explanation:  The name of the DB2 subsystem used to gain access is
          required during object access method (OAM) start-up.  This value is
          usually provided by the storage management subsystem (SMS), which
          gets it from the DB2SSID keyword specified in the parmlib member
          IEFSSNxx for SMS.  The DB2SSID keyword was not specified, so the
          SSID is not available for OAM use.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM waits for an operator response.
          Operator Response:  If you know the DB2 subsystem ID, provide it in
          the response to the message; the ID must be from one to four
          characters in length.  OAM uses the ID to establish a connection to
          DB2. If this fails, message CBR0010I is issued.  If you do not know
          the ID, reply 'C' to the message.  OAM initialization abnormally
          ends.
          System Programmer Response:  This message will be issued during
          each OAM start-up until the parmlib member IEFSSNxx is updated to
          include the DB2SSID keyword.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0007I Name of OAM DB2 plan not specified.  Initialization terminated.
          Explanation:  The APLAN= startup keyword was not specified with the
          PARM keyword on the JCL EXEC statement used to start object access
          method (OAM).  The APLAN= startup keyword must be specified with
          the PARM keyword on the JCL EXEC statement used to start OAM.
          Following the APLAN= keyword should be the name of the DB2 plan
          used by OAM to access the optical configuration database.  The name
          of the DB2 plan must be from one to eight characters in length.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Specify the name of the DB2 plan using
          the APLAN= keyword with the PARM keyword on the JCL EXEC statement
          used to invoke OAM.  The name should be one to eight characters in
          length.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0008I OAM is already active on this system.  Initialization terminated.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) has already been started
          on this system.  Only one OAM address space can be active at a
          time.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  There is no need to start OAM again because it
          is already active.  If you are attempting to re-start OAM, wait
          until the previous invocation of OAM is stopped before attempting
          to bring OAM up again.  Message CBR0099I will be issued when the
          previous invocation of OAM has stopped.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0009I Unable to load user interface module.  Initialization terminated.
          Explanation:  The Object access method (OAM) control task was
          unable to load the user interface module.  This module should
          reside in the link pack area (LPA).
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Verify that OAM was correctly
          installed.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0010I Unable to connect to the Optical Configuration Data Base, RC =
          rc. Initialization terminated.
          Explanation:  The object access method (OAM) control task was
          unable to connect to the optical configuration database.  The OAM
          task called OAM to connect to the optical configuration database,
          but OAM returned a nonzero return code, indicating a failure during
          the connect.
          In the message text:
          rc        The return code, to be used for internal diagnostic
                    purposes.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Verify the following:
          °   OAM optical configuration database was correctly defined and
              initialized.
          °   The correct DB2 subsystem name was specified with the SSID
              keyword with the PARM keyword on the JCL EXEC statement used to
              invoke OAM.
          °   The correct DB2 plan name was specified with the PLAN keyword
              with the PARM keyword on the JCL EXEC statement used to invoke
              OAM.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0011I Unable to disconnect from the Optical Configuration Data Base, RC
          = rc.
          Explanation:  The object access method (OAM) control task was
          unable to disconnect from the optical configuration database.  The
          OAM task called OAM to disconnect from the optical configuration
          database, but OAM returned a nonzero return code, indicating a
          failure during the disconnect.
          In the message text:
          rc        The return code, to be used for diagnostic purposes.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM abnormal completion.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Check for any preceding DATABASE 2
          messages that may indicate the nature of the problem.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0012I Unable to load OAM cross memory module.  Initialization
          terminated.
          Explanation:  The object access method (OAM) control task was
          unable to load the OAM cross memory module.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Verify that OAM was correctly
          installed.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0013I Unable to load OAM CTC I/O driver.  Initialization terminated.
          Explanation:  The object access method (OAM) control task was
          unable to load the OAM CTC I/O driver module.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Verify that OAM was correctly
          installed.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0018I Unable to OPEN the Optical Configuration Data Base, RC = rc.
          Initialization terminated.
          Explanation:  The object access method (OAM) control task was
          unable to OPEN the optical configuration database.  The OAM control
          task called to OPEN the optical configuration database, but OAM
          returned a nonzero return code, indicating a failure during the
          OPEN.
          In the message text:
          rc        The return code, to be used for diagnostic purposes.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Verify the following:
          °   OAM optical configuration database was correctly defined and
              initialized.
          °   Correct DB2 subsystem name was specified with the SSID keyword
              with the PARM keyword on the JCL EXEC statement used to invoke
              OAM.
          °   Correct DB2 plan name was specified with the APLAN keyword with
              the PARM keyword on the JCL EXEC statement used to invoke OAM.
          °   OAM started task has the correct authority to OPEN the Optical
              Configuration Database.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0019I Unable to CLOSE the Optical Configuration Data Base, RC = rc.
          Initialization terminated.
          Explanation:  The object access method (OAM) control task was
          unable to CLOSE the optical configuration database.  The OAM
          control task called to CLOSE the optical configuration database,
          but OAM returned a nonzero return code, indicating a failure during
          the CLOSE.
          In the message text:
          rc        The return code, to be used for diagnostic purposes.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Verify the following:
          °   OAM optical configuration database was correctly defined and
              initialized.
          °   Correct DB2 subsystem name was specified with the SSID keyword
              with the PARM keyword on the JCL EXEC statement used to invoke
              OAM.
          °   Correct DB2 plan name was specified with the APLAN keyword with
              the PARM keyword on the JCL EXEC statement used to invoke OAM.
          °   OAM started task has the correct authority to access the
              Optical Configuration Database.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0020I Error during CTC initialization.  Initialization terminated.
          Explanation:  An error occurred during the CTC initialization phase
          of object access method (OAM) initialization.  This message is
          preceded by other messages indicating the cause of the error.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Respond as indicated by the programmer
          response section for the preceding messages.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0021I UCB not found for CTC devnum.  Initialization terminated.
          Explanation:  An error occurred during the CTC initialization phase
          of object access method (OAM) initialization.  The optical
          configuration database contained an entry for an optical disk drive
          or an optical disk library, indicating the device was at a
          channel-to-channel adapter address.  OAM did not find an MVS Unit
          Control Block (UCB) for the specified device number.
          In the message text:
          devnum    The device number.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Verify that:
          °   All device numbers specified as the CTC device number in the
              drive table in the optical configuration database are indeed
              devices that have been defined to the MVS operating system.
          °   All device numbers specified as the CTC device number in the
              library table in the optical configuration database are indeed
              devices that have been defined to the MVS operating system.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0022I UCB for device devnum does not indicate that it is a CTC device.
          Initialization terminated.
          Explanation:  An error occurred during the CTC initialization phase
          of object access method (OAM) initialization.  The optical
          configuration database contained an entry for an optical disk drive
          or an optical disk library, indicating the device was at a
          channel-to-channel adapter address.  OAM found an MVS Unit Control
          Block (UCB) for the specified device number, but the UCB did not
          indicate that the device was a channel-to-channel adapter.
          In the message text:
          devnum    The device number.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Verify that:
          °   All device numbers specified as the CTC device number in the
              drive table in the optical configuration database are indeed
              devices that have been defined to the MVS operating system as
              channel-to-channel adapters.
          °   All device numbers specified as the CTC device number in the
              library table in the optical configuration database are indeed
              devices that have been defined to the MVS operating system as
              channel-to-channel adapters.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0023I Storage unavailable for CTC work area.  Initialization
          terminated.
          Explanation:  An error occurred during the CTC initialization phase
          of Object access method initialization.  For each
          channel-to-channel adapter device used by Object access method, a
          CTC work area is obtained using the STORAGE macro from subpool 241
          and anchored to the MVS Unit Control Block.  The STORAGE for one of
          the CTC work areas failed.  This message is preceded by message
          CBR7004I, which indicates the failing return code from the STORAGE
          macro.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Determine the cause of the STORAGE
          error by investigating the return code from the STORAGE macro and
          referring to the documentation for message CBR7004I.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0024I Storage unavailable for CTC list.  Initialization terminated.
          Explanation:  An error occurred during the CTC initialization phase
          of object access method (OAM) initialization.  During CTC
          initialization, the OAM constructs a list of all the unique CTC
          devices it uses.  The STORAGE for an area of virtual storage in
          which to construct the CTC list failed.  This message is preceded
          by message CBR7004I, which indicates the failing return code from
          the STORAGE macro.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Determine the cause of the STORAGE
          error by investigating the return code from the STORAGE macro and
          referring to the documentation for message CBR7004I.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0026I Invalid option specified with MAXS= keyword.  Parameters
          specified = parms.  Initialization terminated.
          Explanation:  The MAXS= startup keyword was specified with the PARM
          keyword on the JCL EXEC statement used to start object access
          method (OAM).  An incorrect value was specified following the MAXS=
          startup keyword.  The MAXS= keyword must either be omitted, in
          which case a default of two will be used, or specify a one or two
          digit numeric value.
          In the message text:
          parms     The specified parameters.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Correct the parameters.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0027I SMS is not active on this system.  Initialization terminated.
          Explanation:  The storage management subsystem (SMS) is not active
          on the system where object access method (OAM) startup has been
          requested.  OAM cannot operate without SMS.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Use the SET SMS operator command to start SMS,
          then start OAM again.
   __________________________________________________________________________
 | CBR0028I Error pinning UCB at address address for device device-number.
 |        Return code = rc, Reason code = reas.
 |        Explanation:  The OAM control task attempt to "pin" the unit
 |        control block (UCB) at address address for device device-number
 |        using the UCBPIN service.  The request was unsucessful.  For
 |        diagnotic purposes, rc and reas are the return code and reason
 |        code, respectively, from the UCBPIN service.
 |        In the message text:
 |        address   Address at which attempt to pin the UCB is made.
 |        device-number
 |                  The device on which the attempt to pin the UCB is made.
 |        rc        The return code.
 |        reas      The reason code.
 |        Source:  Object access method (OAM)
 |        System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
 |        The system sends messages about the job to one of the following:
 |        °   The primary console
 |        °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
 |        °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
 |            (MCS)
 |        Operator Response:  Repeat the OAM start-up procedure.  If the
 |        failure persists, notify the system programmer.
 |        System Programmer Response:  For information on UCBPIN return codes
 |        and reason codes, see MVS/ESA Component Diagnosis and Logic:
 |        Input/Output Supervisor.  Contact an IBM programming service
 |        representative for assistance.  If the error recurs and the program
 |        is not in error, search problem reporting databases for a fix for
 |        the problem.  If no fix exists, contact the IBM Support Center.
   __________________________________________________________________________
 | CBR0029I Error unpinning UCB at address address for device device-number.
 |        Return code = rc, Reason code = reas.
 |        Explanation:  The OAM control task attempt to "unpin" the MVS Unit
 |        Control Block (UCB) at address address for device device-number
 |        using the UCBPIN service.  The request was unsucessful.  For
 |        diagnotic purposes, rc and reas are the return code and reason
 |        code, respectively, from the UCBPIN service.
 |        In the message text:
 |        address   Address at which attempt to unpin the UCB is made.
 |        device-number
 |                  The device on which the attempt to unpin the UCB is made.
 |        rc        The return code.
 |        reas      The reason code.
 |        Source:  Object access method (OAM)
 |        System Action:  OAM initialization stops.  The system sends
 |        messages about the job to one of the following:
 |        °   The primary console
 |        °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
 |        °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
 |            (MCS)
 |        Operator Response:  Repeat the OAM start-up procedure.  If the
 |        failure persists, notify the system programmer.
 |        System Programmer Response:  For information on UCBPIN return codes
 |        and reason codes, see MVS/ESA Component Diagnosis and Logic:
 |        Input/Output Supervisor.  Contact an IBM programming service
 |        representive for assistance.  If the error recurs and the program
 |        is not in error, search problem reporting data bases for a fix for
 |        the problem.  If no fix exists, contact the IBM Support Center.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0030I Unable to load CDS activation listen exit routine.
          Initialization terminated.
          Explanation:  The object access method (OAM) control task was
          unable to load the listen exit routine which receives control from
          the Event Notification Facility (ENF) when the storage management
          subsystem (SMS) activates a Control Data Set (CDS).
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Verify that OAM has been correctly
          installed.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0031I Unable to establish CDS activation listen exit routine, RC = rc.
          Initialization terminated.
          Explanation:  The object access method (OAM) control task was
          unable to establish the Event Notification Facility (ENF) listen
          exit routine which receives control when the storage management
          subsystem (SMS) activates a Control Data Set (CDS).
          In the message text:
          rc        The ENF return code.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Repeat the OAM start-up procedure.  If the
          failure persists, notify the system programmer.  Contact the
          Service Representative for assistance.
          System Programmer Response:  If the error recurs and the program is
          not in error, search problem reporting databases for a fix for the
          problem.  If no fix exists, contact the IBM Support Center.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0032I Unable to load OAM Resource Manager.  Initialization terminated.
          Explanation:  The object access method (OAM) control task was
          unable to load the OAM Resource Manager module.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Verify that OAM was correctly
          installed.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0033I Unable to establish OAM Resource Manager, RC = rc.
          Initialization terminated.
          Explanation:  An error occurred during processing of the RESMGR
          macro.
          In the message text:
          rc        The return code.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  See the RESMGR macro for the return
          codes.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0034I Unable to load OAM PC Routine for User Swap Control.
          Initialization terminated.
          Explanation:  The object access method (OAM) control task was
          unable to load the OAM PC Routine for User Swap Control.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Verify that OAM was correctly
          installed.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0035I Unable to load OAM SRB Routine for User Swap Control.
          Initialization terminated.
          Explanation:  The object access method (OAM) control task was
          unable to load the OAM SRB Routine for User Swap Control.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Verify that OAM was correctly
          installed.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0060I Storage unavailable for OVT control block.  Initialization
          terminated.
          Explanation:  The control task issued the STORAGE macro to obtain
          storage for the OVT control block, but the STORAGE macro failed.
          This message is preceded by message CBR7004I, which contains the
          return code from the STORAGE macro.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  See message CBR7004I, then determine
          the cause of the STORAGE error by investigating the return code.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0062I Storage unavailable for LCB control block.  Initialization
          terminated.
          Explanation:  The control task attempted to STORAGE storage for the
          LCB control block, but the STORAGE failed.  This message is
          preceded by message CBR7004I which contains the return code from
          the STORAGE macro.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  See message CBR7004I, then determine
          the cause of the STORAGE error by investigating the return code.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0063I Storage unavailable for ODCB control block.  Initialization
          terminated.
          Explanation:  The control task issued the STORAGE macro to obtain
          storage for the ODCB control block, but the STORAGE macro failed.
          This message is preceded by message CBR7004I, which contains the
          return code from the STORAGE macro.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  See message CBR7004I, then determine
          the cause of the STORAGE error by investigating the return code.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0064I Storage unavailable for VSCB control block.  Initialization
          terminated.
          Explanation:  The control task issued the STORAGE macro to obtain
          storage for the VSCB control block, but the STORAGE macro failed.
          This message is preceded by message CBR7004I, which contains the
          return code from the STORAGE macro.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  See message CBR7004I, then determine
          the cause of the STORAGE error by investigating the return code.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0065I Storage unavailable for VCB control block.  Initialization
          terminated.
          Explanation:  The control task attempted to get storage for the VCB
          control block, but the STORAGE macro failed.  This message is
          preceded by message CBR7004I, which contains the return code from
          the STORAGE macro.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  See message CBR7004I, then determine
          the cause of the STORAGE error by investigating the return code.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0075I Unable to establish a cross memory environment.  Initialization
          terminated.
          Explanation:  The control task attempted to establish a cross
          memory environment by issuing the MVS macros ATSET, ETCRE and ETCO.
          One of the macros failed.  A preceding message contains the return
          code from the macro that failed.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Determine the cause of the error by
          investigating the return code from the macro which failed.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0080I Error establishing the {Operator Command Task | Library control
          task | Drive control task | OAM Storage Management Component Task}.
          Initialization terminated.
          Explanation:  During the initialization phase of processing, the
          control task attempted to establish one of the major subtasks.  The
          major subtasks are:
          °   Operator Command Task
          °   Library control task
          °   Drive control task
          °   OAM Storage Management Component Task
          The control task was unable to establish the subtask because the
          ATTACH of the subtask failed or the subtask did not initialize
          successfully.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Either the ATTACH failed or the
          subtask initialization failed.  If the ATTACH failed, this message
          will be preceded by message CBR7000I, which contains the return
          code from the ATTACH macro.  If the subtask initialization failed,
          this message will be preceded by messages which further describe
          that failure.  Refer to preceding messages.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0081I Error re-establishing the {Operator Command Task | Library
          control task | Drive control task | OAM Storage Management
          Component Task}.  OAM processing terminates.
          Explanation:  During object access method (OAM) processing, a major
          subtask abnormally ended.  The major subtasks are:
          °   Operator Command Task
          °   Library control task
          °   Drive control task
          °   OAM Storage Management Component Task
          The control task attempted to re-establish the failing subtask.
          This attempt failed because the ATTACH of the subtask failed or the
          subtask did not initialize successfully.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM completion processing begins.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Either the ATTACH failed or the
          subtask initialization failed.  If the ATTACH failed, this message
          will be preceded by message CBR7000I, which contains the return
          code from the ATTACH macro.  If the subtask initialization failed,
          this message will be preceded by messages which further describe
          that failure.  Refer to preceding messages.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0082I Error detaching the {Operator Command Task | Library control task
          | Drive control task | OAM Storage Management Component Task}.
          Explanation:  The control task attempted to detach one of the major
          subtasks.  The major subtasks are:
          °   Operator Command Task
          °   Library control task
          °   Drive control task
          °   OAM Storage Management Component Task.
          The control task was unable to detach the subtask.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM processing continues.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  This message will be preceded by
          message CBR7001I, which contains the return code from the DETACH
          macro.  Refer to the documentation for message CBR7001I.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0095E OAM waiting for SMS Control Data Set activation.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) has initialized with a
          null configuration.  That is, no optical drives have been defined.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM waits for operator action.  No useful work can
          be done until a new optical configuration has been activated.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.  If there are no
          plans to add optical drive definitions to the SMS Control Data Set
          in the near future, use the STOP OAM command to stop the OAM
          address space.
          System Programmer Response:  Define the optical configuration using
          the ISMF Storage Administrator library, drive, and storage group
          define panels.  When the definitions are completed, activate the
          control data set (CDS).
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0096I OAM restart in progress.
          Explanation:   The storage management subsystem (SMS) has activated
          a new control data set (CDS); the optical configuration may have
          changed.  The object access method (OAM) control task has begun the
          process of replacing the current optical configuration with the new
          one.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  All currently active requests are allowed to
          complete.  Requests which are currently queued and waiting for
          processing, and requests which are received while the restart is in
          progress, are canceled with the reason code that indicates that the
          OAM address space is not available.  These requests may be
          reentered when the restart is complete.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0097I OAM restart completed.
          Explanation:   The storage management subsystem (SMS) has activated
          a new control data set (CDS); the optical configuration may have
          changed.  The object access method (OAM) control task has completed
          construction of the new optical configuration.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM receives and processes all user requests.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0098I OAM termination starting.
          Explanation:  The object access method (OAM) control task has
          received a request to stop processing from the system operator.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0099I OAM termination completed.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) has completed processing
          and has returned control to the MVS operating system.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0100I Unable to access library table.  Return code = rc, Reason code =
          rsncde, SQL error code = sqlercde, CAF error code = cafercde.
          Explanation:  An error occurred attempting to access the OLIBRARY
          table in the optical configuration database.
          In the message text:
          rc        The return code.
          rsncde    The reason code.
          sqlercde  The SQL error code.
          cafercde  The Call Attach Facility (CAF) error code.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For information on SQL and CAF error
          reason codes consult IBM DATABASE 2 Version 2 Messages and Codes.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0101I Invalid number of slots slots specified for library libname.
          Explanation:  The number of slots specified for library is
          incorrect.  The number of slots must be 64.
          In the message text:
          slots     The number of slots.
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Correct the number of slots for the
          specified library in the library table in the DB2 optical
          configuration database.  Use SPUFI to make the correction.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0102I Invalid number of empty slots slots specified for library
          libname.
          Explanation:  The number of empty slots specified for the library
          is incorrect.  The number of empty slots must be in the range 0 to
          64.
          In the message text:
          slots     The number of empty slots.
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Correct the number of empty slots for
          the specified library in the library table in the DB2 optical
          configuration database.  Use SPUFI to make the correction.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0103I Invalid maximum number of drives drives specified for library
          libname.
          Explanation:  The maximum number of drives specified for a library
          is incorrect.  The maximum number of drives must be 4.
          In the message text:
          drives    The maximum number of drives specified.
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Correct the maximum number of drives
          for the specified library in the library table in the DB2 optical
          configuration database.  Use SPUFI to make the correction.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0104I Invalid device number devnum  specified for primary CTC for
          library libname.
          Explanation:  The device number specified for the primary CTC for a
          library is incorrect.  The device number must consist of four
          hexadecimal digits (0 through 9 and A through F).
          In the message text:
          devnum    The device number.
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Correct the device number of the
          primary CTC for the specified library using the ISMF Storage
          Administrator library alter panel.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0105I Invalid port number portnum specified for primary port for
          library libname.
          Explanation:  The port number specified for the primary port for a
          library is incorrect.  The port number must be either 1 or 2.
          In the message text:
          portnum   The port number.
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Correct the port number of the primary
          port for the specified library, using the ISMF Storage
          Administrator library alter panel.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0106I Invalid device number devnum specified for alternate CTC for
          library libname.
          Explanation:  The device number specified for the alternate CTC for
          a library is incorrect.  The device number must consist of four
          hexadecimal digits (0 through 9 and A through F).
          In the message text:
          devnum    The device number.
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Correct the device number of the
          alternate CTC for the specified library, using the ISMF Storage
          Administrator library alter panel.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0107I Invalid port number portnum specified for alternate port for
          library libname.
          Explanation:  The port number specified for the alternate port for
          a library is incorrect.  The port number must be either 1 or 2.
          In the message text:
          portnum   The port number.
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Correct the port number of the
          alternate port for the specified library, using the ISMF Storage
          Administrator library alter panel.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0115I SMS optical library definitions unavailable.  SSI RC = ssirc, SMS
          RC = smsrc, SMS REASON = smsrsncd.
          Explanation:  During object access method (OAM) initialization
          processing, a Subsystem Interface (SSI) call to the storage
          management subsystem (SMS) has been made to determine the optical
          library configuration in the Active Control Data Set (ACDS).  The
          call failed.
          In the message text:
          ssirc     The SSI return code.
          smsrc     The SMS return code.
          smsrsncd  The SMS reason code.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.  The system sends
          messages about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For information on the SMS return
          codes and reason codes see MVS/DFP Version 3 Release 2: Diagnosis
          Reference.  If the error recurs and the program is not in error,
          search problem reporting data bases for a fix for the problem.  If
          no fix exists, contact the IBM Support Center.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0116I SMS optical library libname not found in Optical Configuration
          Data Base.
          Explanation:  An optical library is defined in the storage
          management subsystem (SMS) Active Control Data Set (ACDS), but is
          not defined in the library table in the DB2 optical configuration
          database.
          In the message text:
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Whether the library name is
          incorrectly specified in the CDS, or the library definition is
          missing in the library table, the correction is the same:  use the
          ISMF Storage Administrator library delete function to delete the
          current library definition, then use the library define panel to
          create a new definition.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0120I Unable to access slot table.  Return code = rc, Reason code =
          rsncde, SQL error code = sqlercde, CAF error code = cafercde.
          Explanation:  An error occurred attempting to access the SLOT table
          in the optical configuration database.
          In the message text:
          rc        The return code.
          rsncde    The reason code.
          sqlercde  The SQL error code.
          cafercde  The Call Attachment Facility (CAF) error code.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For information on SQL and CAF error
          reason codes consult IBM DATABASE 2 Version 2 Messages and Codes.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0121I Invalid slot name slotname in slot table.
          Explanation:  The slot table contains a row that contained an
          incorrect slot name.  The slot name must consist of three decimal
          digits (0 through 9) or the three characters "GRP" or the two
          characters "IO."
          In the message text:
          slotname  The slot name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Correct the row in the slot table that
          contains the incorrect slot name.  Use SPUFI to make the
          correction.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0123I Slot name slotname invalid for library libname.
          Explanation:  There is a row in the slot table for a slot in a
          library.  The slot name specified in the slot table is greater than
          the number of slots contained in the library as specified in the
          library table.
          In the message text:
          slotname  The slot name.
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Correct the definition of the slot for
          the library in the slot table in the DB2 optical configuration
          database.  Use SPUFI to make the correction.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0124I Definition of slot slotname in library libname missing.
          Explanation:  There is no row in the slot table for a slot in a
          library.
          In the message text:
          slotname  The slot name.
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM will automatically create a row in the slot
          table for the missing slot.  Object access method initialization
          continues.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0125I Definition of slot slotname in library libname created.
          Explanation:  There was no row in the slot table for a slot in a
          library.  Object access method (OAM) successfully created a row in
          the slot table for the slot.  The newly created row indicates that
          the slot is empty and operational.
          In the message text:
          slotname  The slot name.
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization continues.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0126I Definition of slot slotname in library libname unsuccessful.
          Explanation:  There is no row in the slot table for the slot in a
          library. Object access method (OAM) attempted to add a row in the
          slot table for the slot.  The attempt to add the row was
          unsuccessful.
          In the message text:
          slotname  The slot name.
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization continues.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Check for preceding messages
          indicating the cause the error.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0127I Return code = rc, Reason code = rsncde, SQL error code =
          sqlercde, CAF error code = cafercde.
          Explanation:  An attempt to create dynamically a slot definition in
          the slot table for a missing slot failed.  This message is preceded
          by message CBR0126I.  Message CBR0126I contains the name of the
          slot and the name of the library containing the slot.
          In the message text:
          rc        The return code.
          rsncde    The reason code.
          sqlercde  The SQL error code.
          cafercde  The Call Attachment Facility (CAF) error code.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For information on SQL and CAF error
          codes consult IBM DATABASE 2 Version 2 Messages and Codes.  After
          the problem has been corrected, restart Object access method.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0140I Unable to access drive table.  Return code = rc, Reason code =
          rsncde, SQL error code = sqlercde, CAF error code = cafercde.
          Explanation:  An error occurred attempting to access the drive
          table in the optical configuration database.
          In the message text:
          rc        The return code.
          rsncde    The reason code.
          sqlercde  The SQL error code.
          cafercde  The Call Attachment Facility (CAF) error code.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For information on SQL and CAF error
          reason codes consult IBM DATABASE 2 Version 2 Messages and Codes.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0141I Invalid library name libname specified for drive drvename.
          Explanation:  The library name specified for a drive is not defined
          in the SMS ACDS or the definition of the specified library
          contained errors.
          In the message text:
          libname   The library name.
          drvename  The drive.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  If the library name in the drive
          definition is in error, correct the library name using the ISMF
          Storage Administrator drive delete function and drive define panel.
          If the library definition is missing from the SMS CDS, add the
          definition using the library define panel.  If the library
          definition is in error, follow the instructions for the message
          describing that error.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0142I Invalid device number devnum specified for CTC for drive
          drvename.
          Explanation:  The device number specified for the CTC for a drive
          is not a valid device number.  The device number must be four
          hexadecimal digits (0 through 9 and A through F).
          In the message text:
          devnum    The device number.
          drvename  The drive.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Correct the device number specified
          for the CTC for the specified drive using the ISMF Storage
          Administrator drive alter panel.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0143I Invalid SCSI bus address busaddr specified for drive drvename.
          Explanation:  The SCSI bus address for a drive is not a valid bus
          address.  The bus address must be one octal digit (0 through 7).
          In the message text:
          busaddr   The bus address.
          drvename  The drive.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Correct the SCSI bus address specified
          for the drive using the ISMF Storage Administrator drive alter
          panel.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0144I Invalid logical unit number lun specified for drive drvename.
          Explanation:  The logical unit number for a drive is not valid.
          The logical unit number must be one octal digit (0 through 7).
          In the message text:
          lun       The logical unit number.
          drvename  The drive.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Correct the logical unit number
          specified for the drive using the ISMF Storage Administrator drive
          alter panel.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0145I Invalid library drive number libdrnum specified for drive
          drvename.
          Explanation:  The library drive number for a drive is not valid.
          The library drive number must be one digit (0 through 3).
          In the message text:
          libdrnum  The library drive number.
          drvename  The drive.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Correct the library drive number
          specified for the drive using the ISMF Storage Administrator drive
          alter panel.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0146I Invalid number of drives num defined as residing in library
          libname.
          Explanation:  The number of drives defined as residing in a library
          in the SMS ACDS is incorrect.  The number of drives must be in the
          range 1 to 4.
          In the message text:
          num       The number of drives.
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  If no drives have been defined for the
          library, use the ISMF Storage Administrator drive define panel to
          add one or more drive definitions.  If too many drives have been
          defined, use the drive delete function to delete one or more drive
          definitions.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0147I No optical drives defined.  OAM initializing with null
          configuration.
          Explanation:  There are no optical drives defined in the SMS ACDS.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initializes with a null configuration.  When
          initialization is complete, message CBR0095E is issued.
          Operator Response:  Wait for message CBR0095E to appear, then
          follow the instructions for that message.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0148I More than 256 drives defined.  Excess ignored.
          Explanation:  The number of optical drives defined in the SMS ACDS
          exceeds 256.  Object access method (OAM) does not support more than
          256 optical drives.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization continues.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Remove the excess optical drive
          definitions using the ISMF Storage Administrator drive delete
          function.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0149I Duplicate library drive number libdrnum specified for drive
          drvename.
          Explanation:  The library drive number for the drive is the same as
          the library drive number specified for another optical drive in the
          same library.
          In the message text:
          libdrnum  The library drive number.
          drvename  The drive.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Correct the library drive number
          specified for the drive using the ISMF Storage Administrator drive
          alter panel.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0155I SMS optical drive definitions unavailable.  SSI RC = ssirc, SMS
          RC = smsrc, SMS REASON = smsrsncd.
          Explanation:  During object access method (OAM) initialization
          processing, a Subsystem Interface (SSI) call to the storage
          management subsystem (SMS) has been made to determine the optical
          drive configuration in the Active Control Data Set (ACDS).  The
          call failed.
          In the message text:
          ssirc     The SSI return code.
          smsrc     The SMS return code.
          smsrsncd  The SMS reason code.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.  The system sends
          messages about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For information on the SMS return
          codes and reason codes see MVS/DFP Version 3 Release 2: Diagnosis
          Reference.  If the error recurs and the program is not in error,
          search problem reporting data bases for a fix for the problem.  If
          no fix exists, contact the IBM Support Center.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0156I SMS optical drive drvename not found in Optical Configuration
          Data Base.
          Explanation:  An optical drive is defined in the storage management
          subsystem (SMS) Active Control Data Set (ACDS), but is not defined
          in the drive table in the DB2 optical configuration database.
          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Whether the drive name is incorrectly
          specified in the CDS, or the drive definition is missing in the
          drive table, the correction is the same:  use the ISMF Storage
          Administrator drive delete function to delete the current drive
          definition, then use the drive define panel to create a new
          definition.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0161I Invalid library name libname specified for storage group
          strgrpnm.
          Explanation:  The library name specified for the storage group is
          not defined in the SMS ACDS or the definition of the specified
          library contained errors.
          In the message text:
          libname   The library name.
          strgrpnm  The storage group name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  If the library name in the storage
          group definition is in error, correct the library name using the
          ISMF Storage Administrator object storage group alter or object
          backup storage group alter panel.  If the library definition is
          missing from the SMS CDS, add the definition using the library
          define panel.  If the library definition is in error, follow the
          instructions for the message describing that error.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0175I SMS object storage group constructs unavailable.  SSI RC = ssirc,
          SMS RC = smsrc, SMS REASON = smsrsncd.
          Explanation:  During object access method (OAM) initialization
          processing, a Subsystem Interface (SSI) call to the storage
          management subsystem (SMS) has been made to determine the object
          storage group configuration in the Active Control Data Set (ACDS).
          The call failed.
          In the message text:
          ssirc     The SSI return code.
          smsrc     The SMS return code.
          smsrsncd  The SMS reason code.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.  The system sends
          messages about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For information on the SMS return
          codes and reason codes see MVS/ESA Version 3 Release 2: Diagnosis
          Reference.  If the error recurs and the program is not in error,
          search problem reporting data bases for a fix for the problem.  If
          no fix exists, contact the IBM Support Center.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0180I Unable to access volume table.  Return code = rc, Reason code =
          rsncde, SQL error code = sqlercde, CAF error code = cafercde.
          Explanation:  An error occurred attempting to access the VOLUME
          table in the optical configuration database.
c.cc 8
          In the message text:
          rc        The return code.
          rsncde    The reason code.
          sqlercde  The SQL error code.
          cafercde  The Call Attachment Facility (CAF) error code.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For information on SQL and CAF error
          reason codes consult IBM DATABASE 2 Version 2 Messages and Codes.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0181I Invalid library name libname specified for volume volser.
          Explanation:  The library name specified for the volume is not
          defined in the SMS ACDS or the definition of the library contained
          errors.
          In the message text:
          libname   The library name.
          volser    The volume serial number.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The volume is not added to the optical
          configuration.  OAM initialization continues.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  If the library name in the volume
          definition is in error, correct the library name in the volume
          table in the DB2 optical configuration database, using SPUFI.  If
          the library definition is missing from the SMS CDS, add the
          definition using the ISMF Storage Administrator library define
          panel.  If the library definition is in error, follow the
          instructions for the message describing that error.  If a new
          configuration is being activated, and if the volume is not to be
          part of that configuration, no action is necessary.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0182I Invalid storage group name strgrpnm specified for volume volser.
          Explanation:  The storage group name specified for the volume is
          not defined in the SMS ACDS, or the definition of the storage group
          contained errors.
          In the message text:
          strgrpnm  The storage group name.
          volser    The volume serial number.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The volume is added to the optical configuration.
          OAM initialization continues.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  If the storage group name in the
          volume definition is in error, correct the storage group name in
          the volume table in the DB2 optical configuration database, using
          SPUFI.  If the storage group definition is missing from the SMS
          CDS, add the definition using the ISMF Storage Administrator object
          storage group or object backup storage group define panel.  If the
          storage group definition is in error, follow the instructions for
          the message describing that error.  If a new configuration is being
          activated, and if the volume is not to be part of that
          configuration, no action is necessary.
c.cc 12
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0183I Invalid slot name slotname specified for volume volser.
          Explanation:  The slot name specified for the volume is not valid.
          A slot name consists of three decimal digits (0 through 9) or the
          three characters "GRP" or the three characters "IO" (IO and a
          blank).
          In the message text:
          slotname  The slot name.
          volser    The volume serial number.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Correct the slot name specified for
          the volume in the volume table in the DB2 optical configuration
          database, using SPUFI.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0184I Slot name slotname does not exist in library libname, specified
          for volume volser.
          Explanation:  The slot name specified for the volume is not valid
          in the indicated library.
          In the message text:
          slotname  The slot name.
          libname   The library name.
          volser    The volume serial number.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Correct the slot name specified for
          the volume in the volume table in the DB2 optical configuration
          database, using SPUFI.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0190I Volume/Slot inconsistent.
            ------- SLOT TABLE ------- ------- VOLUME TABLE -------
            LIBRARY  SLT VOL0   VOL1   VOL0   LIBRARY  SLT O VOL1
            lib1     st1 vol1   vol2   vol3   lib2     st2 o vol4
            REASONS
            errors
          Explanation:  The information in the volume table is inconsistent
          with the information in the slot table.  This message contains
          selected information from the volume table and the slot table.  The
          following fields are displayed:
          lib1      Library name from the row of the slot table in the
                    optical configuration database.
          st1       Slot name from the row in the slot table in the optical
                    configuration database.
          vol1      Volume serial number of the optical disk volume that
                    should be at orientation 0 in slot st1 in library lib1.
          vol2      Volume serial number of the optical disk volume that
                    should be at orientation 1 in slot st1 in library lib1.
          vol3      Volume serial number from the row in the volume table.
          lib2      Library that should contain vol3.
          st2       Name of the slot in library lib2 that should contain
                    volume vol3.
          o         Orientation of volume vol3 in slot st2 in library lib2.
          vol4      Volume serial number of the volume on the opposite side
                    of the optical disk media containing volume vol3.
          errors    Reasons why the slot table is inconsistent with the
                    volume table:
                    1.  Slot table indicates that volume vol1 resides in
                        library lib1 in slot st1 at orientation 0, but there
                        is no row in the volume table for volume vol1.  This
                        error can be the result of a previously detected
                        error in the definition of volume vol1, as indicated
                        by message CBR0181I or message CBR0182I.
                    2.  Slot table indicates that volume vol1 resides in
                        library lib1 in slot st1 at orientation 0, but the
                        library name lib2 associated with volume vol1 in the
                        volume table does not match the library name lib1 in
                        the slot table.
                    3.  Slot table indicates that volume vol1 resides in
                        library lib1 in slot st1 at orientation 0, but the
                        slot name st2 associated with volume vol1 in the
                        volume table does not match the slot name st1 in the
                        slot table.
                    4.  Slot table indicates that volume vol1 resides in
                        library lib1 in slot st1 at orientation 0, but the
                        orientation o associated with volume vol1 in the
                        volume table indicates it resides in orientation 1.
                    5.  Slot table indicates that volume vol2 resides in
                        library lib1 in slot st1 at orientation 1, but there
                        is no row in the volume table for volume vol2.  This
                        error can be the result of a previously detected
                        error in the definition of volume vol2, as indicated
                        by message CBR0181I or message CBR0182I.
                    6.  Slot table indicates that volume vol2 resides in
                        library lib1 in slot st1 at orientation 1, but the
                        library name lib2 associated with volume vol2 in the
                        volume table does not match the library name lib1 in
                        the slot table.
                    7.  Slot table indicates that volume vol2 resides in
                        library lib1 in slot st1 at orientation 1, but the
                        slot name st2 associated with volume vol2 in the
                        volume table does not match the slot name st1 in the
                        slot table.
                    8.  Slot table indicates that volume vol2 resides in
                        library lib1 in slot st1 at orientation 1, but the
                        orientation o associated with volume vol2 in the
                        volume table indicates it resides in orientation 0.
                    9.  Volume table indicates that volume vol3 resides in
                        library lib2 in slot st2 However, the entry in the
                        slot table for the same slot in the same library
                        indicates that the slot is not occupied.
                    10. Volume table indicates that volume vol3 resides in
                        library lib2 in slot st2 in orientation 0.  However,
                        the entry in the slot table for the specified slot in
                        the specified library indicates that the volume at
                        orientation 0 is vol1, which is different than volume
                        vol3.
                    11. Volume table indicates that volume vol3 resides in
                        library lib2 in slot st2 in orientation 1.  However,
                        the entry in the slot table for the specified slot in
                        the specified library indicates that the volume at
                        orientation 1 is vol2, which is different than volume
                        vol3.
                    12. Volume table indicates that volume vol3 resides in
                        library lib2 in slot st2 in orientation 0 and that
                        the volume on the other side of the cartridge is
                        vol4.  However, the entry in the slot table for the
                        specified slot in the specified library indicates
                        that the volume at orientation 1 is vol2, which is
                        different than volume vol4.
                    13. Volume table indicates that volume vol3 resides in
                        library lib2 in slot st2 in orientation 1 and that
                        the volume on the other side of the cartridge is
                        vol4.  However, the entry in the slot table for the
                        specified slot in the specified library indicates
                        that the volume at orientation 0 is vol1, which is
                        different than volume vol4.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Make the appropriate corrections in
          the slot table or the volume table in the DB2 optical configuration
          database using SPUFI.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR0195I Volume table inconsistent.
            ------ VOLUME TABLE ------ ------ VOLUME TABLE -------
            VOL0   VOL1   LIBRARY  SLT VOL0   VOL1   LIBRARY   SLT
            vol1   vol2   lib1     st1 vol3   vol4   lib2      st2
            REASONS
            reasons
            END OF DISPLAY
          Explanation:  The information in the volume table for one optical
          disk volume is inconsistent with information in the volume table
          for another optical disk volume.  This message contains selected
          information from the volume table for the two optical disk volumes.
          The following fields are displayed:
          vol1      Volume serial number of the optical disk volume.
          vol2      Volume serial number of the optical disk volume that
                    should be on the opposite side of vol1.
          lib1      Library name of the library that contains volume vol1.
          st1       Slot name of the slot that contains volume vol1.
          vol3      Volume serial number of the optical disk volume that
                    should be on the opposite side of vol1.  This volume
                    serial number should be the same as vol2.
          vol4      Volume serial number of the optical disk volume that
                    should be on the opposite side of vol3.  This volume
                    serial number should be the same as vol1.
          lib2      Library name of the library that contains volume vol3.
                    This library name should be the same as lib1.
          st2       Slot name of the slot that contains volume vol3.  This
                    slot name should be the same as st1.
          reasons   Reasons why the volume table is inconsistent:
                    1.  Volume table indicates that volume vol1 resides in
                        library lib1 in slot st1.  The opposite side volume
```
