                        is vol2.  However, there is no row in the volume
                        table for volume vol2.
                    2.  Volume table indicates that volume vol1 resides in
                        library lib1 in slot st1.  The opposite side volume
                        is vol2.  However, the row in the volume table for
                        volume vol2 indicates that the opposite side of
                        volume vol2 is vol4, which is different than vol1.
                    3.  Volume table indicates that volume vol1 resides in
                        library lib1 in slot st1.  The opposite side volume
                        is vol2.  However, the row in the volume table for
                        volume vol2 indicates that volume vol2 resides in
                        library lib2, which is different than lib1.
                    4.  Volume table indicates that volume vol1 resides in
                        library lib1 in slot st1.  The opposite side volume
                        is vol2.  However, the row in the volume table for
                        volume vol2 indicates that volume vol2 resides in
                        slot st2, which is different than st1.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Make the appropriate corrections in
          the volume table in the DB2 optical configuration database using
          SPUFI.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR1000I OAM verb command execution scheduled.
          Explanation:  The operator entered a command of the form:
                F OAM,verb,operand(s)
          The command has been scheduled for processing by the appropriate
          component in the object access method (OAM) address space.
          In the message text:
          verb      The command verb entered by the operator.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system processes the command.  When processing
          completes, another message is issued to inform the operator of the
          result.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR1010I OAM verb command execution failed.
          Explanation:  The operator entered a command of the form:
                F OAM,verb,operand(s)
          An abnormal end has occurred during processing of the command by
          the OAM Operator Command Task.
          In the message text:
          verb      The command verb entered by the operator.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The command can not be processed, depending on when
          the error occurred.  OAM attempts to continue processing in
          degraded mode.
          The system sends messages about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Do not attempt to reenter the failing command
          until OAM has been stopped and restarted.  Schedule an OAM restart
          at the earliest convenient time.
          System Programmer Response:  Notify the service representative.  If
          the error recurs and the program is not in error, search problem
          reporting data bases for a fix for the problem.  If no fix exists,
          contact the IBM Support Center.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR1050I Command rejected.  Verb verb invalid.
          Explanation:  The operator entered a command of the form:
                F OAM,verb,operand(s)
          The command verb is not one of those recognized by object access
          method (OAM):
          °   DISPLAY or D
          °   EJECT or E
          °   LABEL or L
          °   START or S
          °   STOP or P
          °   VARY or V
          In the message text:
          verb      The command verb entered by the operator.  If the verb is
                    more than eight characters long, only the first eight
                    characters appear in the message.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system rejects the command.
          Operator Response:  Determine the cause of the error, then enter a
          command with the correct verb.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR1051I Command rejected.  Invalid syntax.
          Explanation:  The operator entered a command of the form:
                F OAM,verb,operand(s)
          The command syntax is incorrect.  Most of the possible syntax
          errors are the result of misplaced commas:  a zero length verb, a
          zero length operand, or more than two operands.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system rejects the command.
          Operator Response:  Determine the cause of the error, then enter a
          command with the correct syntax.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR1052I Command rejected.  Operand operand too long.
          Explanation:  The operator entered a command of the form:
                F OAM,verb,operand(s)
          An operand is more than eight characters long, or a volume serial
          number operand is more than six characters long.
          In the message text:
          operand   The operand specified.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system rejects the command.
          Operator Response:  Determine the cause of the error, then enter a
          command with a correct operand.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR1053I Command rejected.  Operand operand extraneous.
          Explanation:  The operator entered a command of the form:
                F OAM,verb,operand(s)
          More operands are entered than are required by correct command
          syntax.
          In the message text:
          operand   The operand specified.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system rejects the command.
          Operator Response:  Determine the cause of the error, then enter a
          command with required operands only.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR1054I Command rejected.  Required operand missing.
          Explanation:  The operator entered a command of the form:
                F OAM,verb,operand(s)
          A required operand is missing from the command.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system rejects the command.
          Operator Response:  Determine the cause of the error, then enter a
          command with all required operands.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR1056I Command rejected.  L= operand invalid for verb verb.
          Explanation:  The operator entered a command of the form:
                F OAM,verb,operand(s),L=cca
          A location operand was specified for the verb.  The location
          operand is valid only for the verb DISPLAY.
          In the message text:
          verb      The command verb entered by the operator.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system rejects the command.
          Operator Response:  Determine the cause of the error, then enter a
          command using the optional location operand only for verb DISPLAY.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR1057I Command rejected.  Invalid L= operand.
          Explanation:  The operator entered a command of the form:
                F OAM,DISPLAY,operand(s),L=cca
          The location operand had an incorrect format.  The valid location
          operand formats are: L=CCA, L=CC, L=C, L=CA, or L=A where C is a
          numeric value from 0 through 9 and A is an alphabetic character.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system rejects the command.
          Operator Response:  Determine the cause of the error, then enter a
          command with a valid location operand.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR1060I Command rejected.  Library name libname undefined.
          Explanation:  The operator entered a command which requires the
          specification of a library name:
                DISPLAY SMS,LIBRARY(library-name),DETAIL
                F OAM,START,LIBMGT,library-name
          The library name is not defined in the optical configuration
          database.
          In the message text:
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system rejects the command.
          Operator Response:  Determine the cause of the error, then enter a
          command with a valid library name.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR1061I Command rejected.  Drive name drvename undefined.
          Explanation:  The operator entered a command which requires the
          specification of a drive name:
                DISPLAY SMS,DRIVE(drivename),DETAIL
          The drive name is not defined in the optical configuration
          database.
          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system rejects the command.
          Operator Response:  Determine the cause of the error, then enter a
          command with a valid drive name.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR1062I Command rejected.  Storage group name strgrpnm undefined.
          Explanation:  The operator entered a command which requires the
          specification of a storage group name:
                F OAM,DISPLAY,GROUP,sgroup-name
          The storage group name is not defined in the optical configuration
          database.
          In the message text:
          strgrpnm  The storage group name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system rejects the command.
          Operator Response:  Determine the cause of the error, then enter a
          command with a valid storage group name.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR1063I Command rejected.  Volume serial number volser invalid.
          Explanation:  The operator entered a command which requires the
          specification of a volume serial number:
                F OAM,DISPLAY,VOL,volser
                F OAM,EJECT,volser
                F OAM,START,RECOVERY,volser
          The volume serial number does not conform to MVS volume serial
          number naming conventions.
          In the message text:
          volser    The volume serial number.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system rejects the command.
          Operator Response:  Determine the cause of the error, then enter a
          command with a valid volume serial number.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR1064I Command rejected.  Volume serial number volser undefined.
          Explanation:  The operator entered a command which requires the
          specification of a volume serial number:
                F OAM,DISPLAY,VOL,volser
                F OAM,EJECT,volser
                F OAM,START,RECOVERY,volser
          The volume serial number is not defined in the optical
          configuration database.
          In the message text:
          volser    The volume serial number.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system rejects the command.
          Operator Response:  Determine the cause of the error, then enter a
          command with a valid volume serial number.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR1070I Command rejected.  OAM termination in progress.
          Explanation:  The operator entered a command of the form:
                F OAM,verb,operand(s)
          Object access method (OAM) address space abnormal end is in
          progress.  The only command which is accepted during this time is
          DISPLAY or D.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system rejects the command.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR1071I Command rejected.  OSMC is not installed.
          Explanation:  The operator has entered a command which must be
          processed by the object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component.
          The following is a list of commands:
          °   F OAM,START,OSMC
          °   F OAM,START,LIBMGT,library-name
          °   F OAM,START,RECOVERY,volser
          °   F OAM,START,DASDSM,storage-group-name
          °   F OAM,STOP,OSMC
          °   F OAM,DISPLAY,OSMC
          °   F OAM,DISPLAY,OSMC,resource-name
          The OAM address space was initialized without OSMC.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system rejects the command.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR1072I Command rejected.  OAM initialization in progress.
          Explanation:  The operator entered a command of the form:
                F OAM,verb,operand(s)
          Object access method (OAM) address space initialization is in
          progress.  No operator command is accepted until initialization is
          complete.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system rejects the command.
          Operator Response:  Wait until message CBR0002I is issued, then
          reenter the command.
   __________________________________________________________________________
 | CBR1073I Command rejected.  OSMC is not active.
 |        Explanation:  The operator has entered a command that must be
 |        processed by the OAM Storage Management Component.
 |            F OAM,START,OSMC
 |            F OAM,START,STORGRP,storage-group-name
 |            F OAM,START,LIBMGT,library-name
 |            F OAM,START,DASDSM,storage-group-name
 |            F OAM,START,RECOVERY,volser
 |            F OAM,START,OBJRECV,collection-name,object-name
 |            F OAM,STOP,OSMC
 |            F OAM,STOP,STORGRP,storage-group-name
 |            DISPLAY SMS,OSMC
 |            DISPLAY SMS,OSMC,resource-name
 |        The OAM address space was initialized with OSMC, but DB2 has
 |        stopped, which has caused OSMC to become inactive.
 |        Source:  Object access method (OAM)
 |        System Action:  The command is rejected.
 |        Operator Response:  None.
 |        System Programmer Response:   Restart DB2.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR1100I OAM status:
            TOT  USE  TOT  USE  AVL  TOT  USE  AVL  TOT  USE
            LIB  LIB  DRV  DRV  DRV  LDR  LDR  LDR  SDR  SDR
            aaa  bbb  ccc  ddd  eee  fff  ggg  hhh  iii  jjj
            AVL  SCR  REQ
            SDR  VOL  CT
            kkk  lll  mmm
          Explanation:  The operator entered the following command:
                DISPLAY SMS,OAM
          A display of object access method (OAM) address space status is
          generated.  The fields displayed in the data line of the multi-line
          message are as follows:
          aaa  Total number of optical libraries in the configuration.
          bbb  Number of usable optical libraries (online and operational).
          ccc  Total number of optical drives in the configuration.
          ddd  Number of usable optical drives.
          eee  Number of available optical drives (online, operational, and
               not currently in use).
          fff  Total number of library optical drives in the configuration.
          ggg  Number of usable library optical drives.
          hhh  Number of available library optical drives.
          iii  Total number of stand-alone optical drives in the
               configuration.
          jjj  Number of usable stand-alone optical drives.
          kkk  Number of available stand-alone optical drives.
          lll  Number of scratch optical volumes in the optical configuration
               database.
          mmm  Total number of read and write requests waiting to be
               scheduled.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR1110I OAM library status:
            LIBRARY  ATT USE AVL TOT EMP SCR PT CTC  ON OP IO
            NAME     DRV DRV DRV SLT SLT VOL    ADDR
            libname  aaa bbb ccc ddd eee fff g  hhhh i  j  k
            LIB   RECOV
            CMD   CMD
            lbcmd rccmd
          Explanation:  The operator entered one of the following commands:
                DISPLAY SMS,LIBRARY(library-name),DETAIL
                DISPLAY SMS,LIBRARY(ALL),DETAIL
          A display of object access method (OAM) library status is
          generated.  When a library name is supplied, there is one data line
          describing the specified library; when ALL is supplied, there is
          one data line for each library in the configuration.  The fields
          displayed in each data line of the multi-line message are as
          follows:
          libname  Name of the optical library.
          aaa      Number of optical drives attached to the optical library.
          bbb      Number of usable optical drives (online and operational).
          ccc      Number of available optical drives (online, operational,
                   and not currently in use).
          ddd      Total number of storage slots in the optical library.
          eee      Number of empty storage slots in the optical library.
          fff      Number of scratch volumes in the optical library.
          g        Active path to the optical library is as follows:
                   P   Primary.
                   A   Alternate.
          hhhh     MVS/ESA device number of the active CTC.
          i        Optical library online status is as follows:
                   Y   Online.
                   N   Offline.
                   P   Pending offline.
          j        Optical library operational status is as follows:
                   Y   Operational.
                   N   Not operational.
          k        Optical library input/output station operational status is
                   as follows:
                   Y   Operational.
                   N   Not operational.
          lbcmd    Library command most recently sent to the optical library.
          rccmd    Recovery command most recently sent to the optical
                   library.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR1115I No libraries defined to OAM.
          Explanation:  The operator entered the following command:
                DISPLAY SMS,LIBRARY(ALL),DETAIL
          There are no libraries defined in the optical configuration
          database.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR1120I OAM drive status:
            DRIVE    TY  LIBRARY  ON  OP  AV  WP  CTC  SCS  LUN
            NAME         NAME                     ADDR
            drvname  a   libname  b   c   d   w   eeee   f    g
            MOUNT   PEND
            VOLUME  VOLUME
            mntvol  pndvol
          Explanation:  The operator entered one of the following commands:
                DISPLAY SMS,DRIVE(drivename),DETAIL
                DISPLAY SMS,DRIVE(ALL),DETAIL
          A display of object access method (OAM) drive status is requested.
          When a drive name is supplied, there is one data line describing
          the specified drive; when no drive name is supplied, there is one
          data line for each drive in the configuration.  The fields
          displayed in each data line of the multi-line message are as
          follows:
          drvname  Name of the optical drive.
          a        Optical drive type is as follows:
                   L   Library.
                   S   Stand-alone.
c.cc 8
          libname  Name of the library to which the optical drive is
                   attached.  For a stand-alone optical drive, this field
                   contains 'STDALONE'.
          b        Optical drive online status is as follows:
                   Y   Online.
                   N   Offline.
                   P   Pending offline.
          c        Optical drive operational status is as follows:
                   Y   Operational.
                   N   Not operational.
          d        Optical drive availability status is as follows:
                   Y   Available.  The optical drive is online, operational,
                       and not busy.
                   N   Not available.
          w        Write Protection status is as follows:
                   Y   Write protection is on. Writing to this drive is not
                       allowed.
                   N   Write protection is off. Writing to this drive is
                       allowed.
                       The write protection status will reflect the switch
                       setting as of the last volume mount, vary online or
                       drive error processing.
          eeee     MVS/ESA device number of the CTC which is used to
                   communicate with the optical drive.
          f        SCSI bus address of the optical drive on the SCSI
                   interface.
          g        Logical unit number of the optical drive on the SCSI
                   interface.
          mntvol   Volume serial number of the volume which is currently
                   mounted on the optical drive.   If there is no mounted
                   volume, this field is blank.
          pndvol   Volume serial number of the volume for which a mount is
                   pending on the optical drive.   If there is no pending
                   mount, this field is blank.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR1130I OAM storage group status:
            STORAGE  TY WRIT  REQ ACT START
            GROUP       VOLS  CT  DRV THRES
            sgname    a  bbb cccc ddd eeeee
            LIBRARY
            NAMES
            libname1 libname2 libname3 libname4
            libname5 libname6 libname7 libname8
          Explanation:  The operator entered one of the following commands:
                F OAM,DISPLAY,GROUP,storage-group-name
                F OAM,DISPLAY,GROUP
          A display of object access method (OAM) storage group  status is
          requested.  When a storage group name is supplied, there is one
          data line describing the specified storage group; when no storage
          group name is supplied,
c.cc 8
          there is one data line for each storage group in the configuration.
          The fields displayed in each data line are as follows:
          sgname    Name of the optical storage group.
          a         Optical storage group type is as follows:
                    B   Backup.
                    G   Group.
 |                  N   Nongroup.  Currently not used.
                    S   Scratch.
          bbb       Number of volumes in the optical storage group which have
                    space available for writing objects.
          cccc      Number of write requests for the optical storage group
                    which are currently pending in OAM.
          ddd       Number of drives which are currently processing write
                    requests for the optical storage group.
          eeeee     Optical drive start-up threshold.  When the number of
                    requests per active optical drive exceeds this threshold,
                    another drive can be started.
          libname1 libname2 libname3 libname4 libname5 libname6 libname7
                    libname8
                    Names of the one to eight optical libraries where volumes
                    from the optical storage group or backup optical storage
                    group can reside.  If volumes from the optical storage
                    group or backup optical storage group reside on the
                    shelf, then the first optical library name is the name of
                    the pseudo library and the other seven names are blank.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR1135I No storage groups defined to OAM.
          Explanation:  The operator entered the following command:
                F OAM,DISPLAY,GROUP
          There are no storage groups defined in the storage management
          subsystem (SMS) activate configuration.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR1140I OAM volume status:
            VOLUME STORAGE  RD WR WP MOUNT    EXPIRE   FREE
                   GROUP             DATE     DATE     SPAC
            volser sgname   a  b  c  dddddddd eeeeeeee fff%
            oppvol sgname   a  b  c  dddddddd eeeeeeee fff%
            MOUNTED  PENDING  REQ
            DRIVE    DRIVE    CT
            mdrvname pdrvname gggg
            mdrvname pdrvname gggg
            {LIBRARY: libname | SHELF LOC: shelfloc}
            {OWNER: owner information}
            [status]
          Explanation:  The operator entered the following command:
                F OAM,DISPLAY,VOL,volser
          A display of object access method (OAM) volume status is requested.
          Status is reported for the requested optical volume and for its
          opposite side volume.  The fields displayed in each data line are
          as follows:
          volser    Volume serial number of the requested optical volume.
          oppvol    Volume serial number of the opposite side volume.
          sgname    Name of the storage group to which the optical volume
                    belongs.
          a         Optical volume readability status is as follows:
                    Y   Readable.
                    N   Unreadable.
          b         Optical volume writability status is as follows:
                    Y   Writable.
                    N   Unwritable.
          c         Optical volume write protection status is as follows:
                    Y   Write protected.
                    N   Not write protected.
          dddddddd  Date the optical volume was last mounted, in the format
                    YY-MM-DD.
          eeeeeeee  Expiration date of the optical volume, in the format
                    YY-MM-DD.
          fff%      Percentage of free space on the optical volume.  For a
                    full optical volume, this field contains 'FULL'.
          mdrvname  Name of the drive where this optical volume is mounted.
                    If the optical volume is not mounted, this field contains
                    blanks.
          pdrvname  Name of the drive where a mount is pending for this
                    optical volume.  If no mount is pending, this field
                    contains blanks.
          gggg      Number of specific requests for this optical  volume
                    which are currently pending in OAM.
          libname   Name of the library in which the optical volume resides.
                    This field appears only for a library optical volume.
          shelfloc  Shelf location where the optical volume is to be found.
                    This field appears only for a shelf optical volume.
          owner information
                    Owner information from the optical volume label.
          status    Additional optical volume status is as follows:  Optical
                    volumes not in assigned optical library slot.  The
                    optical library slot assigned to these optical volumes is
                    empty or contains different optical volumes.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR1190I DISPLAY rejected.  Resource type restype invalid.
          Explanation:  The operator entered a command of the form:
                F OAM,DISPLAY,resource-type
          The resource type to be displayed is incorrect.  It must be one of
          the following:
          °   OAM
          °   LIB
          °   DRIVE
          °   VSET
          °   VOL
c.cc 8
          In the message text:
          restype   The resource type.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system rejects the command.
          Operator Response:  Determine the cause of the error, then enter a
          DISPLAY command with the correct resource type.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR1200I EJECT rejected.  Volume volser not in a library.
          Explanation:  The operator entered a command of the form:
                F OAM,EJECT,volser
          The specified volume does not reside in a library.
          In the message text:
          volser    The volume serial number.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system rejects the command.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR1201I EJECT rejected.  Volume volser-1 or volser-2 busy.
          Explanation:  The operator has entered a command of the form:
                F OAM,EJECT,volser
          The specified volume volser-1, or its opposite side volume
          volser-2, is busy and therefore not available to be ejected from
          the library where it currently resides.  A volume is busy when a
          mount is pending, or when a pending unit of work has specifically
          requested it.
          In the message text:
          volser-1
          volser-2  The volume serial numbers.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The command is rejected.
          Operator Response:  Use the DISPLAY SMS,VOL command to determine
          why the volume is busy.  The EJECT command can be reentered at a
          later time.
          System Programmer Response:  None.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR1202I EJECT rejected.  Library libname cannot eject volume volser.
          Explanation:  The operator entered a command of the form:
                F OAM,EJECT,volser
          The library in which the specified volume resides is not currently
          capable of ejecting an optical disk.  The library is offline or not
          operational, or the library input/output station is not
          operational.
          In the message text:
          libname   The library name.
          volser    The volume serial number.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system rejects the command.
          Operator Response:  Use the OAM DISPLAY command to determine
          library status.  If the library is currently offline, use the OAM
          VARY command to VARY it online.  Once the library is online,
          reenter the EJECT command.  If either the library or the
          input/output station is not operational, contact a service
          representative.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR1203I EJECT rejected.  Invalid operand.
          Explanation:  The operator entered a command of the form:
                F OAM,EJECT,volser,LOCATION
          The specified operand, LOCATION or L, is incorrect.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system rejects the command.
          Operator Response:  Determine the cause of the error, then enter a
          command with the correct operand syntax.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR1204I EJECT rejected.  Volume volser-1 or volser-2 EJECT already in
          process.
          Explanation:  The operator entered a command of the form:
                F OAM,EJECT,volser
          or an ISMF user has entered a line operator eject.  The specified
          volume, or its opposite side volume, are in the process of being
          ejected from a previous eject command.
          In the message text:
          volser-1
          volser-2  The volume serial numbers.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system rejects the command.
          Operator Response:  Use the OAM DISPLAY command to determine the
          volume status.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR1300I LABEL rejected.  No stand-alone drive defined.
          Explanation:  The operator entered a command of the form:
                F OAM,LABEL
          There are no stand-alone optical drives defined in the optical
          configuration database.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system rejects the command.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR1301I LABEL rejected.  No stand-alone drive usable.
          Explanation:  The operator entered a command of the form:
                F OAM,LABEL
          All stand-alone optical drives in the configuration are either
          offline or not operational.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system rejects the command.
          Operator Response:  Use the OAM DISPLAY command to display drive
          status.  If there is a stand-alone drive which is currently
          offline, use the OAM VARY command to VARY it online, then reenter
          the LABEL command.  If all stand-alone drives are not operational,
          contact a service representative.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR1400I STOP rejected.  Component name name invalid.
          Explanation:  The operator entered a command of the form:
                F OAM,STOP,name
          The name of the component to be stopped is incorrect.  It must be
          object access method (OAM) or OSMC.
          In the message text:
          name      The name of the incorrect component.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system rejects the command.
          Operator Response:  Determine the cause of the error, then enter a
          STOP command with the correct component name.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR1500I START rejected.  Component name name invalid.
          Explanation:  The operator entered a command of the form:
                F OAM,START,name
          The name of the component to be started is incorrect.  It must be
          LIBMGT, OSMC, or RECOVERY.
          In the message text:
          name      The name of the incorrect component.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system rejects the command.
          Operator Response:  Determine the cause of the error, then enter a
          START command with the correct component name.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR1600I VARY rejected.  Drive or library name name undefined.
          Explanation:  The operator entered a command of the form:
               VARY SMS,drive(name)
          The name specified in the command is not defined in the optical
          configuration database, either as a drive name or as a library
          name.
          In the message text:
          name      The specified name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system rejects the command.
          Operator Response:  Determine the cause of the error, then enter a
          VARY command with a valid drive or library name.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR1601I VARY rejected.  Status status invalid.
          Explanation:  The operator entered a command of the form:
                VARY SMS [DRIVE()],status
                         [LIB()]
          The status operand is neither ONLINE nor OFFLINE.
          In the message text:
          status    The incorrect status.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system rejects the command.
          Operator Response:  Determine the cause of the error, then enter a
          VARY command with the correct status operand.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR1602I Drive drvename already status.
          Explanation:  The operator entered a command of the form:
                VARY SMS,DRIVE(name),status
          The specified drive already has the requested status.
          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive.
          status    The requested status.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system does not process the command.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR1603I Library libname already status.
          Explanation:  The operator entered a command of the form:
                VARY SMS,LIBRARY(name),status
          The specified library already has the requested status.
          In the message text:
          libname   The library name.
          status    The requested status.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system does not process the command.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR1604I VARY rejected.  Cannot demount volume on drive drvename.
          Explanation:  The operator entered a command of the form:
                VARY SMS,DRIVE(name),OFFLINE
          A volume is currently mounted on the drive, which is attached to a
          library, and the library is either offline or not operational.
          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system rejects the command.  The drive is left
          in pending offline status; no new work will be scheduled to the
          drive.
          Operator Response:  If the library is offline, VARY it online, then
          reenter the VARY command.  If the library is not operational,
          contact a service representative.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR1610I Drive drvename in library libname online and operational.
          Explanation:  The operator entered a command of the form:
                VARY SMS,LIBRARY(name),OFFLINE
          The named drive is attached to the specified library and is online
          and operational.
          In the message text:
          libname   The library name.
          drvename  The drive.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  Message CBR1611D is issued.  In the response, the
          operator can confirm or cancel the VARY offline request.
          Operator Response:  Wait until message CBR1611D is issued, then
          respond as directed in the description of that message.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR1611D Reply 'U' to VARY library libname OFFLINE, 'C' to cancel.
          Explanation:  The operator entered a command of the form:
                VARY SMS,LIBRARY(name),OFFLINE
          Message CBR1610I has been issued.  Before allowing the named
          library to VARY offline, object access method (OAM) requires
          operator confirmation of the VARY offline request.
          In the message text:
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The OAM operator command processing component waits
          for a response from the operator.
          Operator Response:  Reply 'U' to confirm the VARY offline request,
          'C' to cancel it.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR1612I VARY library libname OFFLINE command canceled.
          Explanation:  The operator entered a command of the form:
                VARY SMS,LIBRARY(name),OFFLINE
          Pending messages CBR1610I and CBR1611D have been issued.  The
          operator responded 'C' to message CBR1611D, thereby cancelling to
          confirm the VARY library offline request.
          In the message text:
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system cancels the VARY command.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR1800I resname VARY completion notification error.  SSI RC = ssirc, SMS
          RC = smsrc, SMS REASON = smsrsncd.
          Explanation:  Following completion of VARY command processing for
          an optical library or an optical drive, object access method (OAM)
          tried to notify the Storage Management Address Space (SMAS) using
          the Subsystem Interface (SSI).  The SSI call failed.
          In the message text:
          resname   The name of the optical library or the optical drive.
          ssirc     The SSI return code.
          smsrc     The storage management subsystem (SMS) return code.
          smsrsncd  The SMS reason code.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM continues normal processing.  If a system
          initial program load (IPL) is performed, the online/offline status
          of the library or drive can not be correct following the IPL.
          The system sends messages about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Repeat the failing VARY command.  If the
          failure persists, notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For information on the SMS return
          codes and reason codes see MVS/DFP Version 3 Release 2: Diagnosis
          Reference.  If the error recurs and the program is not in error,
          search problem reporting data bases for a fix for the problem.  If
          no fix exists, contact the IBM Support Center.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR1900I START OAM rejected. OAM address space already active.
          Explanation:  A request has been made in the Storage Management
          Address Space (SMAS) to start the object access method (OAM)
          address space, but the OAM address space is already active.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system rejects the request.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR1910I {DISPLAY|VARY|STOP} OAM rejected.  OAM address space is not
          active.
          Explanation:  A request has been made in the Storage Management
          Address Space (SMAS) which requires processing in the object access
          method (OAM) address space.  The request is one of the following:
          °   Display the status of a resource in the OAM address space.
          °   Vary the online/offline status of an optical library or an
              optical drive.
          °   Stop the OAM address space.
          The OAM address space is not active or is in the process of
          starting up or shutting down.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system rejects the request.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR1920I {START|DISPLAY|VARY|STOP} OAM not scheduled.  Command scheduling
          error.
          Explanation:  A request has been made in the Storage Management
          Address Space (SMAS) which requires the scheduling of a command for
          processing in the object access method (OAM) address space.  The
          request is one of the following:
          °   Start the OAM address space.
          °   Display the status of a resource in the OAM address space.
          °   Vary the online/offline status of an optical library or an
              optical drive.
          °   Stop the OAM address space.
          The MVS command scheduling service (MGCR - SVC 34) did not schedule
          the command.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system does not process the request.  The
          system sends messages about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  The command scheduling facility issues its own
          message describing the error it has detected.  If you are able to
          correct the error, do so; if not, contact the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Ensure that load module CBRFCMD is in
          an authorized program facility (APF)-authorized library.  If it is,
          contact the service representative for assistance.  If the error
          recurs and the program is not in error, search problem reporting
          data bases for a fix for the problem.  If no fix exists, contact
          the IBM Support Center.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR1930I {START|DISPLAY|VARY|STOP|UNKNOWN} OAM command execution failed.
          Explanation:  A request has been made in the Storage Management
          Address Space (SMAS) which requires the scheduling of a command for
          processing in the object access method (OAM) address space.  The
          request is one of the following:
          °   Start the OAM address space.
          °   Display the status of a resource in the OAM address space.
          °   Vary the online/offline status of an optical library or an
              optical drive.
          °   Stop the OAM address space.
          An abnormal end has occurred in the SMAS during the preparation for
          command scheduling.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The request can not have been scheduled, depending
          on when the error occurred.  The system sends messages about the
          job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  If a VARY SMS or DISPLAY SMS command has
          failed, reenter the failing command.  If the failure persists,
          notify the service representative.
          System Programmer Response:  If the error recurs and the program is
          not in error, search problem reporting data bases for a fix for the
          problem.  If no fix exists, contact the IBM Support Center.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR2000I Volume volser marked unwritable.
          Explanation:  Three consecutive attempts to write on optical an
          volume have failed with a permanent error on the disk.  Object
          access method (OAM) marked the volume unwritable in the optical
          configuration database.
          In the message text:
          volser    The volume serial number.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM attempts to retry the failing request on
          another volume.  Any future request to write on the unwritable
          volume fails; a request to read an object that was previously
          written on the volume is allowed.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR2001I Volumes volser-1 and volser-2 not found in library libname.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) attempted to mount a
          library-resident optical volume to read or write a data object on
          the volume.  The library slot where the volume resides, according
          to information in the optical configuration database, is empty or
          contains a different volume.  This error is probably the result of
          improper manual movement of library volumes.
          In the message text:
          volser-1
          volser-2  The volume serial numbers.
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM marks the volumes lost.  If the current request
          is non-specific, an attempt is made to locate another suitable
          volume.  If no other volume is found, or if the current request is
          for the specific volume, OAM fails the request.  Any future
          specific request for either volume fails.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Determine where the volumes are
          actually located.  Stop the OAM address space.  Using interactive
          SPUFI services, update the volume table and slot table entries in
          the optical configuration database to present the correct
          information.  Start the OAM address space.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR2002I Cross-memory copy error between OAM address space and ASID asid.
          Explanation:  A user has requested the writing of a data object to
          an optical volume or the reading of a data object from an optical
          volume.  An error occurred during the attempt to copy either data
          or control information cross-memory between user the address space
          and the OAM address space.
          In the message text:
          asid      The address space identifier.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM cancels the user request.  Request completion
          is not signaled to the user address space, since the likely result
          is another cross-memory failure.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  This is a probable user error.  This
          error can follow the premature end of the user address space, or
          the premature end of the task in the user address space which
          requested OAM services, or the premature release of the storage
          containing the buffer from which the data object is to be written
          or into which the data object is to be read.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR2100I Volumes volser-1 and volser-2 entered into library libname.
          Explanation:  The operator entered an optical disk into the
          input/output station of a library and OAM scheduled a request to
          enter the optical disk into the library.  That request has now been
          successfully processed.  The two volumes that constitute the
          optical disk are in the library and available for use by OAM.
          In the message text:
          volser-1
          volser-2  The volume serial numbers.
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The newly entered volumes will be used by OAM as
          they are needed.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR2101I Optical disk entry into library libname failed.
          Explanation:  The operator entered an optical disk into the
          input/output station of a library and object access method (OAM)
          scheduled a request to enter the optical disk into the library.
          That request failed to process successfully, as noted in a previous
          message to the operator.
          In the message text:
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          Operator Response:  Follow the instructions on the library error
          message which accompanied the failure.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR2102I LABEL function complete for volumes volser-1 and volser-2.
          Explanation:  The operator entered a command of the form:
                F OAM,LABEL
          Object access method (OAM) scheduled a request to write volume
          labels on an unlabeled optical disk.  That request has successfully
          processed.
          In the message text:
          volser-1
          volser-2  The volume serial numbers.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The newly labeled volumes will be used by OAM as
          they are needed.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR2103I LABEL function on stand-alone drive drvename failed.
          Explanation:  The operator entered a command of the form:
                F OAM,LABEL
          Object access method (OAM) scheduled a request to write volume
          labels on an unlabeled optical disk.  That request failed to
          process successfully, as noted in a previous message to the
          operator.
          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          Operator Response:  Follow the instructions on the drive error
          message which accompanied the failure.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR2104I Drive drvename now online.
          Explanation:  The operator has entered a command of the form:
                VARY SMS,DRIVE(name),ONLINE
          The specified drive has been VARYed online, as requested.
          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The drive is now available for use by OAM.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR2105I Drive drvename VARY ONLINE failed.
          Explanation:  The operator has entered a command of the form:
                VARY SMS,DRIVE(name),ONLINE
          The attempt to VARY the specified drive online has failed, for the
          reason noted in a previous message to the operator.  The most
          likely reason for the failure is the lack of an operational path to
          the drive.
          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The drive is left offline.
          Operator Response:  Notify the service representative.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR2106I Drive drvename now offline.
          Explanation:  The operator has entered a command of the form:
                VARY SMS,DRIVE(name),OFFLINE
          The specified drive has been VARYed offline, as requested.
          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The drive is no longer available for use by OAM.
c.cc 10
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR2107I Drive drvename VARY OFFLINE failed.
          Explanation:  The operator has entered a command of the form:
                VARY SMS,DRIVE(name),OFFLINE
          The attempt to VARY the specified drive offline has failed, for the
          reason noted in a previous message to the operator.  The most
          likely reason for the failure is the inability to demount the
          volume that is currently mounted on the drive.
          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The drive is left pending offline.  This means that
          no new work will be scheduled to the drive.  If there is a volume
          that cannot be demounted, that volume is unavailable until the
          situation is corrected.
          Operator Response:  Notify the service representative.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR2200I Scratch volumes volser-1 and volser-2 added to storage group
          strgrpnm.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) has assigned the two
          scratch volumes that constitute an optical disk to a storage group.
          Either there is no free space left on the volumes that are
          currently in the storage group, or there are not enough volumes
          with free space to occupy all the optical drives which have been
          started for the storage group.
          In the message text:
          volser-1
          volser-2  The volume serial numbers.
          strgrpnm  The storage group name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The newly added volumes will be used by OAM for the
          writing of data objects directed to the storage group.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR2210I No empty slots in library libname.  Disk to be ejected.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) has determined that
          scratch volumes are needed in a library, but there are no empty
          storage slots in the library.  A request has been sent to the OAM
          Storage Management Component to select an appropriate optical disk
          and eject it from the library.
          In the message text:
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM schedules the ejection processing, then issues
          message CBR2211E, requesting the operator to insert an unlabeled
          optical disk into the library input/output station.
          Operator Response:  Wait for the optical disk to be ejected from
          the library before following the instructions in message CBR2211E.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR2211E Enter unlabeled optical disks into library libname to relieve the
          out of space condition in storage group strgrpnm.
          Explanation:  A request has been made to write a data object on a
          volume which resides in a library.  All volumes residing in the
          library and belonging to the requested storage group are full or
          are currently in use, and there are no scratch volumes in the
          library.  This message requests the operator to prepare scratch
          volumes for use in the library.
c.cc 6
          In the message text:
          libname   The library name.
          strgrpnm  The storage group name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  If space is available on a volume in another
          library, and if the request is eligible to use that library, the
          write operation is processed normally.  If space is available on a
          volume that is currently in use, the write request waits until the
          volume becomes available.  If no space is available on a drive for
          which the request is eligible, the request fails.
          Operator Response:  Insert an unlabeled optical disk into the
          library input/output station.  Be prepared to supply volume label
          information for the two volumes on the disk.  Message CBR2211E is
          an action message which is removed from the console when the first
          unlabeled disk has been successfully entered into the library.  If
          possible, insert several unlabeled disks into the library; consult
          your system programmer.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR2212E Use the OAM LABEL command to label optical disks for shelf use to
          relieve the out of space condition in storage group strgrpnm.
          Explanation:  A request has been made to write a data object on a
          volume which resides on the shelf.  All volumes residing on the
          shelf and belonging to the requested storage group are full or are
          currently in use, and there are no scratch volumes on the shelf.
          This message requests the operator to prepare scratch volumes for
          shelf use.
          In the message text:
          strgrpnm  The storage group name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  If space is available on a volume that is currently
          in use, the write request waits until the volume becomes available.
          If no space is available, the request fails.
          Operator Response:  Use the OAM LABEL operator command to request
          the labeling of an optical disk.  Be prepared to supply volume
          label information for the two volumes on the disk.  Message
          CBR2212E is an action message which is removed from the console
          when the first disk has been successfully labeled for shelf use.
          If possible, label several disks; consult your system programmer.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR2213I No space left in storage group strgrpnm.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) has been requested to
          write a data object to a volume in a storage group.  All the
          volumes assigned to the storage group are full.  If the storage
          group is library-resident, there are no scratch volumes available
          in the library or libraries.  If the storage group is
          shelf-resident, there are no scratch volumes available on the
          shelf.
          In the message text:
          strgrpnm  The storage group name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The write request failed.  If the storage group is
          library-resident, message CBR2211E has already been issued for each
          library.  If the storage group is shelf-resident, message CBR2212E
          has already been issued.  Either message requests the creation of
          scratch volumes by writing volume labels on an unlabeled optical
          disk.
          Operator Response:  Follow the procedure described in message
          CBR2211E or message CBR2212E.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR2500I No drive usable for optical disk entry into library libname.
          Explanation:  The operator has entered an optical disk into the
          input/output station of a library.  In order to enter the optical
          disk into the library, one of the optical drives attached to the
          library must be used to perform volume label verification.  All
          these drives are either offline or not operational.
          In the message text:
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The optical disk is not entered into the library.
          Operator Response:  Remove the optical disk from the library
          input/output station.  Use the OAM DISPLAY command to display drive
          status.   If there is a library-attached drive that is currently
          offline, use the OAM VARY command to VARY it online, then reenter
          the optical disk into the library input/output station.  If all
          library-attached drives are not operational, contact a service
          representative.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR2501I Optical disk entry into library libname rejected.  OAM
          termination in progress.
          Explanation:  The operator has entered an optical disk into the
          input/output station of a library.  However, object access method
          (OAM) address space end is already in progress, and no new work can
          be scheduled.
          In the message text:
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The optical disk is not entered into the library.
          Operator Response:  Remove the optical disk from the library
          input/output station.  When the OAM address space has been
          restarted, try the optical disk entry again.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR2510I Volume Entry Scheduler failure for library libname.
          Explanation:  The operator has entered an optical disk into the
          input/output station of a library.  In order to enter the optical
          disk into the library, the volume entry scheduler has been called
          to schedule the use of one of the library-attached optical drives
          to perform volume label verification.  An abnormal end has occurred
          during volume entry scheduler processing.
          In the message text:
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The optical disk can not be entered into the
          library, depending on when the error occurred.  OAM attempts to
          continue processing in degraded mode.  The system sends messages
          about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Do not attempt to enter the optical disk until
          OAM has been stopped and restarted.  Schedule an OAM restart at the
          earliest convenient time.
          System Programmer Response:  Notify the service representative.  If
          the error recurs and the program is not in error, search problem
          reporting data bases for a fix for the problem.  If no fix exists,
          contact the IBM Support Center.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR2550I Optical disk entry into library libname scheduled.
          Explanation:  The operator entered an optical disk into the
          input/output station of a library.  Object access method (OAM)
          scheduled a request to enter the optical disk into the library.
          In the message text:
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  When an optical drive that is attached to the
          library is available, the optical disk will be mounted, and volume
          label verification will be performed.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR2600A Specify shelf location for volumes volser-1 and volser-2.
          Explanation:  A request has been made to eject an optical disk from
          a library.  The request can have been made by the object access
          method (OAM) Storage Management Component or it can have been made
          by operator command:
                F OAM,EJECT,volser
          The operator is asked to provide the shelf location where the
          optical disk cartridge is to be stored, so the information can be
          recorded in the optical configuration database.  The response can
          be up to 32 characters in length and can contain any information
          that the installation considers pertinent.  The response is stored
          as supplied with no format or content check.
          In the message text:
          volser-1
          volser-2  The volume serial numbers.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The OAM component, either OSMC or operator command
          processing, waits for a response from the operator.  When the
          response is received, it is stored in the two volume records in the
          optical configuration database.
          Operator Response:  Supply the requested information.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR2610I Volume Eject Scheduler failure for volume volser.
          Explanation:  A request has been made either by the operator or by
          the object access method (OAM) Storage Management Component to
          eject an optical disk from a library.  The volume eject scheduler
          has been called to schedule the request for processing.  An
          abnormal end has occurred during volume eject scheduler processing.
          In the message text:
          volser    The volume serial number.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The optical disk can not be ejected from the
          library, depending on when the error occurred.  OAM attempts to
          continue processing in degraded mode.  The system sends messages
          about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Do not attempt to repeat the optical disk eject
          sequence until OAM has been stopped and restarted.  Schedule an OAM
          restart at the earliest convenient time.
          System Programmer Response:  Notify the service representative.  If
          the error recurs and the program is not in error, search problem
          reporting data bases for a fix for the problem.  If no fix exists,
          contact the IBM Support Center.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR3000I Storage unavailable for LTCB control block.  Library
          initialization terminated.
          Explanation:  The library control task attempted to get storage for
          the LTCB control block but the STORAGE macro failed.  This message
          is preceded by message CBR7004I, which contains the return code
          from the STORAGE macro.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  Library initialization stops.  The system sends
          messages about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Determine the cause of the STORAGE
          failure by referring to documentation for CBR7004I.  If the error
          recurs and the program is not in error, search problem reporting
          data bases for a fix for the problem.  If no fix exists, contact
          the IBM Support Center.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR3001A Remove cartridge from input station on library libname.  Place in
          shelf location shelfloc.
          Explanation:  A cartridge is in the input station either as a
          result of a request to eject a cartridge from a library, or because
          it was there when the unit was powered on or reset and object
          access method (OAM) is unsure how to proceed.  In the latter case,
          the shelf location is 'UNKNOWN'.
          In the message text:
          libname   The library name.
          shelf location
                    The shelf location.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system continues processing.
          Operator Response:  Remove the cartridge and close the input/output
          station door.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR3002I Library libname no longer usable.
          Explanation:  A major library component cannot be used until
          serviced.
          In the message text:
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The library is marked not operational. Requests are
          purged and the library driver task enters into the wait state.
          Operator Response:  See a previous error message for details.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR3003I Library libname now OFFLINE.
          Explanation:  The operator issued a request to VARY the library
          offline.  All queued requests have been serviced and the library is
          now offline.
          In the message text:
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The library is marked offline. No further requests
          will be honored until it is VARYed back online.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR3004I Library libname now ONLINE.
          Explanation:  The operator issued a request to VARY the library
          online.  All initialization procedures have completed successfully.
          In the message text:
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The library is marked online and the drive tasks
          are posted to ask for work.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR3100I Jam in library libname, fault code nnn.
          Explanation:  A command was issued to perform a library function;
          however, the command could not complete because of a jam in the
          library mechanism.
          In the message text:
          libname   The library name.
          nnn       The fault code describes what mechanism is at fault.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The library is marked not operational and cannot be
          used again until it is VARYed back online.  The system sends
          messages about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Obtain the SYS1.LOGREC error record.
          If the error recurs and the program is not in error, search problem
          reporting data bases for a fix for the problem.  If no fix exists,
          contact the IBM Support Center.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR3101I No slot available to store the cartridge in library libname.
          Explanation:  Two situations can cause this message:
          °   A cartridge is entered into the input/output station when no
              slots are free in the library
          °   The search for an empty slot to store the cartridge that is
              currently in the gripper has failed.  The gripper cannot be
              used.  Normally this should not happen, and reflects that the
              SLOT tables and OLIBRARY table do not match what is in the
              library.
          In the message text:
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  In situation one, a request to remove the cartridge
          is issued and the enter request is rejected.  In situation two, the
          library is marked not operational.  Any outstanding queued requests
          are purged and the library driver task enters the wait state.  The
          system sends messages about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  If entering a cartridge, remove it.  Start
          Library Space Management by entering the operator command F
          OAM,START,LIBMGT,libname.  If Library Space Management does not
          free a slot for cartridge, notify database administrator.
          System Programmer Response:  Check the tables against the contents
          of the library. If a cartridge has been left in the library, have a
          service representative remove it.  Obtain the SYS1.LOGREC error
          record.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR3102I Hardware component unusable in library libname.  Service
          required, fault code nnn.
          Explanation:  A command was issued to perform a library function.
          The command failed because of a hardware malfunction.
          In the message text:
          libname   The library name.
          nnn       The fault code describes what mechanism was at fault.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The component is marked offline and the error is
          marked permanent.  The system sends messages about the job to one
          of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Obtain the SYS1.LOGREC error record.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR3103I Slot slotname in library libname indicates it is full, fault code
          nnn.
          Explanation:  A STORE command was issued to put a cartridge in the
          specified slot, but sensors indicate the slot is full.
          In the message text:
          libname   The library name.
          slotname  The slot name.
          nnn       The fault code describes what mechanism was at fault.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The slot is marked unusable.  The cartridge is
          stored in another slot.  The system sends messages about the job to
          one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Check the optical configuration database to see
          if the slot is indeed full.  If it indicates it is empty, notify
          the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For information on library errors,
          consult FileNet OSAR Library Unit Product Description FileNet.
          Obtain the SYS1.LOGREC error record.  If the error recurs and the
          program is not in error, search problem reporting data bases for a
          fix for the problem.  If no fix exists, contact the IBM Support
          Center.
   __________________________________________________________________________
