   CBR3104I Drive drivenum in library libname failed to load, fault code nnn.
          Explanation:  An Insert command was issued but a library indicated
          that the cartridge did not go all the way into the drive.
          In the message text:
          drivenum  The drive number.
          libname   The library name.
          nnn       The fault code describes what mechanism was at fault.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The drive is marked not operational and cannot be
          used again until VARYed online.  The system sends messages about
          the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          System Programmer Response:  For information on library errors,
          consult Filenet OSAR Library Unit Product Description.  Obtain the
          SYS1.LOGREC error record.  If the error recurs and the program is
          not in error, search problem reporting data bases for a fix for the
          problem.  If no fix exists, contact the IBM Support Center.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR3105I Drive drivenum in library libname failed to unload, fault code
          nnn.
          Explanation:  A RETRACT command was issued to a library, but the
          drive failed to unload the cartridge successfully.
          In the message text:
          drivenum  The drive number.
          libname   The library name.
          nnn       The fault code describes what mechanism was at fault.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system marks the drive unusable and returns a
          permanent error to the caller.  The drive cannot be used until it
          is VARYed online.  The system sends messages about the job to one
          of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Use a VARY command to vary the drive online.
          If that fails, notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For information on library errors,
          consult Filenet OSAR Library Unit Product Description.  Obtain the
          SYS1.LOGREC error record.  If the error recurs and the program is
          not in error, search problem reporting data bases for a fix for the
          problem.  If no fix exists, contact the IBM Support Center.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR3106I Tables describing library libname may be invalid, fault code nnn.
          Explanation:  A command was issued to a library to perform a move
          action, but the slot, drive or gripper was in an unexpected state.
          In the message text:
          libname   The library name.
          nnn       The fault code describes what mechanism was at fault.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system returns a permanent error to the caller.
          The system sends messages about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Notify the data administrator of the error.
          System Programmer Response:  Use DB2 to get the tables in
          synchronization with the library.  Obtain the SYS1.LOGREC error
          record.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR3107W OAM I/O driver could not obtain storage while processing for
          name.
          Explanation:  When processing a library or drive command, there was
          insufficient storage for the I/O driver in subpool 245.  This is a
          severe problem and indicates a re-initial program load (IPL) is
          necessary.
          In the message text:
          name      The name of the library or drive.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system stops the I/O operation.  The system
          sends messages about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Get a dump and determine what
          component is using up the storage in the system queue area (SQA).
          Obtain the SYS1.LOGREC error record.  If the error recurs and the
          program is not in error, search problem reporting data bases for a
          fix for the problem.  If no fix exists, contact the IBM Support
          Center.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR3108I Invalid parameter list to the OAM I/O driver for name.
          Explanation:  When processing a library or drive command, there was
          an error in the parameter list passed to the I/O driver.  This is a
          program problem.
          In the message text:
          name      The name of the library or drive.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system stops the I/O operation.  The system
          sends messages about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Determine the source of the failure
          and notify the service representative.  Obtain the SYS1.LOGREC
          error record.  If the error recurs and the program is not in error,
          search problem reporting data bases for a fix for the problem.  If
          no fix exists, contact the IBM Support Center.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR3109I The OAM I/O driver was unable to establish an ESTAE while
          processing for name.
          Explanation:  When processing a library or drive command, the I/O
          driver failed to establish an ESTAE.
          In the message text:
          name      The name of the library or drive.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system stops the I/O operation.  The system
          sends messages about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Determine the source of the failure
          and notify the service representative.  Obtain the SYS1.LOGREC
          error record.  If the error recurs and the program is not in error,
          search problem reporting data bases for a fix for the problem.  If
          no fix exists, contact the IBM Support Center.
   __________________________________________________________________________
 | CBR3110I An I/O error occurred on the channel to channel adapter
 |        unit-number, error code error-code.
 |        Explanation:  When processing a channel command, there was an I/O
 |        error error-code.  on the channel to channel (CTC) adapter
 |        unit-number.
 |        In the message text:
 |        unit-number
 |                  The CTC adapter.
 |        error-code
 |                  The specified I/O error.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The I/O operation is stopped.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Determine the source of the failure
          and notify the service representative.
          Problem Determination:   Error codes
          °   Error Code  4 - Incorrect residual byte count
          °   Error Code XX - IOS error return code (IOSRC)
          Note:   Refer to MVS/ESA data areas (IOSB) for IOSRC return code
          definitions.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR3111I The OAM I/O driver timed out because a {Library|Drive} command
          for lib/drv-name was rejected.
          Explanation:  An error occurred when processing a lib/drv command
          for lib/drv-name. The device controller did not respond within 30
          seconds and the I/O driver timed out.  Either the device controller
          or the library is in error.
c.cc 6
          In the message text:
          lib/drv-name
                    The name of the library or drive.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The I/O operation is stopped.  The system sends
          messages about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Determine the source of the failure
          and notify the service representative.
          Obtain the SYS1.LOGREC error record.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR3112I OAM I/O driver abended with a code of xxx when issuing a command
          for name.
          Explanation:  When processing a library or drive command, the I/O
          driver abnormally ended with the specified ABEND code.
          In the message text:
          xxx       The specified completion code.
          name      The name of the library or drive.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system stops the I/O operation.  The system
          sends messages about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Determine the source of the failure
          and notify the service representative.  Obtain the SYS1.LOGREC
          error record.  If the error recurs and the program is not in error,
          search problem reporting data bases for a fix for the problem.  If
          no fix exists, contact the IBM Support Center.  Format the SVC dump
          with the interactive problem control system (IPCS).
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR3113I Drive drivenum in library libname not operational.
          Explanation:  An Insert command was issued but the library
          indicated that the door of the drive was closed, which implies a
          fault or no power.
          In the message text:
          drivenum  The drive number.
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The drive is marked not operational and cannot be
          used until it is VARYed back online.
          Operator Response:  Use a VARY command to vary the drive online.
          If that fails, notify the system programmer.  The system sends
          messages about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          System Programmer Response:  For information on library errors,
          consult Filenet OSAR Library Unit Product Description.  Obtain the
          SYS1.LOGREC error record.  If the error recurs and the program is
          not in error, search problem reporting data bases for a fix for the
          problem.  If no fix exists, contact the IBM Support Center.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR3114I Single sided cartridge in library libname invalid.
          Explanation:  A fault 148 or 248 occurred stating that the library
          gripper 1 or gripper 2 suspects that a cartridge is single-sided
          and is trying to insert the opposite side.
          In the message text:
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The error is treated as permanent.  The system
          sends messages about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Issue the MODIFY OAM,EJECT command to remove
          the cartridge from the library.  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For information on library errors,
          consult Filenet OSAR Library Unit Product Description.  Obtain the
          SYS1.LOGREC error record.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR3115I The OAM I/O driver timed out waiting for a response from
          {Library|Drive} lib/drv-name.
          Explanation:  When processing a library or drive command, the
          device controller did not respond within 30 minutes for a library
          calibrate command or 5 minutes for all other commands.  Either the
          device controller or the library is in error.
          In the message text:
          lib/drv-name
                    The name of the library or drive.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The I/O operation is stopped.  The system sends
          messages about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Determine the source of the failure
          and notify the service representative.  Obtain the SYS1.LOGREC
          error record.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR3116I J33 missing in the plug panel for library libname.
          Explanation:  As a result of service or a jam on a library, the J33
          pin was inadvertently left out of the plug at the plug panel.  The
          fault code is 124.
          In the message text:
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The command is rejected.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For information on library errors,
          consult Filenet OSAR Library Unit Product Description.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR3117I Channel to channel adapter unit-number OFFLINE.
          Explanation:  When processing a library / drive command, the I/O
          driver found that channel to channel (CTC) adapter unit-number was
          OFFLINE.
          In the message text:
          unit-number
                    The CTC adapter.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The I/O operation is stopped.
          Operator Response:  Vary channel to channel adapter unit-number
          ONLINE.
          System Programmer Response:  None.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR3120I Unable to obtain fault status for library libname.  Error
          recovery canceled.
          Explanation:  When status from a command for a library was obtained
          showing a fault or fatal error, the request fault status command
          failed, causing error recovery to abort.
          In the message text:
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The error is marked permanent and the library is
          not operational.  The system sends messages about the job to one of
          the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Determine if hardware or software
          error and notify the service representative.  Obtain the
          SYS1.LOGREC error record.  If the error recurs and the program is
          not in error, search problem reporting data bases for a fix for the
          problem.  If no fix exists, contact the IBM Support Center.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR3122I Volumes volser1 and volser2 were ejected from library libname,
          shelf location is shelfloc.
          Explanation:  A request has been made to eject an optical disk from
          a library.  The request can have been made by the OAM Storage
          Management Component, an operator command, or an ISMF user.  This
          request will cause an optical disk cartridge to be sent to the
          library's input/output station.
          In the message text:
          volser-1  The volume serial number.
          volser-2  The volume serial number.
          libname   The library name.
          shelfloc  The shelf location.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The records in the optical configuration database
          are updated to show these volumes returned to their shelf location.
          Operator Response:  Remove the optical disk from the library
          input/output station and return to a shelf location.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR3123I Eject of volumes volser1 and volser2 from library libname failed.
          Explanation:  A request has been made to eject an optical disk from
          a library.  The request can have been made by the OAM Storage
          Management Component, an operator command, or an ISMF user.  This
          request failed.
          In the message text:
          volser1   The volume serial number.
          volser2   The volume serial number.
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The optical disk can not be ejected from the
          library, depending on when the error occurred.  OAM will continue
          processing.
          Operator Response:  Do not attempt to repeat the eject until OAM
          has been stopped and restarted.
          System Programmer Response:  Notify the service representative.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR3124I Eject of volume volser on drive drive-name in library
          library-name pending.
          Explanation:  The operator has entered an optical disk into the I/O
          station of library library-name.  An error has occurred during
          volume entry scheduler processing for volume volser and because of
          a subsequent library or drive error, the volume on drive drive-name
          could not be ejected at this time.  The volume will be ejected on a
          subsequent mount, demount or vary online of this drive.
          In the message text:
          volser    The volume serial number.
          drive-name
                    The specified drive name.
          library-name
                    The specified library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The optical disk can not be ejected from the
          library, at the present time.  OAM will continue processing.
          Operator Response:  None.
          System Programmer Response:  None.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR3125I STORAGE failed obtaining storage for the TPUT macro.
          Explanation:  An ISMF line operator eject was scheduled but because
          of a STORAGE error, storage for the TPUT message buffer could not
          be obtained.  Notification of the success or failure of this eject
          cannot be sent to the Time Sharing Option (TSO) issuer.  This
          message is preceded by message CBR7004I, which contains the return
          code from the STORAGE error.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM processing continues.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Determine the cause of the STORAGE
          error by investigating the return code from the STORAGE macro and
          referring to the documentation for message CBR7004I.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR3130I Library adapter not responding for library libname.
          Explanation:  An I/O operation was issued to a library, but the
          library adapter returned a return code of X'02' indicating not
          responding.
c.cc 6
          In the message text:
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The library command is retried on the alternate
          port.  If it fails a second time, the library is marked not
          operational.  The system sends messages about the job to one of the
          following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For information on adapter errors,
          consult "Asynchronous Adapter Device Driver Table" in the MVS/DFP
          Version 3 Release 2: Diagnosis Reference manual.  Obtain the
          SYS1.LOGREC error record.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR3131I Library adapter function call unknown to library libname.
          Explanation:  An I/O operation was issued to a library, but the
          library adapter returned a return code of X'01', that indicates the
          function call was unknown or unsupported.
          In the message text:
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The library command is aborted.  The system sends
          messages about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For information on adapter errors,
          consult "Asynchronous Adapter Device Driver Table" in the MVS/DFP
          Version 3 Release 2: Diagnosis Reference manual.  Discover from
          SYS1.LOGREC what command was issued.  Obtain the SYS1.LOGREC error
          record.  If the error recurs and the program is not in error,
          search problem reporting data bases for a fix for the problem.  If
          no fix exists, contact the IBM Support Center.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR3132I Library adapter function call rejected.  No acknowledgement from
          library libname.
          Explanation:  An I/O operation was issued to a library, but the
          library adapter returned a return code of X'03', that indicates the
          library returned a "NACK" (no acknowledgement) to the function
          call.
          In the message text:
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The library command is retried from a different
          port.  If that does not work, the command is rejected.  The system
          sends messages about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For information on adapter errors,
          consult "Asynchronous Adapter Device Driver Table" in the MVS/DFP
          Version 3 Release 2: Diagnosis Reference manual.  Obtain the
          SYS1.LOGREC error record.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR3133I Library adapter function call rejected.  Library libname not
          responding.
          Explanation:  An I/O operation was issued to a library, but the
          library adapter returned a return code of X'04' that indicates the
          library is not responding to the function call.
          In the message text:
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The library command is retried from a different
          port.  If it fails there, the command is rejected and the library
          marked offline.  The system sends messages about the job to one of
          the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For information on adapter errors,
          consult "Asynchronous Adapter Device Driver Table" in the MVS/DFP
          Version 3 Release 2: Diagnosis Reference manual.  Obtain the
          SYS1.LOGREC error record.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR3134I Library libname communications not enabled.
          Explanation:  An I/O operation was issued to a library, but the
          library adapter returned a return code of X'05' that indicates that
          library communications were not enabled.
          In the message text:
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The library command is retried from a different
          port.  If it fails there, the library is marked unusable.  The
          system sends messages about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For information on adapter errors,
          consult "Asynchronous Adapter Device Driver Table" in the MVS/DFP
          Version 3 Release 2: Diagnosis Reference manual.  Obtain the
          SYS1.LOGREC error record.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR3135I Library adapter function call unknown error using library
          libname.
          Explanation:  An I/O operation was issued to a library, but the
          library adapter returned a non-supported return code, that
          indicates an unknown error occurred while processing a function
          call.
          In the message text:
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The library command is retried.  The system sends
          messages about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For information on adapter errors,
          consult "Asynchronous Adapter Device Driver Table" in the MVS/DFP
          Version 3 Release 2: Diagnosis Reference manual.  Obtain the
          SYS1.LOGREC error record.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR3136I Library adapter function call internal error using library
          libname.
          Explanation:  An I/O operation was issued to a library, but the
          library adapter returned a return code of X'08' that indicates no
          pending messages in the receive message buffer were found while
          processing a function call.
          In the message text:
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The library command is retried.  The system sends
          messages about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For information on adapter errors,
          consult "Asynchronous Adapter Device Driver Table" in the MVS/DFP
          Version 3 Release 2: Diagnosis Reference manual.  Obtain the
          SYS1.LOGREC error record.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR3137I Incomplete message sent from library libname.
          Explanation:  The library adapter has determined that a library has
          sent an incomplete message to the adapter and is now unable to
          continue.  This error is either a X'0A' or X'0C' from the library
          adapter.
          In the message text:
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The library is marked not operational and the
          command fails.  OAM can continue on other libraries and drives.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For information on adapter errors,
          consult "Asynchronous Adapter Device Driver Table" in the MVS/DFP
          Version 3 Release 2: Diagnosis Reference manual.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR3198I Offline or unknown status status from library libname.
          Explanation:  A library returned a status that is either unknown or
          says the service representative has issued a Listen command.
          In the message text:
          libname   The library name.
          status    The status returned by the library.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The library is marked not operational and the
          command fails.  OAM can continue on other libraries and drives.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR3199I Unsupported fault code for library libname.
          Explanation:  A fault occurred for a library that is not yet
          supported.  It is treated as a permanent error until supported.
          In the message text:
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The error is treated as permanent.  The system
          sends messages about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Keep the console information and notify the
          system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For information on library errors,
          consult Filenet OSAR Library Unit Product Description.  Obtain the
          SYS1.LOGREC error record.
   __________________________________________________________________________
 | CBR3200I A permanent error occurred in Library library-name, status
 |        status, fault code fff, failing command command.
 |        Explanation:  While processing a command command, in library
 |        library-name, fault code fff, status status occurred for which the
 |        ERP could not recover.  See the secondary error message for an
 |        explanation of the fault code.
 |        In the message text:
 |        library-name
 |                  The specified library name.
 |        status    The status.
 |        fff       The fault code.
 |        command   The command that failed.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
 |        System Action:  See the secondary error message system action.  The
 |        system sends messages about the job to one of the following:
 |        °   The primary console
 |        °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
 |        °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
 |            (MCS)
          Operator Response:  See the secondary error message operator
          action.
          System Programmer Response:  See the secondary error message
          programmer response.  Obtain the SYS1.LOGREC error record.  If the
          error recurs and the program is not in error, search problem
          reporting data bases for a fix for the problem.  If no fix exists,
          contact the IBM Support Center.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR3201I The I/O station in library libname is no longer usable.
          Explanation:  An I/O error has occurred processing a library
          command.  The library returned a fault 044 that indicates an input
          command was received but the I/O station does not contain a
          cartridge.
c.cc 6
          In the message text:
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The I/O station is marked non-operational, causing
          all subsequent entry and eject requests to fail until the library
          is VARYed offline and then back online.  The system sends messages
          about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For information on library errors,
          consult Filenet OSAR Library Unit Product Description.  Obtain the
          SYS1.LOGREC error record.  If the error recurs and the program is
          not in error, search problem reporting data bases for a fix for the
          problem.  If no fix exists, contact the IBM Support Center.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR3202I Invalid command command to library libname status status.
          Explanation:  An I/O error has occurred processing a library
          command.  The library returned status of E, that indicates it
          detected an incorrect command.  The cartridge is left in the
          gripper and can be stored or removed by varying the library off and
          then back online.
          In the message text:
          command   The library command.
          status    The status.
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system stops the I/O operation.  The system
          sends messages about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For information on library errors,
          consult Filenet OSAR Library Unit Product Description.  Obtain the
          SYS1.LOGREC error record.  If the error recurs and the program is
          not in error, search problem reporting data bases for a fix for the
          problem.  If no fix exists, contact the IBM Support Center.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR3203I Interrupt control circuitry fault on library libname.
          Explanation:  An I/O error has occurred processing a library
          command.  The library returned a fault 008 that indicates it
          detected a fault in the interrupt control circuitry.
          In the message text:
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system stops the I/O operation.  The system
          sends messages about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For information on library errors,
          consult Filenet OSAR Library Unit Product Description.  Obtain the
          SYS1.LOGREC error record.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR3204I Multiple timer interrupt fault in library libname.
          Explanation:  An I/O error has occurred processing a library
          command.  The library returned a fault 009 that indicates it
          received a second interrupt without finishing an earlier one on the
          same timer.
          In the message text:
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system stops the I/O operation.  The system
          sends messages about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For information on library errors,
          consult Filenet OSAR Library Unit Product Description.  Obtain the
          SYS1.LOGREC error record.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR3205I Gripper 1 rear limit sensor fault in library libname.
          Explanation:  An I/O error has occurred processing a library
          command.  The library returned a fault 021 that indicates gripper 1
          exceeded the maximum step count when single stepping from rear
          limit sensor after getting a cartridge.
          In the message text:
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system stops the I/O operation.
          The system sends messages about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.

          SYS1.LOGREC error record.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR3206I Gripper 2 rear limit sensor fault in library libname.
          Explanation:  An I/O error has occurred processing a library
          command.  The library returned a fault 022 that indicates gripper 2
          exceeded the maximum step count when single stepping from rear

   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR3207I Gripper front sensor fault in library libname.
          Explanation:  An I/O error has occurred processing a library

   CBR3212I Gripper undetermined fault in library libname.

   CBR3236I Horizontal limit failure in library libname.
          Explanation:  A fault occurred in a library that indicates that a
          failure occurred with a horizontal limit sensor.  This is an error
          that is reported only when a recovery procedure was incapable of
          continuing.
          In the message text:
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The request is rejected and the failing component
          is marked not operational.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  If the error recurs and the program is
          not in error, search problem reporting databases for a fix for the
          problem.  If no fix exists, contact the IBM Support Center.
          Provide the SYS1.LOGREC error record.

c.cc 8
          System Action:  The system stops the I/O operation.  The system
          sends messages about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For information on library errors,
          consult Filenet OSAR Library Unit Product Description.  Obtain the
          SYS1.LOGREC error record.
   __________________________________________________________________________

   CBR3255I Gripper 1 slot full sensor could not find the end of target in
