 | CBR3256I Cartridge jammed in library library-name between the gripper and
 |        drive drive-number.
 |        Explanation:  An I/O error has occurred while processing a library
 |        command.  Library library-name returned a fault 235 or 236
 |        indicates that during processing of a retract command or the
 |        retract portion of a retract and exchange command, the cartridge
 |        got jammed between the gripper and drive drive-number.
 |        In the message text:
 |        library-name
 |                  The specified library name.
 |        drive-number
 |                  The specified drive number.
 |        Source:  Object access method (OAM)
 |        System Action:  The I/O operation is stopped and the library is
 |        left in an unusable state until the cartridge is removed and the
 |        library is VARYed back online.  The system sends messages about the
 |        job to one of the following:
 |        °   The primary console
 |        °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
 |        °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
 |            (MCS)
 |        Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
 |        System Programmer Response:  For information on library errors,
 |        consult Filenet OSAR Library Unit Product Description.  Obtain the
 |        SYS1.LOGREC error record.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR3899I Protocol error of psc received from device controller trying to
          access library libname.
          Explanation:  The device controller has determined that the
          communications packet is in error.  The specific error can be
          referenced below by using the protocol status code (PSC) value:
          1     Packet ID is incorrect
          2     Length of packet out of range
          3     Command type not recognized
          4     SCSI bus ID out of range
          5     Logical unit number out of range
          6     Length of data out of range
          7     Tag value is out of sequence
          8     SCSI command not recognized
          9     Protocol error status received from host
          In the message text:
          psc       The protocol status code.
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  Depending upon the operation that was issued to the
          library, OAM can continue.  The system sends messages about the job
          to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Use the PSC in the explanation to
          determine the reason for the error.  Obtain the SYS1.LOGREC error
          record.  If the error recurs and the program is not in error,
          search problem reporting data bases for a fix for the problem.  If
          no fix exists, contact the IBM Support Center.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR3900A Close the input/output station door on library libname.
          Explanation:  The cartridge in the gripper is ready to be placed in
          the input/output station of a library, but the door is open.
          In the message text:
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system continues processing.
          Operator Response:  Close the input/output station door.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR3910I There is no online and operational library.
          Explanation:  During OAM initialization, none of the libraries came
          up online and operational, the last library online was varied
          offline, or the last operational library was marked not
          operational.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  No library requests will be honored until a library
          is both online and operational.
          Operator Response:  If a library is offline but operational, issue
          the SMS VARY command to vary the library online.  If a library is
          not operational and online, issue the SMS VARY command to vary the
          library offline and then online.  If a library is nonoperational
          and offline, issue the SMS VARY command to vary the library online.
          If the operational status does not change by varying the library on
          and offline, contact the service representative.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR4400A Mount volume volser on drive drvname.  Shelf location is
          shelfloc.
          Explanation:  An optical disk volume is to be mounted on an optical
          disk drive.  If the volume is labeled, message CBR4440A will
          precede this message that indicates its shelf location.
          °   If the volume serial number is 6 characters or less, then the
              optical disk volume with that volume serial number is to be
              mounted on the specified drive.
          °   If the volume serial number is '******', a nonlabeled disk
              volume is to be mounted on the specified drive. Both volumes on
              the optical disk cartridge must be nonlabeled.
          In the message text:
          volser    The volume serial number.
          drvname   The drive name.
          shelfloc  The shelf location.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system waits for the requested optical disk
          volume to be mounted.
          Operator Response:  Mount the requested optical disk volume on the
          specified drive and reply 'M'.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR4401I Volume volser mounted on drive drvename.
          Explanation:  An optical disk volume has been mounted on an optical
          disk drive.
          In the message text:
          volser    The volume serial number.
          drvename  The drive name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM accepts the volume.
   __________________________________________________________________________
 | CBR4402I Demount volume volser on drive drvename, shelf location is
 |        shelfloc.
 |        Explanation:  An optical disk volume on an optical disk drive is to
 |        be demounted and returned to a shelf location.  The volser will be
 |        '??????' for an unknown volser and '******' for an unlabeled
 |        volume.
 |        In the message text:
 |        volser    The volume serial number.
 |        drvename  The drive name.
 |        shelfloc  The shelf location.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM processing continues.
          Operator Response:  Demount the optical disk volume from the
          specified drive and return the volume to its shelf location.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR4403I Unlabeled volume on drive drvename.  Volume rejected.
          Explanation:  An unlabeled optical disk volume was mounted on an
          optical disk drive.
          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM processing continues.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR4404I Volume volser on drive drvename is rejected.
          Explanation:  During the object access method (OAM) initialization
          phase or during a vary on line, a volume was found on an optical
          disk drive for which the DB2 volume table did not have an entry to
          match its volume serial number.
          In the message text:
          volser    The volume serial number.
          drvename  The drive name.
          The volume is ejected from the library or a demount request follows
          this message.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM processing continues.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR4405D Enter VOLSER for volume on drive drvename.
          Explanation:  An unlabeled optical disk volume was mounted on an
          optical disk drive in response to a mount no label volume request.
          To write the volume label a volume serial number is required from
          the operator.
          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM processing waits for a response from the
          operator.
          Operator Response:  Enter a 1 to 6 character volume serial number
          to be given to the optical disk volume currently mounted on the
          drive.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR4406D Enter owner information for volume volser on drive drvename.
          Explanation:  An unlabeled optical disk volume was mounted on an
          optical disk drive in response to a mount or enter volume into
          library request.  To write the volume, label owner information is
          required from the operator.
          In the message text:
          volser    The volume serial number.
          drvename  The drive name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM processing waits for a response from the
          operator.
          Operator Response:  Enter up to 64 characters of owner information
          to be placed in the volume label of the optical disk volume
          currently mounted on the drive.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR4407I Volume serial number volser already exists.
          Explanation:  An unlabeled optical disk volume was mounted on an
          optical disk drive in response to a mount or enter volume into the
          library request. The operator replied to message CBR4405D or
          CBR4412D with a volume serial number that already exists in the DB2
          volume table, or was used to label the other volume in this
          cartridge.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM processing continues.
          Operator Response:  On certain conditions you can enter another
          volume serial number in response to message CBR4405D or CBR4412D.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR4408I Write protection set on drive drvename.
          Explanation:  Write protection is currently set on the drive, the
          volume, or both.  Object access method (OAM) expects to write on
          this volume.
          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM processing continues.
          Operator Response:  Expect further informational messages.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR4409A Change the write protect switch on drive drvename.  Reply 'U'
          when done.
          Explanation:  The write protect switch on the operator panel of the
          optical disk drive is set to a write protect status on the drive.
          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM processing waits for the reply.
          Operator Response:  Release the write protect switch on the
          operator panel of the optical disk drive.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR4410I Incorrect VOLSER volser1 found.  Expected VOLSER volser2.
          Explanation:  The optical disk volume that was recently mounted did
          not contain the expected volume serial number.
          In the message text:
          volser1   The incorrect volume serial number.
          volser2   The expected volume serial number.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM processing continues.  The optical disk volume
          is returned to the correct library cell location, is ejected from
          the library, or is demounted from the optical disk drive.
          Operator Response:  If the volume is mounted on a library drive,
          notify the system programmer.  If the volume is located on a
          standalone drive, remove this volume and insert the correct volume.
          If you are in the process of changing the write protection on the
          volume, reply to the forthcoming message CBR4413A.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR4411I Volume on drive drvename is rejected.  Reason code is rc.
          Explanation:  During the object access method (OAM) initialization
          phase, when a drive is varied online, or when entering a volume
          into the library, OAM was not able to process the volume found on
          drive drvename The volume will be ejected from the library or a
          demount request will follow.
          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive name.
          rc        The reason code is as follows:
                    1       Failure during read optical disk label.
                    2       Failure during optical device ready.
                    3       Failure during optical device start.
                    4       Failure during write optical disk label.
                    5       Failure during write protect check.
                    6       Failure during optical device stop.
                    7       Failure during the OAM system processing.
                    9       Failure during library cartridge flip.
                    10      Failure during optical device command.
                    11      Failure during verification of next available
                            volume table of contents (VTOC) or data block.
                    12      Failure during DB2 function.
                    13      Failure during GET VCB service.
                    14      A system-initiated eject was pending on this
                            drive.
          System Action:  OAM initialization processing continues.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Notify the service representative.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR4412D Enter VOLSER for volume on drive drvename in library libname.
          Explanation:  An unlabeled optical disk volume was mounted on an
          optical disk drive in response to a disk volume being physically
          entered into a library.  To write the volume label, a volume serial
          number is required from the operator.
          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive name.
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM processing waits for a response from the
          operator.
          Operator Response:  Enter a 1 to 6 character volume serial number
          to be given to the optical disk volume currently mounted on the
          drive.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR4413A Write protection set on volume volser located on drive drvename.
          Explanation:  The volume on the drive has the write protection tab
          set to the on position.  Object access method (OAM) expects to
          write on this volume.
          In the message text:
          volser    The volume serial number.
          drvename  The drive name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system will wait for the cartridge to be
          removed from the drive and then loaded into the drive.
          Operator Response:  Remove the cartridge from the drive and change
          the write protection tab to the off position.  Load the volume back
          into the drive.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR4415I Volume label written to volume on drive drvename.  Volume serial
          number is volser.
          Explanation:  A volume label was written to the optical disk volume
          mounted on the drive.
          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive name.
          volser    The volume serial number.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM processing continues.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR4416I Entered volume serial number volser is invalid.
          Explanation:  The volume serial number entered for message CBR4405D
          does not conform to MVS volume serial number conventions.
          In the message text:
          volser    The volume serial number.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM processing continues.
          Operator Response:  Reenter the volume serial number on forthcoming
          message CBR4405D.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR4417I The volume label located on drive drvename is invalid.
          Explanation:  The block containing the volume label on the drive
          does not contain the correct header information.
          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM processing continues.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR4418I Invalid label operation on drive drvename volume serial number
          volser.
          Explanation:  The disk mounted on the drive for a label volume for
          on-shelf status contained a volume serial number that is already in
          the DB2 volume table.  One volume label on this disk can have
          already been written prior to finding this condition.  If a label
          was written, the DB2 volume table was not updated with this volume
          serial number.
          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive name.
          volser    The volume serial number.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM processing continues.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR4419I Previously labeled volume volser was mounted on drive drvename.
          Explanation:  The volume mounted on the drive for a label volume
          for on-shelf status contained a previously written volume serial
          number.
          In the message text:
          volser    The volume serial number.
          drvename  The drive name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  Processing for this volume will continue.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR4420I Volume table did not contain information for volume volser on
          drive drvename.
          Explanation:  While entering the volume onto the drive, object
          access method (OAM) could not locate information in the DB2 volume
          table for this volume.
          In the message text:
          volser    The volume serial number.
          drvename  The drive name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  An entry to the DB2 volume table will be created.
          A DB2 entry for this volume was added to the volume table if
          message CBR4401I was issued after this message.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR4421D Ready pending for drive drvename.  Reply 'R' to retry or 'C' to
          cancel.
          Explanation:  An object access method (OAM) drive ready pending
          time limit has been exceeded.  The start command to the drive has
          been issued but for some reason the drive failed to become ready.
          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM processing waits for a response from the
          operator.
          Operator Response:  Reply C if you wish to cancel this ready
          request.  OAM processing continues and the application requesting
          this mount is informed.
          If OAM should continue the ready request for this volume, reply 'R'
          to this message.  If you reply 'R' to this message, OAM will
          continue to test the drive for the ready condition.  Should this
          message repeat, it can indicate a hardware failure.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR4422D The disk mounted on drive drvename was not flipped.  Reply 'R' to
          retry or 'C' to cancel request.
          Explanation:  The disk mounted on the drive was not flipped as
          requested by message CBR4430A.  If the disk was correctly flipped
          then both volumes in this cartridge contain the same volume serial
          number.
          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  Processing for this label request will depend on
          the reply to this message.  If the reply is R, processing will
          continue.  If the reply is C, processing for this request will
          stop.
          Operator Response:  Reply R to allow access to the cartridge.
          Remove the cartridge from the drive and reinsert the correct
          volume.  Reply C is you wish to cancel this label request.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR4423D Enter shelf information for volume volser on drive drvename.
          Explanation:  An optical disk volume was mounted on an optical disk
          drive in response to a mount no-label volume request.  In order to
          create the volume table entry, shelf information is required from
          the operator.
          In the message text:
          volser    The volume serial number.
          drvename  The drive name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM processing waits for a response from the
          operator.
          Operator Response:  Enter up to 32 characters of shelf information
          to be placed in the volume table entry for the optical disk volume
          currently mounted on drive drive-name.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR4425D Removal of cartridge on drive drvename is pending.  Reply 'R' to
          retry or 'C' to cancel this request.
          Explanation:  An object access method (OAM) cartridge removal
          pending time limit has been exceeded.  OAM has requested a removal
          of a cartridge from a drive and has not been able to detect this
          removal.
          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM processing waits for a response from the
          operator.
          Operator Response:   If OAM should cancel this request, reply 'C'
          to this message.  OAM processing continues and the application
          requesting this mount is informed.
          If OAM should continue the removal request for this volume, reply
          'R' to this message.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR4426D Mount pending for volume volser on drive drvename.  Reply 'R' to
          retry or 'C' to cancel this request.
          Explanation:  An object access method (OAM) cartridge insertion
          pending time limit has been exceeded.  OAM has requested a
          cartridge load into a drive and has not been able to detect this
          load.
          In the message text:
          volser    The volume serial number.
          drvename  The drive name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM processing waits for a response from the
          operator.
          Operator Response:   If OAM should cancel this request, reply 'C'
          to this message.  OAM processing continues and the application
          requesting this mount is informed.
          If OAM should continue the load request for this volume, reply 'R'
          to this message.
          Review message CBR4400A, CBR4413A or CBR4430A for the mount
          request.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR4427I Volume volser which was entered into library libname1 with a DB2
          library name of libname2 was ejected.
          Explanation:  A labeled optical disk volume was entered into a
          library.  The library name in the DB2 volume table did not match
          the library name into which the volume was placed.
c.cc 6
          In the message text:
          volser    The volume serial number.
          libname1  The library name into which the volume serial number was
                    entered.
          libname2  The library name of the DB2.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM processing continues.
          Operator Response:  If this volume is to be entered into this
          library, the DB2 database must be changed to reflect the new
          library name prior to entering this volume into the library.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR4428I Volume volser which was entered into library libname can have an
          incorrect volume table entry and was ejected.
          Explanation:  A labeled optical disk volume was entered into a
          library.  The DB2 volume table entry states that this volume serial
          number is already contained in a library.  This volume can be a
          duplicate volser to a volume already in a library, or the
          associated DB2 volume table entry or slot table can be incorrect.
          In the message text:
          volser    The volume serial number.
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM processing continues.
          Operator Response:  If this volume is to be entered into this
          library, the DB2 volume table and or the slot table must be changed
          to reflect the correct status of the volume location.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR4429I Volume volser ejected from library libname. A mount is currently
          pending on drive drvename for volume volser.
          Explanation:  A labeled optical disk volume was entered into a
          library.  A mount request for this volume exists on the drive.
          In the message text:
          volser    The volume serial number.
          libname   The library name.
          drvename  The drive name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM processing continues.
          Operator Response:  Remove the cartridge from the library I/O
          station and mount the volume on the drive.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR4430A Remove and flip cartridge on drive drvename.
          Explanation:  The first volume label on a label for on-shelf status
          has been completed.  Remove the cartridge from the drive, flip this
          cartridge so that the other volume is up, and reinsert the
          cartridge into the drive.  Ready this drive.
          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM processing waits for the device to ready.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5504A Depress the start switch on drvename.  Reply 'U' when done or 'C'
          to cancel this drive initialization.
          Explanation:  An error has occurred while establishing the initial
          communications to an optical disk drive.  The start/stop switch on
          this optical disk drive must be in the start position prior to
          initializing this drive.
          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization phase will continue if the
          switch is changed.
          Operator Response:  Change the start switch position and reply to
          the message.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5508I Drive drvename in library libname is write protected.  Usage is
          read only.
          Explanation:  The write protection switch is currently set to the
          write protection position on the specified drive.  Until this
          drive's write protection status is reset, this drive will be used
          only for read requests.
          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive name.
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM processing continues.
          Operator Response:  Reset drive status by changing the switch,
          varying the drive offline and then varying the drive back online.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5509I Drive drvename is write protected.  Usage is read only.
          Explanation:  The write protection switch is currently set to the
          write protection position on the specified drive.  Until this
          drive's write protection status is reset, this drive will be used
          only for read requests.
          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM processing continues.
          Operator Response:  Reset drive status by changing the switch,
          varying the drive offline and then varying the drive back online.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5701I Device Controller initialization started.
          Explanation:  The device controller has begun initialization.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system continues processing.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5702I Device Controller initialization completed successfully.
          Explanation:  The device controller initialization completed
          successfully.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system continues processing.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5703A Device Controller initialization failed.
          Explanation:  The device controller initialization did NOT complete
          successfully.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system continues processing, but some functions
          can be inoperative.
          System Programmer Response:  Verify the error and notify the
          service representative.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5704I To restart, perform power on reset.
          Explanation:  To restart, power the device controller off, wait for
          10 seconds, and then power it back on.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system continues processing, but some functions
          can be inoperative.
          Operator Response:  Perform the power on reset.  This is not
          necessarily required.  See other messages to determine proper
          action.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5705A SCSI device driver not installed.
          Explanation:  An unsuccessful attempt was made to install the SCSI
          device driver.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system continues processing, but some optical
          drives will be inoperative.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.  Perform the
          power on reset.  This is not necessarily required.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5706I The SCSI device driver version is: version.  revision.
          Explanation:  The SCSI device driver for the specified version is
          installed.
          In the message text:
          version   The version installed.
          revision  The revision of the version.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system continues processing.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5707I xxxx xxxx xxxx
          Explanation:  These are messages that are triggered by a message
          level greater than 0.  They are intended for diagnostic purposes
          only.
          In the message text:
          xxxx xxxx xxxx
                    Diagnostic text.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system continues processing.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5708A Library device driver not installed.
          Explanation:  An unsuccessful attempt was made to install the
          library device driver.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system continues processing.  An alternate path
          to the library can be used.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.  Perform the
          power on reset.  This is not necessarily required.
          System Programmer Response:  Notify the service representative.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5709I The library device driver version is:  version.revision.
          Explanation:  The library device driver for the specified version
          revision is installed.
          In the message text:
          version   The version installed.
          revision  The revision of the version.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system continues processing.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5710I The SCSI device driver failed with the following function call
          return code: rc.
          Explanation:  The SCSI device driver failed.
          In the message text:
          rc        The return code.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system continues processing, but some functions
          can be inoperative.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Notify the service representative.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5711I The library device driver failed with the following function call
          return code: rc.
          Explanation:  The library device driver failed.
          In the message text:
          rc        The return code.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system continues processing, but some functions
          can be inoperative.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Notify the service representative.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5712I Message level set to nnn.
          Explanation:  Indicates the current message level selected.  See
          message number CBR5714I.
          In the message text:
          nnn       Indicates the current message level.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system continues processing.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5714I Help information:
          Active keys:
            F1 = Help
            F10 = Quit - WARNING: This key is for diagnostic
                                  purposes only.
                                  NEVER use during normal
                                  operation.
            n = Where n = 0-9 to set message level.
                0 = important messages only
                1 = display selective and system resets
                2 = data packet entry and exit times
                3 = dump packet headers
                4 = module trace messages
                5 = diagnostic messages
                7 = dump internal data
                9 = display all messages
                Current message level is: nnn.
          Explanation:  This is the help information.  It indicates the
          active options.
c.cc 6
          In the message text:
          nnn       Indicates the current message level.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system continues processing.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5715A Correct usage is: cbrtcont Message_level(0-9)
          Control_unit_block(0-F)
          Explanation:  The device controller program (cbrtcont) was invoked
          with incorrect parameters.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The device controller will halt.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Change the invocation of cbrtcont in
          AUTOEXEC.BAT to use valid parameters.  Notify the service
          representative.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5716I The Control_unit_block is set to: xxx.
          Explanation:  The device controller PCCA card is set to respond to
          the specified address on the channel.
          In the message text:
          xxx       The current setting of the control_unit_block.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system continues processing.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5717I Packet from host has the following protocol error: code {Packet
          id is incorrect.  Packet is ignored. |
            Length of packet out of range. |
            Device type not recognized. Packet is ignored.|
            SCSI bus ID out of range. |
            SCSI LUN out of range. |
            Length of data out of range. |
            Library port number out of range. |
            Protocol error received from host. }
          Explanation:  A packet was received from the host.  The packet
          cannot be processed because it is in error.
          In the message text:
          code      The code of the protocol error.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system continues processing, but some functions
          can be inoperative.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Notify the service representative.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5718I Unsupported Function key
          Explanation:  A function key that is not supported was pressed.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system continues processing.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5719D Termination requested.  Are you sure you want to terminate the
          Device Controller? (y,n) ==>
          Explanation:  Function key 10 was pressed.  This requests the
          device controller to stop.  This should never be done except by
          service personnel.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system continues processing or the device
          controller program stops.
          Operator Response:  Enter 'n' unless you really want to stop the
          device controller.  If so, enter a 'y'.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5720I Device Controller terminated.
          Explanation:  The device controller program was stopped.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The device controller program stops.
          Operator Response:  See other messages to determine action.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5721I Unexpected channel status: status.
          Explanation:  The device controller PCCA card detected a channel
          status that was unexpected.
          In the message text:
          status    The channel status.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system continues processing.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Notify the service representative.
   __________________________________________________________________________
 | CBR5722I Channel speed set to channel-speed.
 |        Explanation:  The 8232 control units set the channel data transfer
 |        rate, depending on the third parameter of the invocation of
 |        CBRTCONT in the AUTOEXEC.BAT file.  The following values are valid:
 |        °   0 - DC Interlock
 |        °   1 - 1 Mb/sec Data streaming
 |        °   3 - 3 Mb/sec Data streaming (default)
 |        In the message text:
 |        channel-speed
 |                  The channel data transfer rate.
 |        Source:  Object access method (OAM)
 |        System Action:  Processing continues.
 |        Operator Response:  None.
 |        System Programmer Response:  None.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5800I I/O error on optical drive drvename, vvvv, ww, xx, yy,
          zzzzzzzzzz.
          Explanation:  An I/O error has occurred on the specified drive.
          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive name.
          vvvv      The drive controller protocol status.
          ww        The SCSI adapter function call return code.
          xx        The SCSI adapter completion code.
          yy        The SCSI drive status code.
          zzzzzzzzzz
                    The sense data returned from the drive.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system stops the I/O operation.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For information on SCSI adapter codes,
          consult SCSI adapter completion code table in the DFP Diagnosis
          Reference For information on drive status code and sense data,
          consult LASERDRIVE 1200 Enhanced Digital Optical Disk Drive with
          Small Computer System Interface Product Specification.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5801I SCSI status byte ww {check condition | busy | reservation
          conflict} on drive drvename.
          Explanation:  An I/O error has occurred on an optical disk drive.
          The SCSI status byte for the last I/O operation indicates either
          CHECK CONDITION or BUSY or RESERVATION CONFLICT was sent by the
          target to the initiator.
          In the message text:
          ww        The SCSI status byte.
          drvename  The drive name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system stops the I/O operation.  The system
          sends messages about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For information on SCSI status drive
          byte, consult LASERDRIVE 1200 Enhanced Digital Optical Disk Drive
          with Small Computer System Interface Product Specification.  Obtain
          the SYS1.LOGREC error record.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5802A Start drive drvename.  Reply 'U' when complete, or 'C' to cancel.
          Explanation:  A drive was found to be in the stopped state when an
          I/O operation was tried.
          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The task will wait until the drive is started and
          the operator replies.  If the reply is 'C' the drive will be set to
          the non-operational status.
          Operator Response:  Press the start button on the drive and reply
          'U' when complete.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5808I Adapter command tag already in use on drive drvename.
          Explanation:  The adapter command tag was in use when the prior
          command was issued for the drive.  The SCSI adapter returned a
          completion code of X'21', indicating the prior command with this
          tag did not complete.
          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system stops the I/O operation.  The system
          sends messages about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Notify the service representative.
          System Programmer Response:  For information on host adapter error
          codes, consult SCSI adapter completion code table in the DFP
          Diagnosis Reference.  Obtain the SYS1.LOGREC error record.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5809I Failing SCSI command: scsibyte.
          Explanation:  The SCSI command bytes for the failing I/O operation
          are displayed in hex.
          In the message text:
          scsibyte  The SCSI bytes displayed.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system stops the I/O operation.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For information on SCSI adapter codes,
          consult LASERDRIVE 1200 Enhanced Digital Optical Disk Drive with
          Small Computer System Interface Product Specification.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5810I Invalid command from controller to SCSI adapter addressing drive
          drvename.
          Explanation:  An I/O error has occurred on an optical disk drive.
          The SCSI adapter returned an error code of 01, indicating incorrect
          command from the drive controller.
          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system stops the I/O operation.  The system
          sends messages about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For information on host adapter error
          codes, consult SCSI adapter device driver table in the DFP
          Diagnosis Reference.  Obtain the SYS1.LOGREC error record.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5811I SCSI CDB byte count error addressing drive drvename.
          Explanation:  An I/O error has occurred on an optical disk drive.
          The SCSI adapter returned a completion code of X'24', indicating a
          SCSI Command Descriptor Block byte count error.  The number of
          bytes is other than 6, 10 or 12.
          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system stops the I/O operation.  The system
          sends messages about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Notify the service representative.
          System Programmer Response:  For information on SCSI adapter error
          codes, consult SCSI adapter completion code table in the DFP
          Diagnosis Reference.  Obtain the SYS1.LOGREC error record.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5812I Invalid SCSI ID error addressing drive drvename.
          Explanation:  An I/O error has occurred on an optical disk drive.
          The SCSI adapter returned a completion code of X'25', indicating an
          incorrect SCSI ID. The SCSI ID must be between 0 and 6.
c.cc 6
          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system stops the I/O operation.  The system
          sends messages about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Notify the service representative.
          System Programmer Response:  For information on SCSI adapter error
          codes, consult SCSI adapter completion code table in the DFP
          Diagnosis Reference.  Obtain the SYS1.LOGREC error record.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5813I SCSI adapter timeout waiting for command completion from drive
          drvename.
          Explanation:  An I/O error has occurred on an optical disk drive.
          The host adapter returned a return code of X'08', or a completion
          code of X'84', indicating a timeout error waiting for a command to
          complete.
          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system stops the I/O operation.  The system
          sends messages about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For information on host adapter error
          codes, consult SCSI adapter device driver table in the DFP
          Diagnosis Reference.  Obtain the SYS1.LOGREC error record.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5814I Parity error on SCSI bus to or from drive drvename.
          Explanation:  An I/O error has occurred on an optical disk drive.
          The SCSI adapter returned a completion code of X'44', indicating a
          parity error on the SCSI bus.
          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system stops the I/O operation.  The system
          sends messages about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For information on SCSI adapter error
          codes, consult SCSI adapter completion code table in the DFP
          Diagnosis Reference.  Obtain the SYS1.LOGREC error record.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5815I Invalid data pointer between device controller and SCSI adapter
          addressing drive drvename.
          Explanation:  An I/O error has occurred on an optical disk drive.
          The SCSI adapter returned a return code of X'03', or a completion
          code of X'41', indicating an incorrect data pointer, or a
          completion code of X'42', indicating a pointer conflict.
          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system retires the I/O operation.  The system
          sends messages about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For information on SCSI adapter error
          codes, consult SCSI adapter device driver table in the DFP
          Diagnosis Reference.  Obtain the SYS1.LOGREC error record.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5817I SCSI bus reset occurred addressing drive drvename.
          Explanation:  An I/O error has occurred on an optical disk drive.
          The SCSI adapter returned a completion code of X'83', indicating a
          reset has occurred on the SCSI bus.
          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system stops the I/O operation.  The system
          sends messages about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For information on SCSI adapter error
          codes, consult SCSI adapter completion code table in the DFP
          Diagnosis Reference.  Obtain the SYS1.LOGREC error record.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5818I SCSI adapter unknown or internal error addressing drive drvename.
          Explanation:  An I/O error has occurred on an optical disk drive.
          The SCSI adapter returned a return code of X'06' or X'07',
          indicating an unknown error or an internal error occurred.
          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system stops the I/O operation.  The system
          sends messages about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For information on host adapter error
          codes, consult SCSI adapter device driver table in the DFP
          Diagnosis Reference.  Obtain the SYS1.LOGREC error record.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5819I SCSI adapter error.  Data boundary crossing using drive drvename.
          Explanation:  An I/O error has occurred on an optical disk drive.
          The SCSI adapter returned a return code of X'0A', indicating that a
          memory segment boundary would be crossed during data transfer.
          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system stops the I/O operation.  The system
          sends messages about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For information on host adapter error
          codes, consult SCSI adapter device driver table in the DFP
          Diagnosis Reference.  Obtain the SYS1.LOGREC error record.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5820I SCSI adapter unsuccessful in selecting drive drvename.
          Explanation:  An I/O error has occurred on an optical disk drive.
          The SCSI adapter returned a completion code of X'47', indicating
          that the target device (optical disk drive) failed to respond
          during the selection phase.
          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system stops the I/O operation.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For information on SCSI adapter error
          codes, consult SCSI adapter completion code table in the DFP
          Diagnosis Reference.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5821I Parity error on data transfer to/from the adapter buffer using
          drive drvename.
          Explanation:  An I/O error has occurred on an optical disk drive.
          The SCSI adapter returned a completion code of X'43', indicating
          that there was a parity error on a data transfer to/from the
          adapter data buffer.
          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system retries the I/O operation.  The system
          sends messages about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.

   CBR5822I Invalid SCSI bus ID.  The drive drvename does not exist.
          Explanation:  An I/O error has occurred on an optical disk drive.
          The SCSI adapter returned a return code of X'02', indicating that
          the SCSI BUS ID is not recognized as being attached or online.
          There is a probable error in the drive table in the database for
          object access method (OAM).

          drvename  The drive name.

          sends messages about the job to one of the following:

          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support

   CBR5824I Unexpected disconnect from SCSI bus using drive drvename.
          Explanation:  An I/O error has occurred on an optical disk drive.

   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5825I SCSI adapter detected differential sense fault using drive

   CBR5827I Error aabbcc occurred requesting sense from drive drvename.
          Explanation:  A check condition occurred on a disk drive.  Another
          error occurred when the device controller issued the Request Sense
          command.
          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive name.
          aabbcc    As follows:

          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system stops the I/O operation.  The system
          sends messages about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles

   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5850I Laser read power fault on drive drvename.
          Explanation:  An I/O error has occurred on an optical disk drive.
          The drive returned a fault code of X'01', indicating a laser read
          power error that could not be recovered from by the LD 1200.  This
          out-of-tolerance condition indicates the laser is nearing the end
          of its useful life, or that there is a malfunction in the laser
          power circuitry.
          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system successfully completes the I/O
          operation.  The system sends messages about the job to one of the
          following:

          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support

          code is reported in conjunction with ILLEGAL REQUEST when a reserve
          command with the extent option is rejected because the LD 1200s
          reserved extents table is full.
          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system stops the I/O operation.  The system
          sends messages about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Obtain the SYS1.LOGREC error record.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5875I SCSI I/O parity error using drive drvename.
          Explanation:  An I/O error has occurred on an optical disk drive.
          The optical disk drive returned a fault code of X'41', indicating

   CBR5882I Laser degraded on drive drvename.
          Explanation:  An I/O error has occurred on an optical disk drive.

          drvename  The drive name.

   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5883I Skip count overflow on drive drvename.
          Explanation:  An I/O error has occurred on an optical disk drive.
          The optical disk drive returned a fault code of X'6C', indicating
          that the skip count field of the sense data block has overflowed
          during the course of a read or write operation.
          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system stops the I/O operation.  The system

   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5884I ARA initialization failed: mode unavailable for volume volser.
          Explanation:  An I/O error has occurred on an optical disk drive.
          The drive returned a fault code of X'91'.  This fault code is
          reported as a result of any read or write in Auto ReAllocate (ARA)
          mode after the cartridge initialization sequence failed to
          determine the ARA orphan table or spares area state for the
          specified volume.  Since object access method (OAM) is not using
          ARA mode, this is likely to be a software error.
          In the message text:
          volser    The volume serial number.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system stops the I/O operation.  The system
          sends messages about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Obtain the SYS1.LOGREC error record.
          If the error recurs and the program is not in error, search problem
          reporting data bases for a fix for the problem.  If no fix exists,
          contact the IBM Support Center.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5885I ARA cartridge initialization failure: orphan phase on volume
          volser.
          Explanation:  An I/O error has occurred on an optical disk drive.
          The optical disk drive returned a fault code of X'92'.  This fault
          code is reported as a result of the Auto ReAllocate (ARA) cartridge
          initialization sequence failure to find a valid copy of the orphan
          table on the specified volume.  Since object access method (OAM) is
          not using ARA mode, this is likely a software error.
          In the message text:
          volser    The volume serial number.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system stops the I/O operation.  The system
          sends messages about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Obtain the SYS1.LOGREC error record.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5886I ARA cartridge initialization failure: spares phase on volume
          volser.
          Explanation:  An I/O error has occurred on an optical disk drive.
          The optical disk drive returned a fault code of X'93', indicating
          that the Auto ReAllocate (ARA) cartridge initialization sequence
          failed to find the beginning of the available spares area on the
          specified volume.  Since object access method (OAM) is not using
          ARA mode, this is likely a software error.
          In the message text:
          volser    The volume serial number.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system stops the I/O operation.  The system

          reporting data bases for a fix for the problem.  If no fix exists,
          contact the IBM Support Center.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5887I Power-up diagnostics aborted on drive drvename.
          Explanation:  An I/O error has occurred on an optical disk drive.
          The optical disk drive returned a fault code of X'A5'.  This fault
          code is reported in conjunction with a UNIT ATTENTION when the
          power-up diagnostics were not completed because the LD 1200
          responded to a selection.  In order for the LD 1200 to complete the
          self-test diagnostics, no host should select the LD 1200 for the
          first 3 minutes after power-up.
          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system stops the I/O operation.  The system
          sends messages about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Obtain the SYS1.LOGREC error record.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5888I Diagnostics fault detected on drive drvename.
          Explanation:  An I/O error has occurred on an optical disk drive.
          The optical disk drive returned a fault code of X'A6', indicating
          that a fault occurred during a diagnostic test.
          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system stops the I/O operation.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5889I Diagnostic data not available for drive drvename.
          Explanation:  An I/O error has occurred on an optical disk drive.
          The optical disk drive returned a fault code of X'A7'.  This fault
          code is reported in response to a Receive Diagnostic Results
          command when no valid data is available to return.
          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system stops the I/O operation.  The system
          sends messages about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console

              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Obtain the SYS1.LOGREC error record.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5890I Illegal sequence (drive not ready) for drive drvename.
          Explanation:  An I/O error has occurred on an optical disk drive.
          The optical disk drive returned a fault code of X'C1', indicating
          that a drive fault has occurred that has not been cleared by the

          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system stops the I/O operation.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5891I Write protected drive error on drive drvename.
          Explanation:  An I/O error has occurred on an optical disk drive.
          The optical disk drive returned a fault code of X'C2', indicating
          the host has attempted a write operation to an LD 1200 that is

          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system stops the I/O operation.  The system

              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Obtain the SYS1.LOGREC error record.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5892I Unable to write with special postfield failure on drive drvename.
          Explanation:  An I/O error has occurred on an optical disk drive.
          The optical disk drive returned a fault code of X'C4'.  This fault

          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system stops the I/O operation.  The system
          sends messages about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console

   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5893I Media error or data field overwrite on drive drvename.
          Explanation:  An I/O error has occurred on an optical disk drive.
          The optical disk drive returned a fault code of X'C6', indicating a
          write operation was attempted at a sector that was previously
          written.  This error can be a result of a media error in the
          control bytes of the record.
          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system stops the I/O operation.  The system
          sends messages about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support

          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Obtain the SYS1.LOGREC error record.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5894I Empty sector detected on drive drvename.
          Explanation:  An I/O error has occurred on an optical disk drive.
          The optical disk drive returned a fault code of X'E5', indicating a

          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system stops the I/O operation.  The system

          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support

          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Obtain the SYS1.LOGREC error record.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5895I Drive error on drive drvename.
          Explanation:  An I/O error has occurred on an optical disk drive.
          The optical disk drive returned a fault code of X'E6', indicating
          that the LD 1200 has detected a device error.
          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system stops the I/O operation.  The system
          sends messages about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support

          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Obtain the SYS1.LOGREC error record.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5896I Unsolicited interrupt on drive drvename.
          Explanation:  An I/O error has occurred on an optical disk drive.
          The optical disk drive returned a fault code of X'15', indicating
          that an unsolicited interrupt occurred during the processing of a
          command.
          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system stops the I/O operation.  The system
          sends messages about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Obtain the SYS1.LOGREC error record.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5897I Timeout occurred during spin up/down on drive drvename.
          Explanation:  A timeout has occurred on an optical disk drive.  The
          optical disk drive returned a fault code of X'87', indicating that
          a timeout has occurred on the spin up or spin down command.
          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system stops the I/O operation.  The system
          sends messages about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Obtain the SYS1.LOGREC error record.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR5899I Protocol error of psc received from device controller trying to
          access drive drvename.
          Explanation:  The device controller has determined that the
          communications packet, CBRPAC, was in error.
          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive name.
          psc       The protocol status code is as follows
                    1     Packet ID is incorrect
                    2     Length of packet out of range
                    3     Command type not recognized
                    4     SCSI bus ID out of range
                    5     Logical unit number out of range
                    6     Length of data out of range
 |                  7     Libary number is out of range
 |                  8     Protocol error status
 |                  9     Checksum error
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  Depending upon the operation that was issued to the
          optical disk drive, OAM can continue.  The system sends messages
          about the job to one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Using the protocol status code above,
          determine the reason for the error.  Obtain the SYS1.LOGREC error
          record.  If the error recurs and the program is not in error,
          search problem reporting data bases for a fix for the problem.  If
          no fix exists, contact the IBM Support Center.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR6000I Error attaching drive task for drive drvename.
          Explanation:  An error was detected while trying to create a task
          to manage the optical drive.
          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM marks the optical drive not operational.  No
          work can be scheduled to, or performed on, the optical drive until
          the OAM address space has been shut down and restarted.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  This message is preceded by message
          CBR7000I, which gives additional information about the cause of the
          error.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR6001I Unexpected drive task termination for drive drvename.
          Explanation:  The task which manages the optical drive has failed
          or ended prematurely.
          In the message text:
          drvename  The drive name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  If OAM initialization has completed, OAM creates a
          new drive task to manage the optical drive.  If a unit of work was
          active on the drive when the task failed, the unit of work is
          canceled.  If OAM initialization has not yet completed, no attempt
          is made to create a new drive task.  The optical drive is marked
          not operational and can not be used until OAM has been abnormally
          ended and restarted.  The system sends messages about the job to
          one of the following:
          °   The primary console
          °   The remote console, for a system with remote consoles
          °   The hard-copy log for a system with multiple console support
              (MCS)
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Notify the service representative.  If
          the error recurs and the program is not in error, search problem
          reporting data bases for a fix for the problem.  If no fix exists,
