          contact the IBM Support Center.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR6100I Cross-memory copy error between OAM address space and ASID asid.
          Explanation:  A user has requested the writing of a data object to
          an optical volume or the reading of a data object from an optical
          volume.  An error occurred during the attempt to copy either data
          or control information cross-memory between the user address space
          and the OAM address space.
          In the message text:
          asid      The address space identifier.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM cancels the user request.  Request completion
          is not signaled to the user address space because the likely result
          is another cross-memory failure.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  This is a probable user error.  This
          error can follow the premature abnormal end of the user address
          space, the premature abnormal end of the task in the user address
          space which requested OAM services, or the premature release of the
          storage containing the buffer from which the data object is to be
          written or into which the data object is to be read.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR6200I Error writing optical VTOC block for object objname, address lba,
          volume volser, drive drvename.
          Explanation:  A user has requested that a data object objname be
          written to optical volume volser on optical drive drvename.  Object
          access method (OAM) encountered an error during the attempt to
          write the optical volume table of contents (VTOC) to record the
          location of the data object.
          In the message text:
          objname   The object name.
          lba       The approximate physical block address that could not be
                    written.
          volser    The volume serial number.
          drvename  The drive name.
          System Action:  OAM will attempt to take the appropriate action to
          complete the write request.  If the write request cannot be
          successfully completed, OAM fails the user's write request.
          Operator Response:  This message is preceded by a hardware-related
          error message of the form CBR5nnnI or CBR3110I.  Follow the
          instructions given in the description of that message.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR6201I Error writing data block for object objname:  address lba, volume
          volser, drive drvename.
          Explanation:  A user has requested the writing of a data object to
          an optical volume on the specified drive.  Object access method
          (OAM) encountered an error during the attempt to write the data
          object.
          In the message text:
          objname   The object name.
          lba       The approximate physical block address that could not be
                    written.
          volser    The volume serial number.
          drvename  The drive name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM will attempt to take the appropriate action to
          complete the write request.  If the write request cannot be
          successfully completed, OAM fails the user's write request.
          Operator Response:  This message is preceded by a hardware-related
          error message of the form CBR5nnnI.  Follow the instructions given
          in the description of that message.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR6300I Error reading optical VTOC block: address lba, volume volser,
          drive drvename.
          Explanation:  A user has requested the reading of a data object
          from an optical volume on the specified drive.  Object access
          method (OAM) encountered an error during the attempt to read the
          optical volume table of contents (VTOC) to find the location of the
          data object.
          In the message text:
          lba       The logical block address that could not be read.
          volser    The volume serial number.
          drvename  The drive name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  If the failure results from a recording medium
          error, OAM fails the user read request.  If the failure is the
          result of a drive error, OAM attempts to select another drive on
          which to process the user read request.
          Operator Response:  This message is preceded by a hardware-related
          error message of the form CBR5nnnI.  Follow the instructions given
          in the description of that message.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR6301I Error reading data block: address lba, volume volser, drive
          drvename.
          Explanation:  A user has requested the reading of a data object
          from an optical volume on the specified drive.  Object access
          method (OAM) encountered an error during the attempt to read the
          data object.
          In the message text:
          lba       The logical block address that could not be read.
          volser    The volume serial number.
          drvename  The drive name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  If the failure results from a recording medium
          error, OAM fails the user read request.  If the failure is the
          result of a drive error, OAM attempts to select another drive on
          which to process the user read request.
          Operator Response:  This message is preceded by a hardware-related
          error message of the form CBR5nnnI.  Follow the instructions given
          in the description of that message.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR6310I Invalid optical VTOC format at block address lba on volume
          volser.
          Explanation:  A user has requested the reading of a data object
          from an optical volume.  While trying to locate the object on the
          volume, object access method (OAM) has detected an incorrect format
          in one of the blocks which belong to the optical volume table of
          contents (VTOC).
          In the message text:
          lba       The logical block address that could not be read.
          volser    The volume serial number.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM skips the incorrect block and continues the
          search for the optical VTOC entry for the object.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR7000I ATTACH error in module modname at label labname, RC = rc.
          Explanation:  An error occurred during processing of an ATTACH
          macro.
          In the message text:
          modname   The module name in which the ATTACH was issued.
          labname   The label name at which the ATTACH was issued.
          rc        The return code.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For additional information on return
          codes, see the ATTACH macro.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR7001I DETACH error in module modname at label labname, RC = rc.
          Explanation:  An error occurred during processing of a DETACH
          macro.
          In the message text:
          modname   The module name in which the DETACH was issued.
          labname   The label name at which the DETACH was issued.
          rc        The return code.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM completion processing continues.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For additional information on return
          codes, see the DETACH macro.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR7004I STORAGE OBTAIN error in module modname at label labname, RC = rc,
          SUBPOOL = subpool, AMOUNT = amount.
          Explanation:  An error occurred during processing of the STORAGE
          macro.
          In the message text:
          modname   The module name in which the STORAGE was issued.
          labname   The label name at which the STORAGE was issued.
          rc        The return code.
          subpool   The subpool from which storage was requested.
          amount    The amount of storage requested.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  If storage is being OBTAINed for a control block,
          an additional message will be issued identifying the control block
          for which storage could not be obtained.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For additional information on return
          codes, see the STORAGE macro.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR7005I STORAGE RELEASE error in module modname at label labname, RC =
          rc, ADDRESS = address, LENGTH = length, SUBPOOL = subpool.
          Explanation:  An error occurred during processing of the STORAGE
          macro.
          In the message text:
          modname   The module name in which the STORAGE was issued.
          labname   The label name at which the STORAGE was issued.
          rc        The return code.
          subpool   The subpool from which storage was requested.
          address   The starting address of the virtual storage to be
                    released.
          length    The length of the virtual storage to be released.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM processing continues.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For additional information on return
          codes, see the STORAGE macro.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR7006I LOAD error in module modname at label labname, RC = rc, ABEND
          CODE = code, ENTRY = entrynme.
          Explanation:  An error occurred during processing of a LOAD macro.
          The error routine specified on the LOAD macro was given control,
          that indicates an error condition that would have caused the task
          to end abnormally was detected.  Register 1 contains the abend code
          that would have resulted had the task abended, and register 15
          contains the reason code associated with the abend.
          In the message text:
          modname   The module name in which the LOAD was issued.
          labname   The label name at which the LOAD was issued.
          rc        The reason code.
          code      The abend code.
          entrynme  The name of the entry to be loaded.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM processing stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For additional information, see the
          LOAD macro.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR7010I ESTAE error in module modname at label labname, RC = rc.
          Explanation:  An error occurred during the processing of an ESTAE
          macro.
          In the message text:
          modname   The module name in which the ESTAE was issued.
          labname   The label name at which the ESTAE was issued.
          rc        The return code.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM processing continues.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For additional information on return
          codes, see the ESTAE macro.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR7011I WTOR error in module modname at label labname, RC = rc.
          Explanation:  An error occurred during processing of a WTOR macro.
          In the message text:
          modname   The module name in which the WTOR was issued.
          labname   The label name at which the WTOR was issued.
          rc        The return code.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM processing continues.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For additional information on return
          codes, see the WTOR macro.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR7014I TIME error in module modname at label labname, RC = rc.
          Explanation:  An error occurred during processing of a TIME macro.
          An error routine was given control following processing of a TIME
          macro indicating the TIME function could not be performed because
          of damaged clocks.
          In the message text:
          modname   The module name in which the TIME was issued.
          labname   The label name at which the TIME was issued.
          rc        The return code.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM processing continues.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For additional information on return
          codes, see the TIME macro.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR7017I WTO error in module modname at label labname, RC = rc.
          Explanation:  An error occurred during processing of a WTO macro.
          In the message text:
          modname   The module name in which the WTO was issued.
          labname   The label name at which the WTO was issued.
          rc        The return code.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM processing continues.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For additional information on return
          codes, see the WTO macro.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR7018I IDENTIFY error in module modname at label labname, RC = rc.
          Explanation:  An error occurred during processing of an IDENTIFY
          macro.
          In the message text:
          modname   The module name in which the IDENTIFY was issued.
          labname   The label name at which the IDENTIFY was issued.
          rc        The return code.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM processing continues.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For additional information, see the
          IDENTIFY macro.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR7019I Storage unavailable for recovery work area.
          Explanation:  The system services that establishes an ESTAE
          recovery environment attempted to STORAGE storage for a recovery
          work area (RWA).  The STORAGE failed.  This message is preceded by
          message CBR7004I, which contains the return code from the STORAGE
          macro.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM processing continues.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Determine the cause of the STORAGE
          error by investigating the return code from the STORAGE macro, and
          referring to the documentation for message CBR7004I.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR7020I LXRES error in module modname at label labname, RC = rc.
          Explanation:  An error occurred during processing of an LXRES
          macro.
          In the message text:
          modname   The module name in which the LXRES was issued.
          labname   The label name at which the LXRES was issued.
          rc        The return code.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For additional information on return
          codes, see the LXRES macro.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR7021I AXSET error in module modname at label labname, RC = rc.
          Explanation:  An error occurred during processing of an AXSET
          macro.
          In the message text:
          modname   The module name in which the AXSET was issued.
          labname   The label name at which the AXSET was issued.
          rc        The return code.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For additional information on return
          codes, see the AXSET macro.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR7022I ETCRE error in module modname at label labname, RC = rc.
          Explanation:  An error occurred during processing of an ETCRE
          macro.
          In the message text:
          modname   The module name in which the ETCRE was issued.
          labname   The label name at which the ETCRE was issued.
          rc        The return code.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For additional information on return
          codes, see the ETCRE macro.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR7023I ETCON error in module modname at label labname, RC = rc.
          Explanation:  An error occurred during processing of an ETCON
          macro.
          In the message text:
          modname   The module name in which the ETCON was issued.
          labname   The label name at which the ETCON was issued.
          rc        The return code.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For additional information on return
          codes, see the ETCON macro.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR7024I ETDES error in module modname at label labname, RC = rc.
          Explanation:  An error occurred during processing of an ETDES
          macro.
          In the message text:
          modname   The module name in which the ETDES was issued.
          labname   The label name at which the ETDES was issued.
          rc        The return code.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For additional information on return
          codes, see the ETDES macro.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR7025I TPUT error in module modname at label labname, RC = rc.
          Explanation:  An error occurred during processing of the TPUT
          macro.
          In the message text:
          modname   The module name in which the TPUT was issued.
          labname   The label name at which the TPUT was issued.
          rc        The return code.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM processing continues.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For additional information, see the
          TPUT macro.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR7050I Invalid date duration type type.
          Explanation:  The caller of the object access method (OAM)
          date/time service module passed an unknown parameter type.
          In the message text:
          type      The unknown date duration type.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM date/duration addition or subtraction does not
          occur.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Correct the date duration type
          parameter and restart the failed operation.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR7051I Calculated date above the valid date range was corrected to the
          maximum valid date.
          Explanation:  The result from object access method (OAM)
          date/duration addition was larger than the highest valid date.  It
          was changed to the maximum valid date - 9999-12-31.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer if excessive
          corrections occur.
          System Programmer Response:  None, unless excessive, and
          unexpected, corrections occur.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR7052I Calculated date below the valid date range was corrected to the
          minimum valid date.
          Explanation:  The result from object access method (OAM)
          date/duration subtraction was smaller than the lowest valid date.
          It was changed to the minimum valid date - 0001-01-01.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer if excessive
          corrections occur.
          System Programmer Response:  None, unless excessive, and
          unexpected, corrections occur.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR7099I Message msgid not found in message CSECT.
          Explanation:  An error occurred when an object access method (OAM)
          module attempted to issue a message that was not found in the
          message CSECT.
          In the message text:
          msgid     The message that is missing from the CSECT.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM processing continues.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Check the module that contains the
          messages for OAM to see if the message is contained in the CSECT.
          If the message is contained in the message CSECT, verify that the
          message is coded correctly, especially the message number.  If the
          message is not contained in the message CSECT, check to see if the
          message is contained in the documentation.  If the message is not
          contained in the documentation, determine the module that is
          attempting to issue the message.  Determine under which conditions
          the message is being issued and what variable data is to be placed
          in the message.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR7100I Abnormal termination ffsssuuu in task taskname tcbaddr at
          location address.
          Explanation:  An abnormal end has occurred in one of the object
          access method (OAM) tasks.  If the task name is CBRCT, the OAM
          control task abnormally ended.  If the characters UNKNOWN appear
          for address, no system diagnostic work area (SDWA) was provided to
          the ESTAI recovery routine so the address of the abnormal end could
          not be placed in the message.
          In the message text:
          ffsssuuu  The type of abnormal end, where ff is the indicator
                    flags, sss is the system completion code and uuu is the
                    user completion code.
          taskname  The task that is abnormally ended.
          tcbaddr   The address of the task control block (TCB) for the
                    abnormally ending task.
          address   The location of the abnormal end.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  For tasks other than CBRCT, the task is re-attached
          and OAM processing continues.  If the abnormally ending task is
          CBRCT, OAM ends.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR7101I PSW at time of error upperpsw lowerpsw.
          Explanation:  An abnormal end has occurred in one of the object
          access method (OAM) tasks.  The processor status word (PSW) at the
          time of error is obtained from the SDWAEC1 field in the system
          diagnostic work area (SDWA).
          In the message text:
          upperpsw  The upper PSW.
          lowerpsw  The lower PSW.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  For tasks other than CBRCT, the task is re-attached
          and OAM processing continues.  If the abnormally ending task is
          CBRCT, OAM ends.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR7102I General purpose registers at time of error:
          Explanation:  An abnormal end has occurred in one of the object
          access method (OAM) tasks.  The general purpose registers at the
          time of the error are displayed in the following four messages:
          CBR7103I, CBR7104I, CBR7105I and CBR7106I.  This message only
          appears if a system diagnostic work area (SDWA) was provided by the
          MVS recovery termination manager (RTM) to the ESTAI recovery
          routine.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  For tasks other than CBRCT, the task is re-attached
          and OAM processing continues.  If the abnormally ending task is
          CBRCT, OAM ends.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR7103I  0-3  reg0 reg1 reg2 reg3
          Explanation:  An abnormal end has occurred in one of the object
          access method (OAM) tasks.  This message displays the contents of
          general purpose registers 0, 1, 2 and 3 at the time of the abnormal
          end.  The registers at time of the error were obtained from the
          SDWAGRSV field of the system diagnostic work area (SDWA).  This
          message only appears if a system diagnostic work area (SDWA) was
          provided by the MVS recovery termination manager (RTM) to the ESTAI
          recovery routine.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  For tasks other than CBRCT, the task is re-attached
          and OAM processing continues.  If the abnormally ending task is
          CBRCT, OAM ends.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR7104I  4-7  reg4 reg5 reg6 reg7
          Explanation:  An abnormal end has occurred in one of the object
          access method (OAM) tasks.  This message displays the contents of
          general purpose registers 4, 5, 6 and 7 at the time of the abnormal
          end.  The registers at time of the error were obtained from the
          SDWAGRSV field of the system diagnostic work area (SDWA).  This
          message only appears if a system diagnostic work area (SDWA) was
          provided by the MVS recovery termination manager (RTM) to the ESTAI
          recovery routine.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  For tasks other than CBRCT, the task is re-attached
          and OAM processing continues.  If the abnormally ending task is
          CBRCT, OAM ends.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR7105I  8-11 reg8 reg9 reg10 reg11
          Explanation:  An abnormal end has occurred in one of the object
          access method (OAM) tasks.  This message displays the contents of
          general purpose registers 8, 9, 10 and 11 at the time of the
          abnormal end.  The registers at time of the error were obtained
          from the SDWAGRSV field of the system diagnostic work area (SDWA).
          This message only appears if a system diagnostic work area (SDWA)
          was provided by the MVS recovery termination manager (RTM) to the
          ESTAI recovery routine.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  For tasks other than CBRCT, the task is re-attached
          and OAM processing continues.  If the abnormally ending task is
          CBRCT, OAM ends.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR7106I 12-15 reg12 reg13 reg14 reg15
          Explanation:  An abnormal end has occurred in one of the object
          access method (OAM) tasks.  This message displays the contents of
          general purpose registers 12, 13, 14 and 15 at the time of the
          abnormal end.  The registers at time of the error were obtained
          from the SDWAGRSV field of the system diagnostic work area (SDWA).
          This message only appears if a system diagnostic work area (SDWA)
          was provided by the MVS recovery termination manager (RTM) to the
          ESTAI recovery routine.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  For tasks other than CBRCT, the task is re-attached
          and OAM processing continues.  If the abnormally ending task is
          CBRCT, OAM ends.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR7107I Abnormal termination located at offset offset in module modname.
          Explanation:  An abnormal end has occurred in one of the object
          access method (OAM) tasks.  The abnormal end is located at the
          specified offset in the module.
          If the characters UNKNOWN appear for the module name, the abnormal
          end occurred outside of the OAM load module.
          In the message text:
          offset    The offset at which the abnormal end occurred.
          modname   The module name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  For tasks other than CBRCT, the task is re-attached
          and OAM processing continues.  If the abnormally ending task is
          CBRCT, OAM ends.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR7111I Internal error in module modname data1 data2 data3 data4 data5
          data6 data7 data8.
          Explanation:  An internal error occurred in the specified module.
          In the message text:
          modname   The module name.
          data1 data2 data3 data4 data5 data6 data7 data8
                    Diagnostic information.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM processing continues.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR7200I Invalid library name libname passed to module CBRSFSCB     .
          Explanation:  An incorrect library name was passed.
          In the message text:
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM processing continues.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Determine which module passed the
          incorrect library name.  Correct the error.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR7201I Invalid slot name slotname passed to module CBRSFSCB.
          Explanation:  An incorrect slot name was passed.
          In the message text:
          slotname  The slot name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM processing continues.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Determine which module passed the
          incorrect slot name.  Correct the error.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR7300I Error occurred in the TSO parsing routine IKJPARS, rc = rc.
          Explanation:  An error occurred parsing the parameter fields
          entered on the interactive problem control system (IPCS)
          invocation.
          In the message text:
          rc        The return code.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  Dump formatting stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Check the parameter fields entered on
          the IPCS invocation.  Contact the service representative.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR7301I Unable to access the contblk control block located at address
          addr.
          Explanation:  CBRIPCS tried to access data from a storage dump for
          the control block at the specified address, but the interactive
          problem control system (IPCS) service routine returned with a
          nonzero return code.
          In the message text:
          contblk   The control block
          addr      The address.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  Dump formatting stops for that control block.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  If an address was specified with the
          parameter at invocation, check to make sure it is a valid address.
          If it is, contact the service representative.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR7302I The pointer to the contblk control block is zero.
          Explanation:  An error is implied if at the time the dump was taken
          there should be a control block of the type indicated.  However, it
          could also be there are no control blocks of that type at this
          time.
          In the message text:
          contblk   The specified control block.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  Dump formatting stops for that control block.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Contact the service representative.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR7303I Hex value hexval supplied with the parm parameter is invalid.
          Explanation:  The hexadecimal value supplied with the parameter
          does not translate into a valid hexadecimal number.
          In the message text:
          hexval    The hexadecimal value.
          parm      The parameter.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  Further processing of that parameter stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Invoke interactive problem control
          system (IPCS) with a valid hexadecimal number on the parameter.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR7304I More than 256 entries of the contblk control block have been
          found.
          Explanation:  When processing the control block, more than 256
          entries were found.  To prevent being in a loop, a maximum of 256
          control blocks of any one type will be formatted.
          In the message text:
          contblk   The control block.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  Dump formatting of that control block stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Contact the service representative.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR7305I The contblk control block located at address addr is invalid.
          Explanation:  When processing the control block, the header does
          not contain a valid identifier and is therefore not a control block
          of that type.
          In the message text:
          contblk   The control block.
          addr      The address.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  Dump formatting of that control block stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Contact the service representative.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR7306I Unable to print the contblk control block, return code = rc.
          Explanation:  When trying to format and print the control block,
          the interactive problem control system (IPCS) service routine
          ADPLSFMT failed.
          In the message text:
          contblk   The control block.
          rc        The return code.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  Dump formatting of that control block stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For additional information on the IPCS
          format and print service ADPLSFMT see the MVS/ESA Interactive
          Problem Control System User's Guide and Reference. Contact the
          service representative.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR7307I Individual control block parameters are mutually exclusive with
          the CBDUMP parameter.
          Explanation:  Do not specify individual control block parameters
          along with the CBDUMP parameter.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  Dump formatting stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Check the input parameters and rerun.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR7308I STORAGE error for the contblk control block, RC = rc, SUBPOOL =
          0, AMOUNT = amount.
          Explanation:  An error occurred during processing of a STORAGE
          macro.  The STORAGE macro was issued to get a private copy of the
          control block.
          In the message text:
          contblk   The control block.
          rc        The return code.
          amount    The amount of storage requested.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  Control block formatting stops for related control
          blocks only.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For additional information on return
          codes, see the STORAGE macro.  Contact the service representative.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR7309I STORAGE RELEASE error for the contblk control block, RC = rc,
          SUBPOOL = 0, AMOUNT = amount.
          Explanation:  An error occurred during processing of a STORAGE
          RELEASE macro.  The STORAGE RELEASE macro was issued to free a
          private copy of the control block.
          In the message text:
          contblk   The control block.
          rc        The return code.
          amount    The amount of storage requested.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For additional information on return
          codes, see the STORAGE RELEASE macro.  Contact the service
          representative.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR7310I The contblk control block is located at address addr.
          Explanation:  CBRIPCS found that the data from a storage dump for
          the control block is located at the indicated address.
          In the message text:
          contblk   The control block.
          addr      The address at which the storage dump data is located.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR7510I OAM unable to CONNECT; DB2 not available.
          Explanation:  The attempt by the Call Attach Facility (CAF) to
          establish the object access method (OAM) address space as a user of
          DB2 failed because the DB2 subsystem was not up.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  Initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  START DB2.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR7515A OAM initialization suspended. Start DB2 required.
          Explanation:  DB2 is not available, therefore there is no way to
          access the Optical Configuration Database.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  Suspend OAM initialization until DB2 is available.
          OAM will wait until DB2 has started, and will attempt to connect to
          DB2.
          Operator Response:  The operator is required to start DB2.  If this
          connection attempt fails MULTIPLE times, the operator can need to
          abnormally end OAM.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR7530E OAM degraded. DB2 is not available. Start DB2.
          Explanation:  DB2 is not available; therefore there is no way to
          access the optical configuration database.  The operator is
          required to start DB2.  Once DB2 has been started, object access
          method (OAM) will attempt to reconnect to DB2.  If this
          reconnection attempt fails multiple times, the operator can need to
          abnormally end OAM.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  Withhold all requests of the Database Manager until
          DB2 is available.
          Operator Response:  START DB2.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR7535I OAM back at full capacity; DB2 now available.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) was operating in degraded
          mode because DB2 was temporarily unavailable.  DB2 is now available
          and OAM has successfully performed a disconnect/reconnect.  OAM
          processing can continue as if DB2 had never been unavailable.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  Allow all requests of the Database Manager to be
          processed.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR7550I OAM connection to DB2 via CAF failed.
          Explanation:  The attempt to establish the object access method
          (OAM) address space as a user of DB2 via Call Attach Facility (CAF)
          failed for some reason other than DB2 unavailability.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  Retry request.  If retry fails, abnormally end OAM.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR7570I OAM termination requested; DB2 unavailable.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) was unable to initialize
          because of a severe error using DB2.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM initialization stops.
          Operator Response:  It is likely that DB2 is encountering severe
          errors, contact the Database Administrator to ensure DB2 is
          functioning correctly and restart OAM.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR7575I CAF has issued a return code of rc and reason code of rsncde
          within function function.
          Explanation:  A nonzero return code was received from the Call
          Attachment Facility (CAF).  The return code is returned in decimal
          and the reason code in hexadecimal.  Descriptions of errors can be
          found in DATABASE 2 Application Programming Guide.
          In the message text:
          rc        The return code.
          rsncde    The reason code.
          function  The function in which the return and reason codes are
                    issued.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system continues processing.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR7580I SQL translation error in routine DSNTIAR, RC = rc.
          Explanation:  An error occurred in DSNTIAR while trying to
          translate an SQL error into its appropriate error message.
          In the message text:
          rc        The return code.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM processing continues.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For additional information on return
          codes from the DSNTIAR routine, see DATABASE 2 Application
          Programming Guide.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR7585I An SQL error occurred:  text
          Explanation:  An SQL error has occurred.
          In the message text:
          text      The text of the error.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM processing continues.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For information on SQL errors, consult
          IBM DATABASE 2 Version 2 Messages and Codes.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR8001I OAM subsystem initialization starting.
          Explanation:  The object access method (OAM) subsystem
          initialization has begun.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  Subsystem processing continues.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR8002I OAM subsystem initialization completed.
          Explanation:  The object access method (OAM) subsystem
          initialization has successfully completed.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  Subsystem processing continues.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR8003I OAM unable to load module modname.
          Explanation:  The object access method (OAM) was unable to load the
          module.
          In the message text:
          modname   The name of the module.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  Initialization stops.  The OAM subsystem will be
          rendered unusable.  Attempts to start the OAM subsystem can result
          in failure.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Verify that the module has been placed
          in an accessible library (ELPA, LPA, LINKLST).
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR8004I OAM unable to obtain virtual storage.
          Explanation:  The object access method (OAM) was unable to obtain
          the virtual storage required for an OSR fundamental control block
          data area.  Initialization stops.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  Subsystem processing stops.  Refer to message
          CBR8003I.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Verify that Extended Common Storage
          Area (ECSA) has been defined.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR8101I The OAM/CICS interface is now connected.
          Explanation:  The object access method (OAM) has been connected to
          this CICS address space.  OAM has initialized the CICS Resource
          Manager Interface and OSREQ macros can be issued.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  Subsystem processing continues.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR8103I The OAM/CICS interface is already connected.
          Explanation:   The object access method (OAM) connection to this
          CICS address space was previously completed.  OAM has initialized
          the CICS Resource Manager Interface and OSREQ macros can be issued.
          This condition can occur when the CBRICONN transaction is entered
          manually after initialization is complete.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  Subsystem processing continues.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR8104I OSR's entry point not found by the load macro.
          Explanation:   The object access method (OAM) connection to this
          CICS address space was not completed because the OSR load module
          entry point needed to initialize the CICS Resource Manager
          Interface could not be found.  This failure is due to an improper
          installation of the OSR function of the OAM.  The OAM has not
          initialized the CICS Resource Manager Interface, and OSREQ macros
          cannot be issued.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  Subsystem processing continues.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Check the installation procedure used
          to install OAM and particularly the load module that contains the
          OSR code.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR8105I OAM/CICS interface is not operational.
          Explanation:  The object access method (OAM) connection
          initialization has not completed for this CICS address space.  The
          reason for this failure is noted in a previously issued message.
          OAM has not initialized the CICS Resource Manager Interface and
          OSREQ macros cannot be issued.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  Subsystem processing continues.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Check the installation procedure used
          to install OAM; particularly the load module that contains the OSR
          code.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9000I OSMC initialization starting.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component initialization is starting.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  Processing begins.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9001I OSMC initialization completed.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component has successfully completed its initialization.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  Processing continues.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9002I OSMC initialization failed.
          Explanation:  The object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component failed during initialization. Refer to the preceding
          messages for further information.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM Storage Management Component processing stops.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9003I Addressability not obtained for sysser.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) could not locate an entry
          for the system service in the OAM External Symbol Dictionary (ESD).
          In the message text:
          sysser    The system service.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM Storage Management Component processing stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Determine why the ESD did not contain
          an entry for the system service.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9004I Storage group name strgrpnm is invalid.
          Explanation:  This is an incorrect storage group name.  A storage
          group name should have been declared as TYPE=OBJECT.  This command
          will not be processed.
          In the message text:
          strgrpnm  The storage group name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM Storage Management Component processing
          continues.
          Operator Response:  Notify your storage administrator.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9005I OSMC is terminating.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component (OSMC) is stopping because of an abnormal condition.  All
          possible work in progress will complete prior to stopping.  OAM
          will attempt to restart OSMC.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM Storage Management Component stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Refer to previous messages and/or dump
          for further detailed information.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9006I Error establishing the Control Task for ctcname.
          Explanation:  The object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component initialization module attempted to establish a control
          task.
          OAM Storage Management Component initialization was unable to
          establish the subtask due to the attach of the subtask failing or
          the subtask not initializing successfully.
          In the message text:
          ctcname   The CTC name, which may include:
                    °   Storage Group Name
                    °   Library Name
                    °   Volume Serial Number for Disk Recovery
                    or the actual name may be one of the following:
                    °   CBRHXINT
                    °   CBRHSGDP
                    °   SUMMARY
                    °   OBJ_RECV
                    °   OBJ_PROC
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM Storage Management Component processing
          continues.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Either the attach failed or the
          subtask initialization failed.  If the attach failed, this message
          will be preceded by message CBR7000I which contains the return code
          from the ATTACH macro.  If the subtask initialization failed, this
          message will be preceded by messages which further describe that
          failure.  Refer to documentation for further information.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9007I Error detaching the Control Task for ctcname.
          Explanation:  The object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component initialization end-of-task routine failed to detach a
          control task.
          In the message text:
          ctcname   The CTC name, which may include:
                    °   Storage Group Name
                    °   Library Name
                    °   Volume Serial Number for Disk Recovery
                    or the actual name may be one of the following:
                    °   CBRHXINT
                    °   CBRHSGDP
                    °   SUMMARY
                    °   OBJ_RECV
                    °   OBJ_PROC
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM Storage Management Component processing
          continues.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  The DETACH macro failed. This message
          will be preceded by message CBR7001I which contains the return code
          from the DETACH macro.  Refer to documentation for message
          CBR7001I.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9009I OSMC completed its Storage Management Cycle.  nnn tasks started.
          xxx tasks completed.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component has completed its Storage Management Cycle.
          In the message text:
          nnn       The number of tasks started.
          xxx       The number of tasks completed.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system continues processing.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9010I OSMC has stopped.
          Explanation:  The object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component has stopped its processing due to an operator request or
          a request from OAM.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM Storage Management Component processing
          stopped.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9011I OAM requested OSMC to terminate.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component received a request to stop processing from the OAM
          control task.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OSMC will not allow current objects to complete
          processing.  OSMC processing stops.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9012I OSMC completed termination.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component has stopped its processing due to a request from OAM.
          Control returns to OAM.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM Storage Management Component stops.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9013I Optical Disk Recovery already started for volume volser /
          ovolser.
          Explanation:  The request for optical disk recovery has been
          rejected because object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component (OSMC) was currently recovering this volume.
          In the message text:
          volser    The volume serial number.
          ovolser   The volume serial number on the opposite side of the
                    disk.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9014I Error establishing the object service objserv for control task
          ctcname.
          Explanation:  The object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component control task attempted to establish an object service
          routine for the control task.  OAM Storage Management Component
          Control Task was unable to start the specified object service
          module or the object service module did not initialize
          successfully.
c.cc 6
          In the message text:
          objserv   The object services are as follows:
                    °   CBRHROPT
                    °   CBRHWOPT
                    °   CBRHWBKP
                    °   CBRHEXEJ
                    °   CBRHRDAS
                    °   CBRHDUPD
                    °   CBRHWDAS
          ctcname   The CTC name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM Storage Management Component initialization
          stops for this control task.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  See preceding messages. If the Control
          Task failed to start the object service with the ATTACH macro,
          message CBR7000I will precede, containing the return code from the
          ATTACH macro.  If the object service initialization failed, this
          message will be preceded by messages which further describes that
          failure.  Refer to documentation for preceding messages.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9015I Error detaching the object service objserv.
          Explanation:  The object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component control task end-of-task routine attempted to detach an
          object service routine.  OAM Storage Management Component Control
          Task end-of-task routine was unable to detach the object service
          routine due to the failure of the DETACH macro.
          In the message text:
          objserv   The object services are as follows:
                    °   CBRHROPT
                    °   CBRHWOPT
                    °   CBRHWBKP
                    °   CBRHEXEJ
                    °   CBRHRDAS
                    °   CBRHDUPD
                    °   CBRHWDAS
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM Storage Management Component processing
          continues.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  The DETACH macro failed. This message
          will be preceded by message CBR7001I which contains the return code
          from the DETACH macro.  Refer to documentation for message
          CBR7001I.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9016I Dormant Task not found. TCB address tcbptr invalid.
          Explanation:  An end-of-task routine can't find the dormant task
          due to an incorrect task control block (TCB) address.
          In the message text:
          tcbptr    The TCB pointer to the TCB address.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM Storage Management Component (OSMC) will
          continue processing.
          Operator Response:  Wait for OSMC cycle to completely cancel OAM.
          System Programmer Response:  Notify the service representative.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9018I OSMC starting Storage Management Cycle.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component (OSMC) is starting its Storage Management Cycle
          processing.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OSMC processing continues.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9019I Library Space Management not started for libname.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component (OSMC) could not start Library Space Management for the
          library.
          In the message text:
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM Storage Management Component continues
          processing.
          System Programmer Response:  Refer to preceding messages for
          additional information.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9020I Optical Disk Recovery not started for volser.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component (OSMC) could not start Optical Disk Recovery for the
          specified volume.
          In the message text:
          volser    The volume serial number.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM Storage Management Component processing
          continues.
          System Programmer Response:  Refer to preceding messages for
          additional information.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9021I Storage unavailable for CBRHMCB control block. Initialization
          terminated.
          Explanation:  The STORAGE macro failed while object access method
          (OAM) Storage Management Component was attempting to obtain storage
          for the control block. This message is preceded by message CBR7004I
          which contains the return code from the STORAGE macro.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM Storage Management Component initialization
          stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Investigate the return code from the
          STORAGE macro and refer to the documentation for message CBR7004I.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9022I Single object executing for objname is already active.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component (OSMC) is already processing that object.  Command is
          ignored.
          In the message text:
          objname   The object name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM Storage Management Component processing
          continues.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9023I OSMC already started Storage Management Cycle.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component (OSMC) is currently processing its Storage Management
          Cycle.  Operator command is ignored.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM Storage Management Component processing
          continues.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9025I CBRHSLSM unable to start library space management for library
          libname.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) was unable to notify OAM
          Storage Management Component (OSMC) to start library space
          management. Refer to the preceding messages for more information.
          In the message text:
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OSMC does not process this library request.
          Application Programmer Response:  Notify the system programmer.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9026I CBRHSRCV unable to start optical disk recovery for volume volser.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) was unable to notify OAM
          Storage Management Component (OSMC) to start optical disk recovery.
          Refer to the preceding messages for more information.
          In the message text:
          volser    The volume serial number.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OSMC continues processing.
          Application Programmer Response:  Notify the system programmer.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9027I Catalog error while locating collection name collname: Return
          code is rc Reason code is rsncde.
          Explanation:  An error occurred while processing a catalog locate
          for a collection name.
          In the message text:
          collname  The collection name.
          rc        The return code.
          rsncde    The reason code.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system continues processing.
          Operator Response:  Notify system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Determine why there was a catalog
          error.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9028I DB2 error while locating collection name collname: Return code is
          rc Reason code is rsncde.
          Explanation:  An error occurred while processing a DB2 request for
          a collection name.
          In the message text:
          collname  The collection name.
          rc        The return code.
          rsncde    The reason code.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system continues processing.
          Operator Response:  Notify system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Determine why there was a DB2 error.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9030I Unable to compare catalog entry with DB2 entry.
          Explanation:  Due to errors, the comparison between the catalog
          entry and the DB2 entry for collection names can not be done.  The
          audit utility will stop processing.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system continues processing.
          Operator Response:  Notify system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Determine why audit utility failed.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9031I OSMC Storage Management Cycle Processing not started because OSMC
          has been requested to {Stop|Terminate} processing.
          Explanation:  The operator has requested object access method (OAM)
          Storage Management Component (OSMC) to process its Storage
          Management Cycle.  However, OSMC will not process the request
          because either the operator has issued an OSMC STOP command or OAM
          has requested OSMC to stop processing.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OSMC will continue processing accordingly.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9032I Invalid option specified with MAXS= keyword.  Parameters
          specified = parms.  Initialization terminated.
          Explanation:  The MAXS= startup keyword was specified with the PARM
          keyword on the JCL EXEC statement used to start object access
          method (OAM).  An incorrect value of zero was specified following
          the MAXS= startup keyword.  The MAXS= keyword must either be
          omitted, in which case a default of two will be used, or specify a
          one or two digit numeric value larger than zero.
          In the message text:
          parms     The parameters specified.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM Storage Management Component (OSMC)
          initialization stops.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9033I Collection audit utility will not process due to failures.
          Explanation:  The collection audit utility will not process due to
          failures from DB2 or from deadlock/timeouts from the system.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system continues processing.
          Operator Response:  Notify system administrator.
          System Programmer Response:  Determine why failures occurred.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9034I Deadlock or time out occurred during collection audit utility.
          Explanation:  A DB2 deadlock occurred on the collection names table
          while collection names were being selected from it.  This may be
          caused by updates being made to the table while collection names
          were being selected.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The collection names table will be closed,
          re-opened, and the collection names will be selected again.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9035I Retry of deadlock or time out exceeded 30 times.
          Explanation:  A DB2 deadlock occurred on the collection names table
          while collection names were being selected from it.  This was
          retried 30 times, and deadlock/timeout situation still exists. The
          collection audit utility will not continue; however, initialization
          processing will continue.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system continues processing.
          Operator Response:  Notify system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Determine why there is a
          deadlock/timeout situation.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9036I A DB2 operation requested by the collection audit utility failed.
          Return code is rc Reason code is rsncde
          Explanation:  An error occurred while requesting a DB2 function.
          In the message text:
          rc        The return code.
          rsncde    The reason code.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system continues processing.
          Operator Response:  Notify database administrator.
          System Programmer Response:  Determine why DB2 failed.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9040I Single storage group processing not started for strgrpnm.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component (OSMC) could not start single storage group processing
          for the specified storage group.
          In the message text:
          strgrpnm  The storage group name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The OAM Storage Management Component processing
          continues.
          System Programmer Response:  Refer to the preceding messages for
          additional information.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9041I Display detail information not started for strgrpnm.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component (OSMC) could not start display detail information for the
          specified storage group.
          In the message text:
          strgrpnm  The storage group name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The OAM Storage Management Component processing
          continues.
          System Programmer Response:  Refer to the preceding messages for
          additional information.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9042I Display summary information not started.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component (OSMC) could not start display summary information.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The OAM Storage Management Component processing
          continues.
          System Programmer Response:  Refer to the preceding messages for
          additional information.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9043I DASD Space Manager not started for strgrpnm.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component (OSMC) could not start DASD Space Manager for that
          storage group.
          In the message text:
          strgrpnm  The storage group name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The OAM Storage Management Component processing
          continues.
          System Programmer Response:  Refer to the preceding messages for
          additional information.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9044I Single Object Recovery not started for collection collname,
          object objname.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component (OSMC) could not start Single Object Recovery for that
          object.
          In the message text:
          collname  The collection name.
          objname   The object name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The OAM Storage Management Component processing
          continues.
          System Programmer Response:  Refer to the preceding messages for
          additional information.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9045I Single Object Processing not started for collection collname,
          object objname.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component (OSMC) could not start Single Object Processing for that
          object.
          In the message text:
          collname  The collection name.
          objname   The object name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The OAM Storage Management Component processing
          continues.
          System Programmer Response:  Refer to the preceding messages for
          additional information.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9046I DB2 stopping, OSMC will terminate.
          Explanation:  The object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component received a request to stop processing from the OAM
          control task because DB2 is stopping.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OSMC will not allow current objects to complete
          processing.  OSMC processing stops.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9047I Operator requested OSMC to stop processing.
          Explanation:  The object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component received a request to stop processing from the operator.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM Storage Management Component processing stops
          after allowing current objects to complete processing.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9048I Storage Group strgrpnm has successfully completed processing.
          Explanation:  The object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component has finished processing a storage group successfully.
          In the message text:
          strgrpnm  The storage group name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system continues processing.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9049I Storage Group strgrpnm has unsuccessfully completed processing.
          Explanation:  The object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component has finished processing a storage group unsuccessfully.
          In the message text:
          strgrpnm  The storage group name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system continues processing.
          System Programmer Response:  Refer to previous messages for
          additional information.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9050I ctcname process module-name requested a nonexistent service for
          collection collection-name, object object-name.
          Explanation:  The control task ctcname process module-name
          requested an undefined read|write optical data or directory update
          operation for object object-name.  This was probably caused by a
          programming error.
