          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  Processing for object object-name fails.
          Operator Response:  None.
          System Programmer Response:  Notify the service representative.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9051I ctcname process modname requested multiple read services for
          collection collname, object objname.
          Explanation:  The control task process requested more than one type
          of read operation for the specified object.  Object access method
          (OAM) Storage Management Component (OSMC) only allows one read for
          each object.
          In the message text:
          ctcname   The CTC name.
          modname   The name of the module.
          collname  The collection name.
          objname   The object name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  Processing for that object fails.
          System Programmer Response:  Notify the service representative.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9052I ctcname process modname requested {a read|a write|an optical
          write} service without {a write|a read|an optical data} service for
          collection collname, object objname.
          Explanation:  The control task process requested a read, write, or
          optical write operation for the specified object without also
          requesting the required corresponding operation.  Each read
          operation must be followed by a write and all writes must be
          preceded by a read.  Optical write operations must be followed by a
          request to test, and potentially update, the expiration and/or
          ejection dates associated with the optical volume.
          In the message text:
          ctcname   The CTC name.
          modname   The name of the module.
          collname  The collection name.
          objname   The object name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  Processing for that object fails.
          System Programmer Response:  Notify the service representative.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9053I cntl process modname tried to read an object from the device to
          which it planned to write that object.  The object is in collection
          collname named objname
          Explanation:  The control task process is requesting object access
          method (OAM) Storage Management Component (OSMC) to read and write
          object on the same device.  OSMC will only write to a device which
          does not already have a copy of the object.  Likewise, it cannot
          read data from a device which does not already have a copy of that
          data.
          In the message text:
          cntl      The control task name.
          modname   The name of the module.
          collname  The collection name.
          objname   The object name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  Object in the collection named by the object name
          will not be processed.  The Control task will stop after issuing
          message CBR9062 after too many request validation errors of this
          type, or any other type, have occurred.
          System Programmer Response:  Notify the service representative.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9055I ctcname process modname did not request completion processing for
          collection collname object objname.
          Explanation:  The control task process failed to request completion
          processing for the collection of an object.  Object access method
          (OAM) Storage Management Component (OSMC) requires completion
          processing for all objects using its services.
          In the message text:
          ctcname   The CTC name.
          modname   The name of the module.
          collname  The collection name.
          objname   The object name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  Processing for object in collection named by the
          object name fails.
          System Programmer Response:  Notify the service representative.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9056I ctcname process modname selected incorrect update transaction
          code trnscde for collection collname, object objname.
          Explanation:  The control task process requested completion
          processing of an object but object access method (OAM) Storage
          Management Component (OSMC) has no completion procedure of the type
          specified.
c.cc 6
          In the message text:
          ctcname   The CTC name.
          modname   The name of the module.
          trnscde   The transaction code.
          collname  The collection name.
          objname   The object name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  Processing for this object fails.
          System Programmer Response:  Notify the service representative.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9057I cntl synchronous OSMC service objsernm requested routing for
          collection collname object objname before it completed processing.
          Explanation:  Control task synchronous OSMC service requested
          routing for a collection of an object before it finished processing
          that object.
          In the message text:
          cntl      The control task name.
          objsernm  The object service name.
          collname  The collection name.
          objname   The object name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  Processing stops for this object.  OSMC will issue
          message CBR9915I and stop the control task if too many errors of
          this type occur.
          System Programmer Response:  Notify the service representative.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9058I ctcname process modname made a routing error for an object in
          collection collname, object objname.
          Explanation:  The object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component (OSMC) router for the control task could not determine
          the next service to which the object should be routed.
          In the message text:
          ctcname   The CTC name.
          modname   The name of the module.
          collname  The collection name.
          objname   The object name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  Processing for the object fails.
          System Programmer Response:  Notify the service representative.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9059I ctcname process modname cannot suppress completion processing for
          collection collname object objname.
          Explanation:  The control task process attempted to suppress object
          access method (OAM) Storage Management Component (OSMC) completion
          processing for the collection.  OSMC only allows the Shelf Manager
          to suppress completion processing.
          In the message text:
          ctcname   The CTC name.
          modname   The name of the module.
          collname  The collection name.
          objname   The object name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  Processing for the collection fails.
          System Programmer Response:  Notify the service representative.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9060I ctcname process modname had a STORAGE RELEASE error in module
          CBRHROUT for collection collname object objname's read buffer.
          Explanation:  The control task process STORAGE RELEASE macro failed
          while the object access method (OAM) Storage Management Component
          (OSMC) object router was trying to free the read buffer for object
          in the collection.  This message is preceded by message CBR7005I
          which contains the return code from the STORAGE RELEASE macro.
          In the message text:
          ctcname   The CTC name.
          modname   The name of the module.
          collname  The collection name.
          objname   The object name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM Storage Management Component stops processing
          this object.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Investigate the return code from the
          STORAGE RELEASE macro and refer to the documentation for message
          CBR7005I.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9061I OSMC stopping. Start storage group command for strgrpnm not
          processed.
          Explanation:  Start storage group command ignored due to impending
          OSMC stop.
          In the message text:
          strgrpnm  The storage group name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  Object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component (OSMC) does not queue the start storage group command.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9062I Module CBRHROUT is stopping OSMC control task cntl process mod
          because of an excessive number of service request errors.
          Explanation:  The object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component (OSMC) router received too many incorrect service
          requests for objects selected by the specified process.  It is
          stopping the control task that governs that process.
          In the message text:
          cntl      The control task name.
          mod       The module name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  Processing stops for the control task.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9063I Storage group strgrpnm already active.
          Explanation:  Storage group already started and active.
          In the message text:
          strgrpnm  The storage group name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM Storage Management Component (OSMC) does not
          queue the start storage group command.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9064I Storage management cycle in process. Storage group strgrpnm will
          be processed next.
          Explanation:  Storage management cycle processes all storage
          groups. The storage group requested for processing will be moved to
          the front of the storage management cycle queue.
          In the message text:
          strgrpnm  The storage group name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM Storage Management Component (OSMC) moves
          processing of requested storage group to front of storage
          management cycle queue in order to process it next.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9066I OSMC already stopping. Operator command to stop OSMC not
          processed.
          Explanation:  Operator command to stop object access method (OAM)
          Storage Management Component (OSMC) ignored due to impending OSMC
          stop.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OSMC does not queue the stop OSMC command.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9069I CBRHPSMC unable to process stop OSMC command.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component (OSMC) unable to queue the stop OSMC command. Refer to
          the preceding messages for more information.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OSMC does not queue the stop OSMC command.
          Application Programmer Response:  Notify the system programmer.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9070I OSMC stopping. Library space management command for libname not
          processed.
          Explanation:  Library space management command ignored due to
          impending object access method (OAM) Storage Management Component
          (OSMC) stop.
          In the message text:
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OSMC does not queue the library space management
          command.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9071I CBRHSDSM unable to start DASD space management for storage group
          strgrpnm.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component (OSMC) unable to queue the DASD space management command.
          Refer to the preceding messages for more information.
          In the message text:
          strgrpnm  The storage group name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OSMC does not queue the DASD space management
          command.
          Application Programmer Response:  Notify the system programmer.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9072I CBRHSSG unable to start storage group strgrpnm.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component (OSMC) unable to queue the start storage group command.
          Refer to the preceding messages for more information.
          In the message text:
          strgrpnm  The storage group name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OSMC does not queue the start storage group
          command.
          Application Programmer Response:  Notify the system programmer.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9073I Stop storage group command for strgrpnm already on queue.
          Explanation:  A stop storage group command for this storage group
          has previously been issued. The current command becomes redundant.
          In the message text:
          strgrpnm  The storage group name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM Storage Management Component (OSMC) does not
          queue the stop storage group command.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9074I Storage group strgrpnm not active. Stop storage group command not
          processed.
          Explanation:  A stop storage group command for an inactive storage
          group has been issued. A storage group must be active to be
          stopped.
          In the message text:
          strgrpnm  The storage group name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM Storage Management Component (OSMC) does not
          queue the stop storage group command.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9075I CBRHPSG unable to stop storage group strgrpnm.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component (OSMC) unable to queue the stop storage group command.
          Refer to the preceding messages for more information.
          In the message text:
          strgrpnm  The storage group name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OSMC does not queue the start storage group
          command.
          Application Programmer Response:  Notify the system programmer.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9076I Start storage group command for strgrpnm deleted from operator
          parameter queue.
          Explanation:  A start storage group command for this storage group
          has previously been issued. This start storage group command will
          be ignored due to the more recent stop storage group command.
          In the message text:
          strgrpnm  The storage group name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM Storage Management Component (OSMC) does not
          process the start storage group command.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9077I Start storage group command for strgrpnm deleted from storage
          management cycle queue.
          Explanation:  A storage management cycle processes all storage
          groups. The command to stop a storage group will cause the storage
          management cycle to not process that storage group.
          In the message text:
          strgrpnm  The storage group name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM Storage Management Component (OSMC) does not
          process the  storage group during the storage management cycle.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9078I OSMC stopping. Stop storage group command for strgrpnm not
          processed.
          Explanation:  Stop storage group command ignored because object
          access method (OAM) Storage Management Component (OSMC) is
          stopping.
          In the message text:
          strgrpnm  The storage group name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OSMC does not queue the stop storage group command.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9079I OSMC stopping. Start single object recovery command for
          collection collname, object objname not processed.
          Explanation:  Start single object recovery command ignored due to
          impending object access method (OAM) Storage Management Component
          (OSMC) stop.
          In the message text:
          collname  The collection name.
          objname   The object name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OSMC does not queue the single object recovery
          command.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9080I Single object recovery already processing collection collname,
          object objname.
          Explanation:  Single object recovery for given object already
          started and active.
          In the message text:
          collname  The collection name.
          objname   The object name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM Storage Management Component (OSMC) does not
          queue the single object recovery command.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9081I OSMC stopping. Display command will not be processed.
          Explanation:  Display command ignored due to impending object
          access method (OAM) Storage Management Component (OSMC) stop.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OSMC does not queue the display command.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9082I Resource resname not active. Display command not processed.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component (OSMC) processes display commands only for active
          resources.
          In the message text:
          resname   The resource name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OSMC does not queue the display command.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9083I CBRHSDSP unable to process display command.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component (OSMC) unable to queue the display command. Refer to the
          preceding messages for more information.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OSMC does not queue the display command.
          Application Programmer Response:  Notify the system programmer.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9084I Start single object recovery command for collection collname,
          object objname already on queue.
          Explanation:  A start single object recovery command for this
          object has previously been issued. The current command becomes
          redundant.
          In the message text:
          collname  The collection name.
          objname   The object name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM Storage Management Component (OSMC) does not
          queue the single object recovery command.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9085I CBRHSOBR unable to start single object recovery for collection
          collname, object objname.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component (OSMC) unable to queue the single object recovery
          command. Refer to the preceding messages for more information.
          In the message text:
          collname  The collection name.
          objname   The object name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OSMC does not queue the single object recovery
          command.
          Application Programmer Response:  Notify the system programmer.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9086I OSMC stopping. Start optical disk recovery command not processed.
          Explanation:  Start optical disk recovery command ignored due to
          impending object access method (OAM) Storage Management Component
          (OSMC) stop.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OSMC does not queue the optical disk recovery
          command.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9087I Start optical disk recovery command for volume volser / ovolser
          already on queue.
          Explanation:  A start optical disk recovery command for this volume
          has previously been issued. The current command becomes redundant.
          In the message text:
          volser    The volume serial number.
          ovolser   The volume serial number of the opposite side of the
                    disk.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM Storage Management Component (OSMC) does not
          queue the optical disk recovery command.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9088I OSMC stopping. Start DASD space management command for storage
          group strgrpnm not processed.
          Explanation:  Start DASD space management command ignored due to
          impending object access method (OAM) Storage Management Component
          (OSMC) stop.
          In the message text:
          strgrpnm  The storage group name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OSMC does not queue the DASD space management
          command.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9089I No storage groups defined in the active configuration.
          Explanation:  If no storage groups are defined, object access
          method (OAM) Storage Management Component (OSMC) will not process
          any operator commands for storage group actions, but will process
          other operator commands.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OSMC does not queue the operator command.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9090I Module modname was unable to obtain storage for CBRHSMSI dynamic
          area.
          Explanation:  The STORAGE macro failed.  This message is preceded
          by message CBR7004I which contains the return code from the STORAGE
          macro.
          In the message text:
          modname   The module name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM Storage Management Component processing stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Investigate the return code from the
          STORAGE macro and refer to the documentation for message CBR7004I.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9091I Module modname could not acquire SMS Storage Group Construct
          Definitions.  The SMS interface reason code is smsirscd.  The SMS
          interface function code is smsifc.  The error indicator code is rc.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component (OSMC) attempted to acquire SMS Construct Definition data
          for Storage Groups and was unable to do so.  OSMC will process the
          Storage Groups only by an operator request. The Storage Groups will
          not start automatically.
          In the message text:
          modname   The module name.
          smsirscd  The SMS interface reason code.
          smsifc    The SMS interface function code.
          rc        The error indicator code.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OSMC will continue processing.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Refer to previous error messages to
          determine why OSMC was unable to acquire the SMS Construct
          Definition data.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9092I The OAM Storage Management Component unable to automatically
          start the Storage Groups.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component (OSMC) is unable to start the Storage Groups
          automatically.  Refer to the previous message for more information.
          OSMC will continue to process Storage Groups by operator request.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OSMC will continue processing.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Examine previous error messages to
          determine why OSMC was unable to start the Storage Groups
          automatically.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9101I ctcname object service objsrvnm STORAGE failed for collection
          collname, object objname's read buffer.
          Explanation:  Control task object service had a STORAGE failure
          while trying to acquire a read buffer for this object.
          In the message text:
          ctcname   The CTC name.
          objsrvnm  The object service name.
          collname  The collection name.
          objname   The object name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM Storage Management Component (OSMC) stops
          processing this object.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9102I DB2 could not find the object for collection collname, object
          name objname in storage group strgrpnm under control task ctcname.
          Explanation:  The object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component (OSMC) DB2 object read service did not have an object
          (DB2 row) for the collection name of an object name.  The read
          service searched the object table indicated by the object's size in
          the collection, in the storage.  The object was selected for
          processing under the OSMC control task.
          In the message text:
          collname  The collection name.
          objname   The object name.
          strgrpnm  The storage group name.
          ctcname   The CTC name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OSMC stops processing this object.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9103I An error occurred during storage management processing for
          collection collection-name, object object-name.  The return code is
          rc and the reason code is reason-code.
          Explanation:  The error was detected during intentional processing
          in preparation for or processing of a read or write request.
          Retries were attempted and were also unsuccessful.  The error may
          be due to a problem with the confirmation data base, the operating
          environment, or with the optical library and media.  Additional
          information is provided for specific reason codes.  If a reason
          code appears in the message that is not listed below, please refer
          to the MVS/DFP Version 3 Release 2: Diagnosis Reference manual.
          In the message text:
          collname  The collection name.
          objname   The object name.
          rc        The return code.
          rsncde    The reason code.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM Storage Management Component stops except where
          otherwise noted.
          Operator Response:  Refer to the Operator Response in the following
          table for the specific return code and reason code contained in the
          message, and inspect other messages issued by OAM to aid in solving
          this problem.  If necessary, contact your system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Refer to the Programmer Response in
          the following table for the specific return code and reason code
          contained in the message, and inspect other messages issued by OAM
          to aid in solving this problem.  If necessary, contact your
          programming support personnel.
          The list of return codes and reason codes follows:
   Return  Reason Code/Description
   Code
   8             An error was encountered in performing a storage management
                 operation.  The probable cause of the error is an incomplete
                 or incorrect definition or modification of the configuration
                 data base.
           441   Volume serial number not defined in optical configuration
                 data base.
           450   Group name not defined in optical configuration data base.
           460   Request for library, but no libraries defined in optical
                 configuration data base.
           461   Library name not defined in optical configuration data base.
           471   BACKUP volume set not defined in optical configuration data
                 base.
                 Programmer Response:  Complete or correct the definition of
                 the table in the configuration data base as indicated by the
                 reason code.
   0C            An error was encountered in performing a storage management
                 operation.  The error code indicates a problem in the
                 operating environment.  This could be an error in system
                 set-up or hardware.
           800   OAM address space not available.
                 Operator Response:  Determine why the OAM address space is
                 not available to correct the problem that led to OAM address
                 space completion.  Restart OAM.
           900   No eligible optical drive is capable of processing this
                 request.
                 Operator Response:  Issue display commands to verify that
                 drives and libraries in the configuration are online and
                 operational.  Issue vary commands to vary drives and
                 libraries online, if necessary.
           910   Specific volume request for unreadable volume.
                 Programmer Response:  Make the volume readable by changing
                 the row in the volume table to make the READABLE column
                 value  Y.'
           920   Operator canceled volume mount request.
                 System Action:  Additional requests for this volume will be
                 bypassed if possible.  Requests that are not bypassed may
                 result in additional mount requests.  Processing continues
                 with requests for other volumes.
           A00   Permanent error on recording medium.
           A10   Permanent error on optical drive.
           A20   Permanent error on optical library.
           A21   Library request purged after failure of prior request on
                 same library.
                 Operator Response:  A permanent hardware error has occurred.
                 Notify IBM service personnel.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9104I Deadlock or time out occurred while selecting object name
          object-name in collection name collection-name in storage group
          storage-group data from object table.
          Explanation:  A DB2 deadlock occurred on the object table while
          object to be read was being selected from it.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  CBRHRDAS will try to read the object again.
          Operator Response:  None.
          Application Programmer Response:  None.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9105I Deadlocks are occurring on the DB2 object data table,
          object-table-name for storage group storage-group-name.
          Explanation:  Many DB2 deadlocks have occurred on the object table
          while object data was being selected from it.  Message CBR9104I
          precedes this message stating object name of object attempting to
          be read.  This object will not be processed at this time but will
          be selected during the next storage management cycle.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  Processing continues unless DB2 deadlocks become
          consistently excessive at which time completion CBR9914I and
          CBR9915I messages are issued.
          Operator Response:  Notify data base administrator.
          Application Programmer Response:  None.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9106I CBRHROPT has incurred an error from an optical read request while
          processing object object-name in collection name collection-name in
          storage group storage-group-name.  Error return code is
          return-code; reason code is reason-code.
          Explanation:  OSMC stops processing for this storage group with one
          exception.  If the reason code is 0440, this object is not
          processed; however, processing continues with other objects in the
          storage group.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  Refer to table of OAM Macro Return Codes and Reason
          Codes in this manual for definition of return code and reason code.
          Operator Response:  Contact your system programmer.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9120I ctcname modname DB2 COMMIT failed, permanent error.
          Explanation:  The control task object delete/directory update
          service module encountered a DB2 failure when updating the object
          directory for the named control task.
          In the message text:
          ctcname   The CTC name.
          modname   The module name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The control task abnormally ends.
          System Programmer Response:  Preceding messages will provide
          additional information about the DB2 failure.  In addition, other
          messages will be issued if other subtasks are affected by the
          failure.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9121I ctcname modname unexpected return code from DB2 COMMIT.
          Explanation:  The control task object delete/directory update
          service module encountered an unexpected return code from DB2 while
          updating the object directory for the named control task.
          In the message text:
          ctcname   The CTC name.
          modname   The module name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          Operator Response:  Monitor for additional messages.
          System Programmer Response:  Preceding messages will provide
          additional information about the DB2 failure.  In addition, other
          messages will be issued if other subtasks are affected by the
          failure.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9122I modname has experienced a DB2 deadlock or timeout.  Recovery
          started for updates to storage group strgrpnm.
          Explanation:  DB2 returned a SQL error code of -911 or -913 to
          object access method (OAM) Storage Management Component (OSMC)
          module which indicates that a timeout or deadlock has occurred. In
          the case of error code -911, any changes to the storage group by
          updates or deletes were lost by a DB2 ROLLBACK.  Recovery has
          started to redo the updates and deletes for this storage group
          since the last COMMIT.
          In the message text:
          modname   The module name.
          strgrpnm  The storage group name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system continues processing.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9123I modname: Retry/recovery processing failed for collection
          collname, object objname in storage group strgrpnm with SQL error
          code sqlerrcd.
          Explanation:  A module was performing recovery for object directory
          updates after a timeout or deadlock.  Retry for an object in the
          storage group failed and returned an SQL error code.  See message
          text for message CBR9122I for more information.
          In the message text:
          modname   The module name.
          collname  The collection name.
          objname   The object name.
          strgrpnm  The storage group name.
          sqlerrcd  The SQL error code.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system continues processing.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9124I modname: DB2 COMMIT failed for collection collname, object
          objname in storage group strgrpnm during recovery following a
          deadlock or timeout. SQL return code is sqlerrcd.
          Explanation:  See a preceding message regarding deadlock or timeout
          during process of the module.  Recovery is in progress for updates
          which were not recorded to the DB2 database as a result of the
          deadlock or timeout.  During the recovery, a DB2 COMMIT failed for
          the object in the storage group with an SQL error code.
          In the message text:
          modname   The module name.
          collname  The collection name.
          objname   The object name.
          strgrpnm  The storage group name.
          sqlerrcd  The SQL error code.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system continues processing.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9125I module-name-: Retry processing failed for collection
          collection-name, object object-name in storage group
          storage-group-name with SQL error code SQL-err-code.
          Explanation:  Module module-name was retrying processing for the
          specified object after a timeout or deadlock.  Retry for object
          object-name in storage group storage-group failed after ten
          attempts and returned a SQL error code of Sql-err-code.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  Processing continues.
          Operator Response:  None.
          Application Programmer Response:  None.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9130I cntl modname attempted to update collection collname, object
          objname in storage group strgrpnm which had been deleted.
          Explanation:  An object was deleted by OSR or another object access
          method (OAM) Storage Management Component (OSMC) process between
          the time the OSMC control task selected it for processing and the
          processing was completed.
          In the message text:
          cntl      The control task name.
          modname   The module name.
          collname  The collection name.
          objname   The object name.
          strgrpnm  The storage group name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OSMC processing continues after ensuring all
          reusable space associated with the object is freed.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9131I cntl modname attempted to update collection collname, object
          objname in storage group strgrpnm.  The directory entry for the
          object was already changed.
          Explanation:  An object was changed by OSR or another object access
          method (OAM) Storage Management Component (OSMC) process between
          the time the OSMC control task selected it for processing and the
          processing was completed.  This object was not updated in this
          cycle.  The change to the object causes its pending action date to
          be set to the next cycle day.
          In the message text:
          cntl      The control task name.
          modname   The module name.
          collname  The collection name.
          objname   The object name.
          strgrpnm  The storage group name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OSMC processing continues.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9150I OAM update error in module modname for optical volume volser.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component (OSMC) attempted to update the expiration date or library
          eject date for an optical volume under an OAM module and failed.
          In the message text:
          modname   The module name.
          volser    The volume serial number.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OSMC processing continues.
          System Programmer Response:  For information on SQL error codes see
          IBM Database 2 V2 Messages and Codes.  reason for the error.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9200I Object Processing starting for storage group strgrpnm.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component (OSMC) Object Processing is starting for the storage
          group specified.  Object Processing selects objects if their
          pending action dates are equal to or earlier than the date of
          processing.  It then schedules and initiates processing of the
          objects.
          In the message text:
          strgrpnm  The storage group name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system begins processing.
          Operator Response:  Notify the storage administrator.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9201I Object Processing completed for storage group strgrpnm.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component (OSMC) Object Processing has completed the storage
          management cycle for this storage group.
          In the message text:
          strgrpnm  The storage group name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OSMC completes storage group processing.
          Operator Response:  Notify the storage administrator.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9222I Object Processing failed during initialization for storage group
          strgrpnm.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component (OSMC) Object Processing attempted to perform
          initialization functions in preparation to process the storage
          group, but failed to complete initialization.  Initialization
          functions include acquiring storage for parameter areas for DB2 and
          the Auto Delete Installation Exit.
          In the message text:
          strgrpnm  The storage group name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OSMC will not process this storage group.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Refer to previous error messages to
          determine why initialization failed.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9224I Object Processing found a directory entry without an associated
          object for object objname in collection collname in storage group
          strgrpnm.
          Explanation:  This object has an entry in the object access method
          (OAM) Storage Management Component (OSMC) DB2 Object Directory but
          there is no object location associated with the entry.
          In the message text:
          strgrpnm  The storage group name.
          collname  The collection name.
          objname   The object name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OSMC stops processing this object.
          Operator Response:  Refer to previous error messages to identify
          why the object is missing.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9230I Object Processing could not acquire SMS Management Class or
          Storage Class Construct Definitions.  The SMS interface reason code
          is smsirscd.  The SMS interface function code is smsifc.  The error
          indicator code is rc.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component (OSMC) Object Processing attempted to acquire SMS
          Construct Definition data for Management Class and Storage Class
          and was unable to do so.
          In the message text:
          smsircd   The SMS interface reason code.
          smsifc    The SMS interface function code.
          rc        The error indicator code.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OSMC will stop this storage group.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Refer to OSR Reason Codes in the
          Object Access Method Application Programmer's Reference.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9231I Object Processing requires more available DASD for moving objects
          from optical to DASD.  Objects in storage group strgrpnm need to be
          moved to DASD from optical media.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component (OSMC) Object Processing attempted to move objects in the
          storage group from optical to DASD and was unable to do so because
          of insufficient available DASD.
          In the message text:
          strgrpnm  The storage group name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OSMC will stop processing of this storage group.
          Some objects in the storage group may have been moved to DASD
          before the out of space condition was detected.
          Operator Response:  Notify the database administrator.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9232I Object Processing did not find the object's Storage Class and/or
          Management Class name(s) in the DB2 Object Administration Database.
          Class transition, backup processing, and expiration are not
          possible for object object-name in collection collection-name,
          storage group storage-group-name.  The SQL return code is
          sql-return-code.
          Explanation:  OSMC Object Processing uses the Storage Class and
          Management Class identifiers found in the DB2 Object Directory
          Table for the object and attempts to match them to entries in the
          DB2 Storage Class and Management Class Identifier Tables.  The
          match did not occur, name(s) of the Storage Class and/or Management
          Class remain(s) unknown; therefore, class transition, backup and
          expiration functions cannot be performed for the object.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OSMC will not process this object during this
          processing of the storage group.  The object will be selected for
          processing again during the next storage management cycle for this
          storage group.
          Operator Response:  Notify the storage administrator.
          System Programmer Response:  For information on SQL error reason
          codes, see IBM DATABASE 2 Version 2 Messages and Codes.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9233I Object Processing does not recognize the object's Management
          Class name, mgtnme in the SMS Construct Definitions data.  Class
          transition, backup processing, and expiration processing are not
          possible for object objname in collection collname in storage group
          storgrp.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component (OSMC) Object Processing attempts to match the object's
          management class name to the SMS Construct Definitions data to find
          the correct management class information for processing the object.
          The management class name was not found in the SMS Construct
          Definitions in the active control dataset.
          In the message text:
          mgtnme    The management class name.
          objname   The object name.
          collname  The collection name.
          storgrp   The storage group.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OSMC will not process this object during this
          storage management cycle of the storage group.  The object will be
          selected for processing again during the next storage management
          cycle of this storage group.
          Operator Response:  Notify the storage administrator.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9234I Object Processing does not recognize the object's Storage Class
          name, strname in the SMS Construct Definitions data.  Class
          transition, backup processing, and expiration processing are not
          possible for object objname in collection collname in storage group
          storgrp.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component (OSMC) Object Processing attempts to match the object's
          storage class name to the SMS Construct Definitions data to find
          the correct storage class information for processing the object.
          The storage class name was not found in the SMS Construct
          Definitions in the active control data set.
          In the message text:
          strname   The storage class name.
          objname   The object name.
          collname  The collection name.
          storgrp   The storage group.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OSMC will not process this object during this
          storage management cycle of this storage group.  The object will be
          selected for processing again during the next storage management
          cycle of this storage group.
          Operator Response:  Notify the storage administrator.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9235I Object Processing could not determine the format of the
          expiration data from the management class definition for management
          class.  The management class definition did not specify if the
          expiration after object creation format was a date or number of
          days.
          Explanation:  The management class definition should specify the
          format of the expiration data.  The expiration data can be in the
          form of days since object creation, or a date since object
          creation.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OSMC will not process this object during this
          processing cycle of the storage group.  The object will be selected
          for processing again during the next storage management cycle for
          this storage group.
          Operator Response:  Notify the storage administrator.
          Application Programmer Response:  None.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9236I Object Processing class transition failed for object objname in
          collection collname in storage group storgrp whose management class
          name is mgtname and whose storage class name is strname.  The SMS
          interface reason code is smsirscd.  The SMS interface function code
          is smsifc.  The error indicator code is rc.
          Explanation:  Object Processing attempted to invoke class
          transition functions for this object.  Class transition functions
          failed.
          In the message text:
          objname   The object name.
          collname  The collection name.
          storgrp   The storage group.
          strname   The storage class name.
          mgtname   The management class name.
          smsircd   The SMS interface reason code.
          smsifc    The SMS interface function code.
          rc        The error indicator code.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM Storage Management Component (OSMC) will not
          process this object during this storage management cycle of this
          storage group.  The object will be selected for processing again
          during the next storage management cycle of this storage group.
          Operator Response:  Notify the storage administrator.
          System Programmer Response:  Refer to OSR Reason Codes in the
          Object Access Method Application Programmer's Reference.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9237I Object Processing detected a change to the management class
          definition because the definition was last used for the transition
          of the object.  Object objname in collection collname in storage
          group storgrp is being transitioned without regard to its previous
          transition history.
          Explanation:  The management class construct definition can have
          been changed, or the management class data stored with the object
          in the object's directory entry was changed explicitly.  Either
          type of change can have occurred since the object was last
          transitioned; therefore, the date of the object's last transition
          can not be relevant in determining whether or not to transition it
          during the current storage management cycle.
          In the message text:
          objname   The object name.
          collname  The collection name.
          storgrp   The storage group.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM Storage Management Component (OSMC) will
          continue to process this object during this storage management
          cycle and will do so strictly according to the new management class
          definition.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9239I Object Processing could not determine the type of periodic class
          transition processing to be performed according to management class
          management class name .  It should be a periodic transition based
          on one of the following, monthly, quarterly, or yearly.
          Explanation:  The management class definition did not specify the
          type of periodic transition processing to be performed for the
          object.  The type of processing should be one of the following
          monthly, quarterly, or yearly.  As a result, the object will not be
          processed.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OSMC will not process this object during this
          processing cycle of the storage group.  The object will be selected
          for processing again during the next storage management cycle for
          this storage group.
          Operator Response:  Notify the storage administrator.
          Application Programmer Response:  None.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9241I Object Processing could not locate the optical copy of the object
          while performing class transition processing.  The object is
          objname  in collection collname in storage group strgrpnm.
          Explanation:  While performing class transition processing, object
          access method (OAM) Storage Management Component (OSMC) could not
          locate the optical copy of the object.
          In the message text:
          objname   The object name.
          collname  The collection name.
          strgrpnm  The storage group name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OSMC will not process this object during this
          processing cycle of the storage group.  The object will be selected
          for processing again during the next processing cycle of the
          storage group.
          Operator Response:  Notify the storage administrator.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9242I Object Processing could not determine how to set the DB2 index

          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) Storage Management

          In the message text:
          objname   The object name.
          collname  The collection name.

          Operator Response:  Notify the storage administrator.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9253I A DB2 operation requested by OSMC Object Processing modname
          failed with return code, RC = rc.  This message is preceded by
          message CBR9700I and message CBR9706I.  Error detected while
          fetching collection collname from the DB2 table of collection
          names, colltab, for storage group storgrp.
          Explanation:  An error occurred while fetching DB2 collection names
          from the collection name table for this storage group.
          In the message text:
          modname   The module name.
          rc        The return code.
          collname  The collection name.
          colltab   The collection table.
          storgrp   The storage group.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM Storage Management Component (OSMC) processing

          Operator Response:  Notify database administrator.
          System Programmer Response:  Determine why DB2 failed during the
          collection name fetch.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9300I DASD Space Management starting for storage group storgrp.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component (OSMC) DASD Space Management is starting for the storage
          group.  DASD Space Management selects objects if their pending
          action dates are equal to or earlier than the date of processing.
          It then schedules and initiates processing of the objects.  It will
          expire objects today which are expiring today, or have been
          scheduled for expiration in the past, but have not yet been
          expired.
          In the message text:
          storgrp   The storage group.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system begins processing.
          Operator Response:  Notify the storage administrator.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9301I DASD Space Management completed for storage group storgrp.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component (OSMC) DASD Space Management has completed for the
          storage group.  DASD Space Management selects objects if their
          pending action dates are equal to or earlier than the date of
          processing.  It then schedules and initiates expiration of the
          appropriate objects.
          In the message text:
          storgrp   The storage group.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OSMC completes storage group DASD space management.
          Operator Response:  Notify the storage administrator.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9322I DASD Space Management failed during initialization for storage
          group storgrp.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) Storage Management

          System Action:  OSMC will not process this storage group.

   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9330I DASD Space Management could not acquire SMS Management Class or

          Construct Definition data for Management Class and Storage Class

          In the message text:
          smsircd   The SMS interface reason code.

          for more information.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9332I DASD Space Management did not find the object's Storage Class
          and/or Management Class name(s) in the DB2 Object Administration
          Database.  Expiration processing is not possible for storage group,
          collection collection name, object object name.  SQL error code is
          sql-error-code.
          Explanation:  OSMC DASD Space Management uses the Storage Class and
          Management Class identifiers found in the DB2 Object Directory
          Table for the object and attempts to match them to entries in the
          DB2 Storage Class and Management Class Identifier Tables.  The
          match did not occur, name(s) of the Storage Class and/or Management
          Class remain(s) unknown; therefore, expiration functions cannot be
          performed for the object.

          processing of the storage group.  The object will be selected for
          processing again during the next DASD space management or storage
          management cycle for this storage group.
          Operator Response:  Notify the storage administrator.
          System Programmer Response:  For information on SQL error reason
          codes, see IBM DATABASE 2 Version 2 Messages and Codes.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9333I DASD Space Management does not recognize the object's Management

   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9334I DASD Space Management does not recognize the object's Storage

   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9335I DASD Space Management could not determine the format of the
          expiration data from the management class definition for management

          expiration after object creation format was a date or number of
          days.
          Explanation:  The management class definition should specify the
