```text
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9350I OSMC Summary Status:
            TASK       TASK    TASK    START      OBJECTS
            NAME       TYPE    STAT    TIME       COMPLETED
            tskname    tsktype tskstat starttime  objcomplete
            OBJECTS
            ACTIVE
            objective
            End of Display Summary
          Explanation:  Summary status information is provided for many of
          the processes performed by object access method (OAM) Storage
          Management Component (OSMC).  The summary information includes the
          name of the task, type of task, a task status of active,
          terminating, or stopping, the time the task was started, how many
          objects were processed, and how many objects are still being
          actively processed.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system continues processing.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9355I No OSMC processes are active at this time.
          Explanation:  The display object access method (OAM) Storage
          Management Component (OSMC) summary status information command was
          issued but no OSMC processes were either active or stopping at the
          time the command was issued.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system continues processing.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9361I Deadlock or time out occurred while selecting collection name
          collname from the storgrp storage group collection name table.
          Explanation:  A DB2 deadlock occurred on the collection names table
          while collection names were being selected from it.  This was
          probably caused by updates being made to the table while collection
          names were being selected.
          In the message text:
          storgrp   The storage group.
          collname  The collection name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The collection names table will be closed,
          re-opened, and the collection names will be selected again.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9362I Deadlocks are occurring on the DB2 collection name table, ctcgrp
          for storage group storgrp.
          Explanation:  Many DB2 deadlocks have occurred on the collection
          name table while collection names were being selected from it.
          This is probably being caused by updates being made to the table
          while collection names are being selected from it.
          In the message text:
          ctcgrp    The collection name table.
          storgrp   The storage group.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The task will stop processing.
          System Programmer Response:  Ensure updates to the collection names
          table are not occurring while OSMC is processing.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9363I A DB2 operation requested by OSMC DASD space management modname
          failed with return code, RC = rc.  This message is preceded by
          message CBR9700I and message CBR9706I.  Error detected while
          fetching collection collname from the DB2 table of collection
          names, colltab, for storage group storgrp.
          Explanation:  An error occurred while fetching DB2 collection names
          from the collection name table for this storage group.
          In the message text:
          modname   The module name.
          rc        The return code.
          collname  The collection name.
          colltab   The collection table.
          storgrp   The storage group.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM Storage Management Component (OSMC) processing
          stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify database administrator.
          System Programmer Response:  Determine why DB2 failed during the
          collection name fetch.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9370I OSMC Detail for taskname:
                        READ  READ  WRITE  WRITE  WRITE  EXPIR  DIR
                        DASD  OPT   DASD   BACKUP  OPT          UPDTS
            WORKQ OBJ:   nn   nn     nn     nn     nn     nn     nn
            WAITQ OBJ:        nn            nn     nn            nn
            COMPLETED:   nn   nn     nn     nn     nn     nn     nn
            End of Display Detail
          Explanation:  Detail status information is provided for the object
          access method (OAM) Storage Management Component (OSMC) task
          specified in the display command.  The number of object-functions
          queued on the work and wait queues and the number of
          object-functions completed for each of the OSMC services is
          displayed.  The number of object-functions does not exactly equate
          to the number of objects processed.
          In the message text:
          taskname  The name of the OSMC task.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system continues processing.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9400I Library Space Manager starting for library libname.
          Explanation:  The Library Space Manager has begun processing.
          In the message text:
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system continues processing.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9401I Library Space Manager completed for library libname.  nnn optical
          disks scheduled for eject.
          Explanation:  The Library Space Manager has completed processing.
          In the message text:
          libname   The library name.
          nnn       The number of optical disks scheduled for eject.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system continues processing.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9402I Could not locate an optical disk for ejection in library libname.
          Explanation:  The library does not hold any optical disk which the
          Library Space Manager could eject.  The library can be empty or
          off-line.
          In the message text:
          libname   The library name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system continues processing.
          Operator Response:  Notify system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Check the library.  If the library is
          on-line and not empty, contact the service representative.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9403I Eject operation called by modname unsuccessful. Library libname
          cannot eject volume volser. Further space management requests for
          this library cannot be processed.
          Explanation:  The Eject operation called by the module returned a
          return code of X'04'.  The library in which the specified volume
          resides is not currently capable of ejecting an optical disk.  The
          library is offline or not operational, or the library input/output
          station is not operational.
          In the message text:
          libname   The library name.
          modname   The name of the module.
          volser    The volume serial number.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system continues processing.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9404I Eject operation called by modname unsuccessful. Volume volser or
          opposite side is busy.
          Explanation:  The Eject operation called by the module returned a
          return code of X'08'.  The specified volume, or its opposite side
          volume, is busy and therefore not available to be ejected from the
          library where it currently resides.  A volume is busy when it is
          mounted, when a mount is pending, or when a pending unit or work
          has specifically requested it.
c.cc 6
          In the message text:
          modname   The name of the module.
          volser    The volume serial number.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system continues processing.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9405I Eject operation called by modname failed. Further space
          management requests cannot be processed.
          Explanation:  The Eject operation called by the module returned a
          return code of X'10'.
          In the message text:
          modname   The name of the module.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system continues processing.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  This message preceded by message
          CBR2610I. Refer to that message for further explanation.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9500I Shelf Manager starting for storage group storgrp.
          Explanation:  The Shelf Manager of the object access method (OAM)
          Storage Management Component has begun processing.
          In the message text:
          storgrp   The storage group.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system continues processing.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9501I Shelf Manager completed for storage group storgrp.  nnn optical
          disks selected.
          Explanation:  The Shelf Manager of the object access method (OAM)
          Storage Management Component has completed processing.
          In the message text:
          storgrp   The storage group.
          nnn       The number of optical disks selected.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system continues processing.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9502I Expiration messages were written to the Disk Librarian.
          Explanation:  The Shelf Manager of the object access method (OAM)
          Storage Management Component has processed a list of optical disks
          that can be discarded from their shelf locations.  The list is
          written to the Disk Librarian.  The storage administrator should
          decide which optical disks should be removed from their shelf
          location.  A CBR9503I message is written for every volume serial
          number that has expired.  The messages will be routed to the log
          file.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system continues processing.
          Operator Response:  Notify the storage administrator.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9503I Volume serial number volser in location loc has expired.
          Explanation:  This particular volume serial number has expired.
          The disk librarian should make a decision on what to do with the
          expired optical disks.
          In the message text:
          volser    The volume serial number.
          loc       The location.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system continues processing.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9700I There was an error in the execution of a DB2 operation.  The
          error code from DB2 is: SQL sqlerrcd.
          Explanation:  An error occurred when accessing DB2.  This message
          is issued immediately before message CBR9701I, CBR9704I or CBR9705I
          which lists the transaction that failed.
          In the message text:
          sqlerrcd  The SQL error code.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system issues message CBR9701I, CBR9704I, or
          CBR9705I.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Interpret the codes presented in this
          message.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9701I There was an error operation a row in the OAM Database
          database-name.  Collection name is collection-name and object name
          is object-name in Storage Group storage-group in table-name in
          MODULE module-name.
          Explanation:  An error occurred when accessing DB2.  The message
          identifies the operation (fetching, updating, or deleting) that was
          requested and the module that called DB2.  The collection-name and
          the object-name indicates the failing row for updating or deleting.
          The collection-name and the object-name is null for errors while
          fetching an object.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  Return to calling module.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Determine why DB2 failed on that row.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9702I Task task processing for Storage Group storgrp terminating
          because of DB2 error.
          Explanation:  An error occurred when accessing DB2.  The task
          processing for the storage group that called DB2 will be stopped to
          avoid integrity problems.
          In the message text:
          task      The task processed for the storage group.
          storgrp   The storage group.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9703I There was an error accessing the Optical Configuration Data Base
          while processing Storage Group storage-group-name.  The error code
          from DB2 is: SQL SQL-error-code.
          Explanation:  An error occurred during Shelf Management processing
          when accessing the Volume Table in the Optical Configuration Data
          Base.  The message lists the SQL codes which existed at the time of
          the failure.
          In the message text:
          storage-group-name
                    The name of the storage group.
          SQL-error-code
                    The specified error code.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  None.
          Operator Response:   Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:   See IBM DATABASE 2 Version 2 Messages
          and Codes for more information.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9704I There was an error xxxx a cursor in the OAM Data Base dbname for
          storage Group storgrp in tabname in MODULE modname.
          Explanation:  An error occurred when accessing DB2 while doing an
          operation on a cursor.
          In the message text:
          xxxx      The operation (opening or closing) that was requested.
          dbname    The name of the object access method (OAM) Database.
          storgrp   The storage group.
          tabname   The table name.
          modname   The name of the module.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Determine why DB2 failed on opening or
          closing the cursor.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9705I There was an error xxxx data in the OAM Data Base dbname for
          Storage Group storgrp in MODULE modname.
          Explanation:  An error occurred when accessing DB2.
          In the message text:
          xxxx      The operation (committing or rolling back) that was
                    requested.
          dbname    The name of the object access method (OAM) Database.
          storgrp   The storage group.
          modname   The name of the module.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  The SQL code identifying the failure
          is given in preceding message CBR9700I.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9800I Optical Disk Recovery starting for volumes volser1 and volser2.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component (OSMC) has started Optical Disk Recovery.  The Optical
          Disk Recovery Utility has begun processing.
          In the message text:
          volser1   The volume serial number.
          volser2   The volume serial number of the opposite side of the
                    disk.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system continues processing.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9803I Volume Type is not valid.
          Explanation:  The volume type recorded in the volume control block
          is neither BACKUP nor GROUP.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system stops processing.
          Operator Response:  Notify the storage administrator.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9805I modname attempted to allocate additional storage and failed.
          Explanation:  The STORAGE macro attempted to obtain storage for an
          internal data area and failed.
          In the message text:
          modname   The name of the module.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  Recovery continues for objects already in process.
          However, recovery will not be attempted for additional objects.
          Operator Response:  Monitor the progress of the recovery task.
          When it ends, restart it.
          Application Programmer Response:  None.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9806I modname detected an error in a DB2 FETCH parameter list.
          Explanation:  Probable programming error.
          In the message text:
          modname   The name of the module.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system ends processing.
          System Programmer Response:  Notify the service representative.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9807I modname detected an SQL error on a DB2 FETCH request.
          Explanation:  Probable programming error.
          In the message text:
          modname   The name of the module.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system ends processing.
          System Programmer Response:  Notify the service representative.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9808I modname detected an error in a DB2 OPEN parameter list.
          Explanation:  Probable programming error.
          In the message text:
          modname   The name of the module.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system ends processing.
          System Programmer Response:  Notify the service representative.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9809I modname detected an SQL error on a DB2 OPEN request.
          Explanation:  Probable programming error.
          In the message text:
          modname   The name of the module.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system ends processing.
          System Programmer Response:  Notify the service representative.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9811I No Storage Group was found.
          Explanation:  Probable incomplete definition of the Configuration
          Database.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the storage administrator.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9812I modname was unable to get storage for a pointer block.
          Explanation:  A STORAGE command failed.
          In the message text:
          modname   The name of the module.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  Recovery continues for objects already in process.
          However, recovery will not be attempted for additional objects.
          Operator Response:  Monitor the progress of the recovery task.
          When it ends, restart it.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9813I There are more than 70,144 objects to be recovered.
          Explanation:  The Optical Disk Recovery Utility has a capacity of
          70,144 objects.  This limit has been reached.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  Recovery continues for objects already in process.
          When processing of these objects is completed, the utility will
          automatically restart to recover the remaining objects.
          Operator Response:  None.
          Application Programmer Response:  None.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9814I modname was unable to get working storage.
          Explanation:  A STORAGE command failed.
          In the message text:
          modname   The name of the module.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  No recovery processing can take place.
          Operator Response:  Monitor the progress of the recovery task. When
          the recovery task ends, restart it.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9815I modname detected an error in a DB2 CLOSE parameter list.
          Explanation:  Probable programming error.
          In the message text:
          modname   The name of the module.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system stops processing.
          System Programmer Response:  Notify the service representative.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9816I modname detected an SQL error on a DB2 CLOSE request.
          Explanation:  Probable programming error.
          In the message text:
          modname   The name of the module.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system stops processing.
          System Programmer Response:  Notify the service representative.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9817I modname was unable to get storage for a process control block.
          Explanation:  A STORAGE command failed.
          In the message text:
          modname   The name of the module.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  Recovery continues for objects already in process.
          However, recovery will not be attempted for additional objects.
          Operator Response:  Monitor the progress of the recovery task.
          When the recovery task ends, restart it.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9820D Reply 'QUIT' to terminate or 'GO' to proceed with recovery.
          Explanation:  A list of volumes has been identified, and listed in
          message CBR9824.  If the volumes are available, recovery can
          proceed.  If the volumes are not available, recovery can be
          abnormally ended and started again when the volumes have been
          retrieved.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system waits for an operator response.
          Operator Response:  Respond to the message with 'GO' or 'QUIT'.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9821I Optical Disk Recovery status, nnn objects selected for recovery.
          Explanation:  The Optical Disk Recovery Utility has completed a
          processing cycle.  The status can be either ENDING or RESTARTING.
          ENDING means that the process is complete for the requested disk.
          RESTARTING means that the capacity of the utility was exceeded, and
          the utility is being restarted to recover the remaining objects.
          In the message text:
          status    The status of the object.
          nnn       The number of objects selected for recovery.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  Processing proceeds as stated in the message.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9824A Optical Disk Recovery.  The following OPTICAL volumes are needed
          for recovery:
           volsr1 volsr2 volsr3 volsr4 volsr5 volsr6 volsr7 volsr8 volsr9
          Explanation:  A list of OPTICAL volumes has been identified, and is
          provided in this message.  If the volumes are available, recovery
          can proceed, so respond GO to message CBR9820D when it is issued.
          If the volumes are not available, recovery can be abnormally ended
          and started again when the volumes have been retrieved, so respond
          QUIT to message CBR9820D when it is issued.  If some of the volumes
          are available and others are not, recovery will be performed for
          objects from the volumes that are available.
          In the message text:
          volsr1 volsr2 volsr3 volsr4 volsr5 volsr6 volsr7 volsr8 volsr9
                    A list of optical volume serial numbers.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system continues processing.
          Operator Response:  Respond to message CBR9820D when it is issued.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9830I Single Object Recovery complete for collection collname, object
          objname.
          Explanation:  A Single Object Recovery command was issued and is
          complete.  Previous messages would describe any error conditions
          that can have been detected in processing the command.
          In the message text:
          collname  The collection name.
          objname   The object name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system continues processing.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9832I Optical Disk Recovery could not acquire SMS Storage Group
          Construct Definitions. The SMS Reason Code is smsrsncd.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component (OSMC) Optical Disk Recovery attempted to acquire SMS
          Construct Definition data for storage groups and was unable to do
          so.
          In the message text:
          smsrsncd  The SMS reason code.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OSMC Optical Disk Recovery will be stopped.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Examine previous error messages to
          determine why Optical Disk Recovery was unable to acquire the SMS
          Construct Definition data.  Refer to the DFP Diagnosis Reference
          for more information.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9833I Backup copy does not exist for collection collname, object
          objname.
          Explanation:  An operator command has been issued to recover a
          single object, however no backup copy exists.
          In the message text:
          collname  The collection name.
          objname   The object name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system stops processing.
          Operator Response:  Notify the storage administrator.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9834I Collection collname, object objname, not found.
          Explanation:  An operator command was issued to recover a single
          object; however, an object with the name specified could not be
          found.
          In the message text:
          collname  The collection name.
          objname   The object name.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system stops processing.
          Operator Response:  Check the spelling of both the collection name
          and the object name and reissue the operator command, if necessary.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9835I modname detected an error in a DB2 SELECT parameter list.
          Explanation:  Probable programming error.
          In the message text:
          modname   The name of the module.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system stops processing.
          System Programmer Response:  Notify the service representative.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9836I modname detected an error on a DB2 SELECT request.
          Explanation:  Probable programming error.
          In the message text:
          modname   The name of the module.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system stops processing.
          System Programmer Response:  Notify the service representative.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9838I Single Object Recovery received an invalid request.
          Explanation:  The Single Object Recovery Utility detected an error
          in a recovery request. No recovery processing can take place.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system stops processing.
          Operator Response:  Notify the service representative.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9839I Single Object Recovery could not acquire a SMS Storage Group
          Construct Definition. The SMS Reason Code is smsrsncd.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component (OSMC) Single Object Recovery attempted to acquire SMS
          Construct Definition data for a Storage Group and was unable to do
          so.
          In the message text:
          smsrsncd  The SMS reason code.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OSMC will not process this object.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Examine previous error messages to
          determine why Single Object Recovery was unable to acquire the SMS
          Construct Definition data.  Refer to the DFP Diagnosis Reference
          for more information.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9840I modname was unable to get working storage.
          Explanation:  A STORAGE command failed.
          In the message text:
          modname   The name of the module.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  Processing for the request is stopped.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9841I modname was unable to get storage for a process control block.
          Explanation:  A STORAGE command failed.
          In the message text:
          modname   The name of the module.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  Processing for the request is stopped.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9901I STORAGE failed in module modname for cntlblk.
          Explanation:  The STORAGE macro failed while object access method
          (OAM) Storage Management Component was attempting to obtain storage
          for the control block.  This message is preceded by message
          CBR7004I which contains the return code from the STORAGE macro.
          In the message text:
          modname   The name of the module that issued the STORAGE macro.
          cntlblk   The control block.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM Storage Management Component processing stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Investigate the return code from the
          STORAGE macro and refer to the documentation for message CBR7004I.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9902I STORAGE RELEASE error in module modname for cntlblk.
          Explanation:  The STORAGE RELEASE macro failed while object access
          method (OAM) Storage Management Component was attempting to free
          storage for the control block.  This message is preceded by message
          CBR7005I which contains the return code from the STORAGE RELEASE
          macro.
          In the message text:
          modname   The name of the module that issued the STORAGE macro.
          cntlblk   The control block.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM Storage Management Component processing stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Investigate the return code from the
          STORAGE RELEASE macro and refer to the documentation for message
          CBR7005I.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9905I CBRHINIT unable to LOAD module modname.
          Explanation:  An error occurred during processing of a LOAD macro.
          The error routine specified on the LOAD macro was given control,
          that indicates an error condition that would have caused the task
          to end abnormally was detected.
          In the message text:
          modname   The name of the module.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM Storage Management Component processing stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For additional information, see the
          LOAD macro.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9906I DELETE error in module modname, RC = rc, ENTRY = entrynme.
          Explanation:  An error occurred during processing of a DELETE
          macro.
          In the message text:
          modname   The name of the module that issued the DELETE macro.
          rc        The return code.
          entryname .*entrynme?
                    The name of the entry of the DELETE macro.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM Storage Management Component processing
          continues.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For additional information, see the
          DELETE macro.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9907I An STIMER macro failed in module modname, RC = rc.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component issued an STIMER macro that failed.
          In the message text:
          modname   The name of the module.
          rc        The return code.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM Storage Management Component processing stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Investigate the return code from the
          STIMER macro.  For additional information on the return codes, see
          the STIMER macro.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9909I An IDENTIFY macro failed in module modname for entry   entrynme.
          Explanation:  Object access method (OAM) Storage Management
          Component issued an IDENTIFY macro that failed.  This message is
          preceded by message CBR7018I.
          In the message text:
          modname   The name of the module.
          entrynme  The name of the entry.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM Storage Management Component Processing stops.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer
          System Programmer Response:  Investigate the return code from the
          IDENTIFY macro and refer to the documentation of message CBR7018I.
          For additional information on the return codes, see the IDENTIFY
          macro.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9910I ESTAE error in module modname, rc = rc.
          Explanation:  An error occurred during processing of an ESTAE
          macro.
          In the message text:
          modname   The name of the module that issued the ESTAE macro.
          rc        The return code.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM Storage Management Component processing
          continues.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For additional information on return
          codes, see the ESTAE macro.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9911I STIMERM SET error in module modname , RC = rc.
          Explanation:  An error occurred during processing of an STIMERM SET
          macro.  An error routine was given control following processing of
          an STIMERM SET macro indicating the STIMERM SET function could not
          be performed.
          In the message text:
          modname   The name of the module that issued the STIMERM SET macro.
          rc        The return code.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM Storage management component continues
          processing.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For additional information on return
          codes, see the STIMERM SET macro.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9912I TIME error in module modname, RC = rc.
          Explanation:  An error occurred during processing of a TIME macro.
          An error routine was given control following processing of a TIME
          macro indicating the TIME function could not be performed because
          of damaged clocks.
c.cc 6
          In the message text:
          modname   The name of the module in which the TIME error occurred.
          rc        The return code.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM Storage Management Component continues
          processing.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  For additional information on return
          codes, see the TIME macro.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9913I CAF open failed in module modname for control task ctcname.
          Return code rc.
          Explanation:  CBRKCAF returned a nonzero return code.
          In the message text:
          modname   The name of the module.
          ctcname   The name of the control task.
          rc        The return code.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system stops processing.
          System Programmer Response:  Notify the service representative.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9914I A DB2 operation for ctcname module modname failed.
          Explanation:  A DB2 operation requested by object access method
          (OAM) Storage Management Component (OSMC) processor for the control
          task or service routine module failed.  This message is preceded by
          by message CBR9700I and either message CBR9701I or message
          CBR9704I.
          In the message text:
          modname   The name of the module.
          ctcname   The name of the control task.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OSMC stops processing for this object.
          Operator Response:  Notify database administrator.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9915I Module modname is stopping OSMC control task ctcname because of
          repeating error condition msgid.
          Explanation:  ctcname module module-name stops processing when a
          specific error condition message-id occurs multiple times.  The
          failures can be either consecutive or cumulative depending of the
          error type.
          In the message text:
          modname   The name of the module.
          ctcname   The name of the control task.
          msgid     The message identifier.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OSMC stops all processing for this control task
          immediately.
          Operator Response:  Examine previous error messages with message
          number message-id to determine the reason that processing stopped.
          Application Programmer Response:  None.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9916I Automatic Deletion Installation Exit sent an invalid return code.
          Collection collname object objname in storage group strgrpnm was
          not deleted.  The invalid return code was rc.
          Explanation:  The Automatic Deletion Installation EXIT sent an
          incorrect return code.  No further deletions will be allowed for
          this storage group.
          In the message text:
          collname  The collection name.
          objname   The object name.
          strgrpnm  The storage group name.
          rc        The return code.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  The system continues processing.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
          System Programmer Response:  Correct the Automatic Deletion User
          Exit.  The next start of OAM will load the corrected version of the
          Automatic Deletion User Exit.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9917I ctcname modname optical volume data request failed for volume
          volser.  Return code is rc.
          Explanation:  The control task module tried to get data about an
          optical volume from object access method (OAM) and failed.  The
          return code identifies the failure.
          In the message text:
          ctcname   The name of the control task.
          modname   The name of the module.
          volser    The volume serial number.
          rc        The return code is as follows:
          Return
          Code
          (hex)          Explanation
          1              The volume was not found in the object access method
                         (OAM) Optical Volume Configuration Table.
          2              The volume was found in the OAM Optical Volume
                         Configuration Table, but some or all of the data was
                         in error.
          3              The STORAGE for the internal copy of the the OAM
                         Optical Volume Configuration Table entry for the
                         volume failed.
          4              DB2 failed while trying to get the OAM Optical
                         Volume Configuration Table entry for the volume.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  OAM Storage Management Component (OSMC) control
          task stops when DB2 failed.  Otherwise, OSMC abends with completion
          code X'16' and reason code X'5FFF'.
          Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
   __________________________________________________________________________
 | CBR9918I The Auto-Delete Installation Exit failed.  Collection
 |        collection-name object object-name in storage group storage-group
 |        was not deleted.  No further deletions will be allowed for this
 |        storage group.
 |        Explanation:  The Auto-Delete Installation Exit ended abnormally.
 |        No further deletions will be allowed for this storage group.
 |        In the message text:
 |        collection-name
 |                  The name of the collection.
 |        object-name
 |                  The name of the object.
 |        storage-group
 |                  The name of the storage group.
 |        Source:  Object access method (OAM)
 |        System Action:  Processing continues.
 |        Operator Response:  Notify the system programmer.
 |        System Programmer Response:  Correct the Automatic Delete
 |        Installation Exit.  The next start of OAM will load the corrected
 |        version of the Auto-Delete Installation Exit.
   __________________________________________________________________________
   CBR9920I ctcname modname A write to DASD was requested for collection
          collection-name object object-name in storage-group.  Object was
          not written as object was already on DASD.
          Explanation:  the control task ctcname module modname attempted to
          write collection collection-name object object-name in storage
          group storage-group from optical to DASD.  Return codes from
          SQL/DB2 indicated that the object already resided in the 4K or 32K
          tables.
          In the message text:
          ctcname   The name of the control task.
          modname   The name of the module.
          collection-name
                    The name of the collection.
          object-name
                    The name of the object.
          storage-group
                    The name of the storage group.
          Source:  Object access method (OAM)
          System Action:  Processing continues.
          Operator Response:  Notify system programmer.
          Application Programmer Response:  None.
```
